VERSION 1.0 CLASS
BEGIN
  MultiUse = -1
END
Attribute VB_Name = "clsActivity"
Option Explicit
Public TaskID As String
Public ActivityID As String
Public Name As String
Public WBSID As String
Public CalendarID As String
Public ActivityType As String
Public Status As String
Public OriginalDurationHours As Double
Public RemainingDurationHours As Double
Public PercentComplete As Double
Public PlannedStart As Date
Public PlannedFinish As Date
Public ActualStart As Date
Public ActualFinish As Date
Public ExpectedFinish As Date
Public SuspendDate As Date
Public ResumeDate As Date
Public HasPlannedStart As Boolean
Public HasPlannedFinish As Boolean
Public HasActualStart As Boolean
Public HasActualFinish As Boolean
Public HasExpectedFinish As Boolean
Public HasSuspendDate As Boolean
Public HasResumeDate As Boolean
Public EarlyStart As Date
Public EarlyFinish As Date
Public LateStart As Date
Public LateFinish As Date
Public TotalFloatHours As Double
Public FreeFloatHours As Double
Public ConstraintType1 As String
Public ConstraintDate1 As Date
Public HasConstraintDate1 As Boolean
Public BudgetCost As Double
Public ActualCost As Double
Public Function Clone() As clsActivity
    Dim x As New clsActivity
    x.TaskID=TaskID: x.ActivityID=ActivityID: x.Name=Name: x.WBSID=WBSID: x.CalendarID=CalendarID
    x.ActivityType=ActivityType: x.Status=Status: x.OriginalDurationHours=OriginalDurationHours
    x.RemainingDurationHours=RemainingDurationHours: x.PercentComplete=PercentComplete
    x.PlannedStart=PlannedStart: x.PlannedFinish=PlannedFinish: x.ActualStart=ActualStart: x.ActualFinish=ActualFinish
    x.ExpectedFinish=ExpectedFinish: x.SuspendDate=SuspendDate: x.ResumeDate=ResumeDate: x.HasPlannedStart=HasPlannedStart: x.HasPlannedFinish=HasPlannedFinish
    x.HasActualStart=HasActualStart: x.HasActualFinish=HasActualFinish: x.HasExpectedFinish=HasExpectedFinish
    x.HasSuspendDate=HasSuspendDate: x.HasResumeDate=HasResumeDate
    x.EarlyStart=EarlyStart: x.EarlyFinish=EarlyFinish: x.LateStart=LateStart: x.LateFinish=LateFinish
    x.TotalFloatHours=TotalFloatHours: x.FreeFloatHours=FreeFloatHours
    x.ConstraintType1=ConstraintType1: x.ConstraintDate1=ConstraintDate1: x.HasConstraintDate1=HasConstraintDate1
    x.BudgetCost=BudgetCost: x.ActualCost=ActualCost
    Set Clone=x
End Function

========================================================================
FILE: src/clsCalendar.cls
========================================================================

VERSION 1.0 CLASS
BEGIN
  MultiUse = -1
END
Attribute VB_Name = "clsCalendar"
Option Explicit
Public CalendarID As String
Public Name As String
Public HoursPerDay As Double
Public Is24Hour As Boolean
Private mWeekdayHours As Object
Private mExceptions As Object
Private Sub Class_Initialize()
    Set mWeekdayHours=CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Set mExceptions=CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
End Sub
Public Sub SetWeekdayHours(ByVal weekdayNo As Long, ByVal hours As Double)
    mWeekdayHours(CStr(weekdayNo))=hours
End Sub
Public Function WorkingHoursOnDate(ByVal d As Date) As Double
    Dim k As String: k=Format$(d,"yyyy-mm-dd")
    If mExceptions.Exists(k) Then
        WorkingHoursOnDate=CDbl(mExceptions(k))
    ElseIf mWeekdayHours.Exists(CStr(Weekday(d,vbMonday))) Then
        WorkingHoursOnDate=CDbl(mWeekdayHours(CStr(Weekday(d,vbMonday))))
    ElseIf Is24Hour Then
        WorkingHoursOnDate=24#
    End If
End Function
Public Sub SetException(ByVal d As Date, ByVal hours As Double)
    mExceptions(Format$(d,"yyyy-mm-dd"))=hours
End Sub

Public Function Clone() As clsCalendar
    Dim c As New clsCalendar
    c.CalendarID = CalendarID
    c.Name = Name
    c.HoursPerDay = HoursPerDay
    c.Is24Hour = Is24Hour
    Dim k As Variant
    For Each k In mWeekdayHours.Keys: c.SetWeekdayHours CLng(k), CDbl(mWeekdayHours(k)): Next k
    For Each k In mExceptions.Keys: c.SetException CDate(k), CDbl(mExceptions(k)): Next k
    Set Clone = c
End Function

========================================================================
FILE: src/clsExtendedRecord.cls
========================================================================

VERSION 1.0 CLASS
BEGIN
  MultiUse = -1
END
Attribute VB_Name = "clsExtendedRecord"
Option Explicit
Public TableName As String
Public RecordKey As String
Public Fields As Object
Public FieldOrder As Collection
Private Sub Class_Initialize()
    Set Fields = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Set FieldOrder = New Collection
End Sub
Public Sub SetField(ByVal fieldName As String, ByVal fieldValue As String)
    If Not Fields.Exists(fieldName) Then FieldOrder.Add fieldName
    Fields(fieldName) = fieldValue
End Sub
Public Function GetField(ByVal fieldName As String, Optional ByVal fallback As String = "") As String
    If Fields.Exists(fieldName) Then GetField = CStr(Fields(fieldName)) Else GetField = fallback
End Function
Public Function Clone() As clsExtendedRecord
    Dim x As New clsExtendedRecord, f As Variant
    x.TableName = TableName: x.RecordKey = RecordKey
    For Each f In FieldOrder: x.SetField CStr(f), CStr(Fields(CStr(f))): Next f
    Set Clone = x
End Function

========================================================================
FILE: src/clsScheduleModel.cls
========================================================================

VERSION 1.0 CLASS
BEGIN
  MultiUse = -1
END
Attribute VB_Name = "clsScheduleModel"
Option Explicit
Public ProjectID As String
Public ProjectName As String
Public SourceFormat As String
Public SourcePath As String
Public DataDate As Date
Public HasDataDate As Boolean
Public Activities As Object
Public Relationships As Object
Public WBS As Object
Public Resources As Object
Public Assignments As Object
Public Calendars As Object
Public Baselines As Object
Public BIMLinks As Object
Public ExtendedRecords As Object
Public RawRecords As Object
Private Sub Class_Initialize()
    Set Activities=CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Set Relationships=CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Set WBS=CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Set Resources=CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Set Assignments=CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Set Calendars=CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Set Baselines=CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Set BIMLinks=CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Set ExtendedRecords=CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Set RawRecords=CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
End Sub
Public Function Validate(ByRef message As String) As Boolean
    Dim k As Variant, r As clsRelationship, a As clsAssignment, w As clsWBS, b As clsBIMLink
    For Each k In Relationships.Keys
        Set r=Relationships(k)
        If Not Activities.Exists(r.PredTaskID) Then
            If Not r.IsExternal Then
                message="Missing predecessor "&r.PredTaskID
                Exit Function
            End If
        End If
        If Not Activities.Exists(r.SuccTaskID) Then message="Missing successor "&r.SuccTaskID: Exit Function
    Next
    For Each k In Assignments.Keys
        Set a=Assignments(k)
        If Not Activities.Exists(a.TaskID) Then message="Assignment task missing: "&a.TaskID: Exit Function
        If Not Resources.Exists(a.ResourceID) Then message="Assignment resource missing: "&a.ResourceID: Exit Function
    Next
    For Each k In WBS.Keys
        Set w=WBS(k)
        If Len(w.ParentWBSID)>0 Then If Not WBS.Exists(w.ParentWBSID) Then message="Missing WBS parent: "&w.ParentWBSID: Exit Function
    Next
    For Each k In BIMLinks.Keys
        Set b=BIMLinks(k)
        If Not Activities.Exists(b.TaskID) Then message="BIM link task missing: "&b.TaskID: Exit Function
    Next
    Dim x As clsExtendedRecord, refId As String
    For Each k In ExtendedRecords.Keys
        Set x = ExtendedRecords(k)
        If x.Fields.Exists("task_id") Then
            refId = x.GetField("task_id")
            If Len(refId) > 0 Then If Not Activities.Exists(refId) Then message = "Extended table " & x.TableName & " references missing task " & refId: Exit Function
        End If
        If x.Fields.Exists("rsrc_id") Then
            refId = x.GetField("rsrc_id")
            If Len(refId) > 0 Then If Not Resources.Exists(refId) Then message = "Extended table " & x.TableName & " references missing resource " & refId: Exit Function
        End If
    Next
    Validate=True
End Function

Attribute VB_Name = "modAIContext"
Option Explicit

Public Sub BuildVerifiedAIContext()
    On Error GoTo EH
    If gSchedule Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 13100, , "No schedule loaded."
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = EnsureSheet("AI Context")
    ws.Cells.Clear
    WriteRow ws, 1, 1, Array("Section", "Key", "Verified Value", "Source")
    ws.Rows(1).Font.Bold = True
    Dim r As Long: r = 2
    AIContextRow ws, r, "Project", "Project ID", gSchedule.ProjectID, "Canonical model"
    AIContextRow ws, r, "Project", "Project Name", gSchedule.ProjectName, "Canonical model"
    AIContextRow ws, r, "Project", "Activities", gSchedule.Activities.Count, "Canonical model"
    AIContextRow ws, r, "Project", "Relationships", gSchedule.Relationships.Count, "Canonical model"
    AIContextRow ws, r, "Project", "Data Date", IIf(gSchedule.HasDataDate, gSchedule.DataDate, ""), "Canonical model"
    AIContextRow ws, r, "Analysis", "Critical Activities", CountFloatBand(gSchedule, -1E+20, SafeDbl(GetSetting("CriticalFloatThresholdHours", 0), 0)), "Deterministic float analysis"
    AIContextRow ws, r, "Analysis", "Negative Float", CountFloatBand(gSchedule, -1E+20, -0.0001), "Deterministic float analysis"
    AIContextRow ws, r, "Analysis", "Forecast Stability", GetSetting("LatestForecastStabilityScore", ""), "Historical deterministic analysis"
    AIContextRow ws, r, "Analysis", "Schedule Health", ReadCellByLabel(SH_SCHEDULE_HEALTH, "Overall Health Score"), "Schedule Health sheet"
    AIContextRow ws, r, "Instruction", "AI Guardrail", "Explain only verified results in this sheet and referenced analysis sheets. Do not invent schedule facts.", "Application architecture"
    ws.Columns.AutoFit
    Exit Sub
EH:
    LogAndShowError "modAIContext", "BuildVerifiedAIContext", "The verified AI context could not be built.", ScheduleTraceContext()
End Sub

Private Sub AIContextRow(ByVal ws As Worksheet, ByRef rowNo As Long, ByVal section As String, ByVal key As String, ByVal value As Variant, ByVal source As String)
    WriteRow ws, rowNo, 1, Array(section, key, value, source)
    rowNo = rowNo + 1
End Sub

Private Function ReadCellByLabel(ByVal sheetName As String, ByVal label As String) As Variant
    Dim ws As Worksheet, f As Range
    Set ws = EnsureSheet(sheetName)
    Set f = ws.Columns(1).Find(label, LookAt:=xlWhole)
    If Not f Is Nothing Then ReadCellByLabel = f.Offset(0, 1).Value
End Function


========================================================================
FILE: src/modBackupRestore.bas
========================================================================

Attribute VB_Name = "modBackupRestore"
Option Explicit

Public Sub BackupRepository(ByVal folderPath As String)
    On Error GoTo EH
    TraceEnter "modBackupRestore","BackupRepository",TraceKV("folderPath",folderPath)
    EnsureRepository
    If Len(Dir$(folderPath,vbDirectory))=0 Then MkDir folderPath
    Dim k As Variant,r As clsRevision,fileName As String,f As Integer
    f=FreeFile:Open folderPath&Application.PathSeparator&"manifest.csv" For Output As #f
    Print #f,"RevisionID,RevisionName,ImportedOn,SourceFormat,FileName,Description"
    For Each k In gRepository.Keys
        Set r=gRepository(k)
        fileName=SafeFileName(r.RevisionName)&"_"&r.RevisionID&".xer"
        SaveModelAsXER r.Model,folderPath&Application.PathSeparator&fileName
        Print #f,Csv(r.RevisionID)&","&Csv(r.RevisionName)&","&Csv(CStr(r.ImportedOn))&","&Csv(r.SourceFormat)&","&Csv(fileName)&","&Csv(r.Description)
    Next k
    Close #f
    If Not gSchedule Is Nothing Then SaveModelAsXER gSchedule,folderPath&Application.PathSeparator&"CURRENT_SCHEDULE.xer"
    WriteAudit "BACKUP","Repository","",folderPath
    TraceExit "modBackupRestore","BackupRepository","revisions="&CStr(gRepository.Count)
    Exit Sub
EH:
    On Error Resume Next:Close #f:On Error GoTo 0
    LogAndShowError "modBackupRestore","BackupRepository","Repository backup failed.",TraceKV("folderPath",folderPath)
End Sub

Public Sub RestoreRepository(ByVal folderPath As String)
    On Error GoTo EH
    TraceEnter "modBackupRestore","RestoreRepository",TraceKV("folderPath",folderPath)
    EnsureRepository:gRepository.RemoveAll
    Dim manifest As String:manifest=folderPath&Application.PathSeparator&"manifest.csv"
    If Len(Dir$(manifest))=0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError+12500,,"manifest.csv was not found."
    Dim f As Integer,line As String,parts As Variant,r As clsRevision,m As clsScheduleModel
    f=FreeFile:Open manifest For Input As #f:Line Input #f,line
    Do While Not EOF(f)
        Line Input #f,line:If Len(Trim$(line))=0 Then GoTo NextLine
        parts=ParseCSVLine(line):If UBound(parts)<5 Then GoTo NextLine
        Set m=ParseXER(folderPath&Application.PathSeparator&CStr(parts(4)))
        Set r=New clsRevision:r.RevisionID=CStr(parts(0)):r.RevisionName=CStr(parts(1)):r.ImportedOn=CDate(parts(2)):r.SourceFormat=CStr(parts(3)):r.SourcePath=folderPath&Application.PathSeparator&CStr(parts(4)):r.Description=CStr(parts(5)):Set r.Model=m:Set gRepository(r.RevisionID)=r
NextLine:
    Loop
    Close #f
    RefreshRepositorySheet
    WriteAudit "RESTORE","Repository","",folderPath
    TraceExit "modBackupRestore","RestoreRepository","revisions="&CStr(gRepository.Count)
    Exit Sub
EH:
    On Error Resume Next:Close #f:On Error GoTo 0
    LogAndShowError "modBackupRestore","RestoreRepository","Repository restore failed.",TraceKV("folderPath",folderPath)
End Sub

Private Function SafeFileName(ByVal s As String) As String
    Dim bad As Variant,x As Variant:bad=Array("\\","/",":","*","?","""","<",">","|")
    SafeFileName=s:For Each x In bad:SafeFileName=Replace(SafeFileName,CStr(x),"_"):Next x
End Function
Private Function Csv(ByVal s As String) As String
    Csv = Chr$(34) & Replace(s, Chr$(34), Chr$(34) & Chr$(34)) & Chr$(34)
End Function
Private Function ParseCSVLine(ByVal line As String) As Variant
    Dim vals() As String:ReDim vals(0 To 5)
    Dim i As Long,pos As Long,ch As String,inQ As Boolean,field As String,idx As Long
    For i=1 To Len(line)
        ch=Mid$(line,i,1)
        If ch="""" Then
            If inQ And i<Len(line) And Mid$(line,i+1,1)="""" Then field=field&"""":i=i+1 Else inQ=Not inQ
        ElseIf ch="," And Not inQ Then vals(idx)=field:field="":idx=idx+1:If idx>UBound(vals) Then ReDim Preserve vals(0 To idx+2)
        Else:field=field&ch
        End If
    Next i
    vals(idx)=field:ReDim Preserve vals(0 To idx):ParseCSVLine=vals
End Function

FILE: src/modBaselines.bas

Attribute VB_Name = "modBaselines"
Option Explicit
Public Sub AddBaseline(ByVal currentModel As clsScheduleModel,ByVal baselineModel As clsScheduleModel,ByVal baselineName As String,ByVal role As String)
    TraceEnter "modBaselines", "AddBaseline", TraceKV("baselineName", baselineName) & "; " & TraceKV("role", role)
    Dim b As New clsBaseline,k As Variant,a As clsActivity
    b.Name=baselineName:b.Role=role:b.SourcePath=baselineModel.SourcePath:b.ImportedOn=Now
    For Each k In baselineModel.Activities.Keys:Set a=baselineModel.Activities(k):Set b.Activities(CStr(k))=a.Clone:Next
    Set currentModel.Baselines(baselineName)=b
End Sub
Public Sub RefreshBaselineView(ByVal m As clsScheduleModel,ByVal baselineName As String)
    TraceEnter "modBaselines", "RefreshBaselineView", TraceKV("baselineName", baselineName) & "; currentActivities=" & CStr(m.Activities.Count)
    If Not m.Baselines.Exists(baselineName) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError+3100,,"Baseline not found."
    Dim b As clsBaseline, ws As Worksheet
    Set b=m.Baselines(baselineName)
    Set ws=Worksheets(SH_BASELINES)
    ws.Cells.Clear:WriteRow ws,1,1,Array("TaskID","Activity ID","Name","Status","BL Start","Current Start","Start Var d","BL Finish","Current Finish","Finish Var d")
    Dim r As Long:r=2
    Dim k As Variant,a As clsActivity,ba As clsActivity
    For Each k In m.Activities.Keys
        Set a=m.Activities(k):ws.Cells(r,1)=a.TaskID:ws.Cells(r,2)=a.ActivityID:ws.Cells(r,3)=a.Name
        If b.Activities.Exists(CStr(k)) Then
            Set ba=b.Activities(CStr(k)):ws.Cells(r,4)="Matched"
            If ba.HasPlannedStart Then ws.Cells(r,5)=ba.PlannedStart
            If a.HasPlannedStart Then ws.Cells(r,6)=a.PlannedStart
            If ba.HasPlannedStart And a.HasPlannedStart Then ws.Cells(r,7)=a.PlannedStart-ba.PlannedStart
            If ba.HasPlannedFinish Then ws.Cells(r,8)=ba.PlannedFinish
            If a.HasPlannedFinish Then ws.Cells(r,9)=a.PlannedFinish
            If ba.HasPlannedFinish And a.HasPlannedFinish Then ws.Cells(r,10)=a.PlannedFinish-ba.PlannedFinish
        Else
            ws.Cells(r,4)="Current only"
        End If:r=r+1
    Next
    Dim bk As Variant
    For Each bk In b.Activities.Keys
        If Not m.Activities.Exists(CStr(bk)) Then
            Set ba=b.Activities(CStr(bk))
            ws.Cells(r,1)=ba.TaskID:ws.Cells(r,2)=ba.ActivityID:ws.Cells(r,3)=ba.Name:ws.Cells(r,4)="Baseline only"
            If ba.HasPlannedStart Then ws.Cells(r,5)=ba.PlannedStart
            If ba.HasPlannedFinish Then ws.Cells(r,8)=ba.PlannedFinish
            r=r+1
        End If
    Next bk
    ws.Rows(1).Font.Bold=True:ws.Columns.AutoFit
End Sub

Public Sub AssignBaselineRole()
    If gSchedule Is Nothing Then Exit Sub
    Dim nm As String: nm=InputBox("Baseline name:","Assign Baseline Role")
    If Len(nm)=0 Then Exit Sub
    If Not gSchedule.Baselines.Exists(nm) Then MsgBox "Baseline not found.",vbExclamation:Exit Sub
    Dim role As String: role=InputBox("Role: Project / Primary / Secondary / Tertiary","Baseline Role","Primary")
    If Len(role)=0 Then Exit Sub
    Dim k As Variant,b As clsBaseline
    For Each k In gSchedule.Baselines.Keys
        Set b=gSchedule.Baselines(k)
        If UCase$(b.Role)=UCase$(role) Then b.Role=""
    Next k
    Set b=gSchedule.Baselines(nm):b.Role=role
    If UCase$(role)="PRIMARY" Then SetSetting "PrimaryBaselineName",nm
    RefreshBaselineView gSchedule,nm
End Sub

Attribute VB_Name = "modBIM"
Option Explicit
Public Sub AddBIMLink(ByVal m As clsScheduleModel,ByVal taskId As String,ByVal modelKey As String,ByVal objectId As String,Optional ByVal objectName As String="")
    If Not m.Activities.Exists(taskId) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError+3200,,"Task not found."
    Dim key As String:key=taskId&"|"&modelKey&"|"&objectId
    If m.BIMLinks.Exists(key) Then Exit Sub
    Dim b As New clsBIMLink:b.LinkID=key:b.TaskID=taskId:b.ModelKey=modelKey:b.ObjectID=objectId:b.ObjectName=objectName:Set m.BIMLinks(key)=b
    WriteAudit "ADD","BIMLink",key,taskId&" -> "&modelKey&"~"&objectId
End Sub


========================================================================
FILE: src/modBIMTools.bas

Attribute VB_Name = "modBIMTools"
Option Explicit

Public Sub InitializeBIMObjectsSheet()
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = EnsureSheet(SH_BIM_OBJECTS)
    If ws.Cells(1,1).Value = "" Then
        WriteRow ws,1,1,Array("Model Key","Object ID","Object Name","Category","Level","System","Quantity","Unit","Source File","Model Path")
        ws.Rows(1).Font.Bold = True
    End If
End Sub

Public Sub ImportBIMObjectsCSV()
    Dim p As String: p = ChooseOpen("Import BIM object metadata CSV","*.csv")
    If Len(p)=0 Then Exit Sub
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = EnsureSheet(SH_BIM_OBJECTS)
    Dim f As Integer, line As String, vals As Variant, r As Long
    f=FreeFile
    Open p For Input As #f
    If EOF(f) Then Close #f: Exit Sub
    Line Input #f,line
    r = ws.Cells(ws.Rows.Count,1).End(xlUp).Row + 1
    Do While Not EOF(f)
        Line Input #f,line
        vals = Split(line,",")
        Dim i As Long
        For i=0 To WorksheetFunction.Min(UBound(vals),9)
            ws.Cells(r,i+1).Value = Replace(Trim$(vals(i)),"""","")
        Next i
        r=r+1
    Loop
    Close #f
    ws.Columns.AutoFit
End Sub

Public Sub LinkSelectedBIMObjectsToActivity()
    If gSchedule Is Nothing Then Exit Sub
    Dim taskId As String: taskId = InputBox("TaskID to link selected BIM object rows to:","BIM Link")
    If Len(taskId)=0 Then Exit Sub
    If Not gSchedule.Activities.Exists(taskId) Then MsgBox "Task not found.",vbExclamation: Exit Sub
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = Worksheets(SH_BIM_OBJECTS)
    Dim area As Range, rw As Range, modelKey As String, objectId As String, linkId As String, b As clsBIMLink
    For Each area In Selection.Areas
        For Each rw In area.Rows
            If rw.Row > 1 Then
                modelKey = CStr(ws.Cells(rw.Row,1).Value): objectId = CStr(ws.Cells(rw.Row,2).Value)
                If Len(modelKey)>0 And Len(objectId)>0 Then
                    linkId = taskId & "|" & modelKey & "|" & objectId
                    If Not gSchedule.BIMLinks.Exists(linkId) Then
                        Set b = New clsBIMLink
                        b.LinkID=linkId:b.TaskID=taskId:b.ModelKey=modelKey:b.ObjectID=objectId:b.ObjectName=CStr(ws.Cells(rw.Row,3).Value)
                        b.Category=CStr(ws.Cells(rw.Row,4).Value):b.Level=CStr(ws.Cells(rw.Row,5).Value):b.SystemName=CStr(ws.Cells(rw.Row,6).Value)
                        b.Quantity=SafeDbl(ws.Cells(rw.Row,7).Value):b.QuantityUnit=CStr(ws.Cells(rw.Row,8).Value)
                        Set gSchedule.BIMLinks(linkId)=b
                    End If
                End If
            End If
        Next rw
    Next area
    RefreshAllViews gSchedule
End Sub

Public Sub OpenSelectedBIMModel()
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = Worksheets(SH_BIM_OBJECTS)
    If ActiveSheet.Name <> SH_BIM_OBJECTS Then MsgBox "Select a row on BIM Objects first.",vbExclamation: Exit Sub
    Dim p As String: p = CStr(ws.Cells(ActiveCell.Row,10).Value)
    If Len(p)=0 Then MsgBox "No local model path is stored for this object.",vbExclamation: Exit Sub
    If Dir(p)="" Then MsgBox "Model file not found:" & vbCrLf & p,vbExclamation: Exit Sub
    ThisWorkbook.FollowHyperlink p
End Sub

Public Sub BIMLinkQA()
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = EnsureSheet(SH_DIAGNOSTICS)
    Dim r As Long: r = ws.Cells(ws.Rows.Count,1).End(xlUp).Row + 2
    ws.Cells(r,1).Value = "BIM Link QA": ws.Cells(r,1).Font.Bold=True: r=r+1
    Dim linked As Object, k As Variant, b As clsBIMLink, a As clsActivity
    Set linked=Dict()
    For Each k In gSchedule.BIMLinks.Keys:Set b=gSchedule.BIMLinks(k):linked(b.TaskID)=True:Next
    For Each k In gSchedule.Activities.Keys
        Set a=gSchedule.Activities(k)
        If Not linked.Exists(a.TaskID) Then WriteRow ws,r,1,Array("Info","No BIM Link",a.TaskID,a.ActivityID,a.Name):r=r+1
    Next
End Sub

Public Sub InitializeBIMAdvancedSheets()
    Dim ws As Worksheet
    Set ws=EnsureSheet(SH_BIM_SETS)
    If ws.Cells(1,1).Value="" Then WriteRow ws,1,1,Array("Set Name","Model Key","Object ID")
    Set ws=EnsureSheet(SH_BIM_RULES)
    If ws.Cells(1,1).Value="" Then WriteRow ws,1,1,Array("Rule Name","Activity Field","Activity Value","BIM Field","BIM Value","Enabled")
    Set ws=EnsureSheet(SH_BIM_RECON)
    If ws.Cells(1,1).Value="" Then WriteRow ws,1,1,Array("Status","TaskID","Model Key","Object ID","Detail")
End Sub

Public Sub CreateBIMSetFromSelection()
    If ActiveSheet.Name<>SH_BIM_OBJECTS Then MsgBox "Select rows on BIM Objects first.",vbExclamation:Exit Sub
    Dim nm As String:nm=InputBox("Set name:","Create BIM Set"):If Len(nm)=0 Then Exit Sub
    Dim src As Worksheet, dst As Worksheet
    Set src=Worksheets(SH_BIM_OBJECTS):Set dst=Worksheets(SH_BIM_SETS)
    Dim area As Range,rw As Range,outRow As Long
    outRow=dst.Cells(dst.Rows.Count,1).End(xlUp).Row+1
    For Each area In Selection.Areas
        For Each rw In area.Rows
            If rw.Row>1 Then
                WriteRow dst,outRow,1,Array(nm,src.Cells(rw.Row,1).Value,src.Cells(rw.Row,2).Value)
                outRow=outRow+1
            End If
        Next rw
    Next area
End Sub

Public Sub ApplyBIMLinkRule()
    Dim ruleName As String:ruleName=InputBox("Rule name from BIM Rules:","Apply BIM Rule"):If Len(ruleName)=0 Then Exit Sub
    Dim wsR As Worksheet, f As Range
    Set wsR=Worksheets(SH_BIM_RULES)
    Set f=wsR.Columns(1).Find(What:=ruleName,LookAt:=xlWhole,MatchCase:=False)
    If f Is Nothing Then MsgBox "Rule not found.",vbExclamation:Exit Sub
    Dim actField As String,bimField As String,actValue As String,bimValue As String
    actField=CStr(f.Offset(0,1).Value):actValue=CStr(f.Offset(0,2).Value):bimField=CStr(f.Offset(0,3).Value):bimValue=CStr(f.Offset(0,4).Value)
    Dim wsA As Worksheet, wsB As Worksheet
    Set wsA=Worksheets(SH_ACTIVITIES):Set wsB=Worksheets(SH_BIM_OBJECTS)
    Dim ac As Long, bc As Long
    ac=HeaderColumn(wsA,actField):bc=HeaderColumn(wsB,bimField)
    If ac=0 Or bc=0 Then MsgBox "Rule field not found.",vbExclamation:Exit Sub
    Dim ar As Long,br As Long,lastA As Long,lastB As Long,taskId As String,modelKey As String,objId As String,b As clsBIMLink
    lastA=wsA.Cells(wsA.Rows.Count,1).End(xlUp).Row:lastB=wsB.Cells(wsB.Rows.Count,1).End(xlUp).Row
    For ar=2 To lastA
        If UCase$(CStr(wsA.Cells(ar,ac).Value))=UCase$(actValue) Then
            taskId=CStr(wsA.Cells(ar,1).Value)
            For br=2 To lastB
                If UCase$(CStr(wsB.Cells(br,bc).Value))=UCase$(bimValue) Then
                    modelKey=CStr(wsB.Cells(br,1).Value):objId=CStr(wsB.Cells(br,2).Value)
                    If Len(modelKey)>0 And Len(objId)>0 Then AddBIMLink gSchedule,taskId,modelKey,objId,CStr(wsB.Cells(br,3).Value)
                End If
            Next br
        End If
    Next ar
    RefreshAllViews gSchedule
End Sub

Public Sub ReconcileBIMLinks()
    TraceEnter "modBIMTools", "ReconcileBIMLinks", ScheduleTraceContext()
    Dim wsO As Worksheet, ws As Worksheet
    Set wsO=Worksheets(SH_BIM_OBJECTS):Set ws=Worksheets(SH_BIM_RECON)
    ws.Cells.Clear:WriteRow ws,1,1,Array("Status","TaskID","Model Key","Object ID","Detail")
    Dim objects As Object, r As Long, key As String, lastRow As Long
    Set objects=Dict()
    lastRow=wsO.Cells(wsO.Rows.Count,1).End(xlUp).Row
    For r=2 To lastRow:key=CStr(wsO.Cells(r,1).Value)&"|"&CStr(wsO.Cells(r,2).Value):objects(key)=True:Next r
    Dim k As Variant,b As clsBIMLink,outRow As Long:outRow=2
    For Each k In gSchedule.BIMLinks.Keys
        Set b=gSchedule.BIMLinks(k):key=b.ModelKey&"|"&b.ObjectID
        WriteRow ws,outRow,1,Array(IIf(objects.Exists(key),"Exact","Missing"),b.TaskID,b.ModelKey,b.ObjectID,IIf(objects.Exists(key),"Stable ID matched","Object not found in current metadata"))
        outRow=outRow+1
    Next k
    ws.Columns.AutoFit
End Sub

Public Sub BuildBIMQuantitySummary()
    Dim wsO As Worksheet, ws As Worksheet
    Set wsO=Worksheets(SH_BIM_OBJECTS):Set ws=EnsureSheet(SH_BIM_RECON)
    Dim qty As Object, objQty As Object, r As Long, lastRow As Long, key As String
    Set qty=Dict():Set objQty=Dict()
    lastRow=wsO.Cells(wsO.Rows.Count,1).End(xlUp).Row
    For r=2 To lastRow:key=CStr(wsO.Cells(r,1).Value)&"|"&CStr(wsO.Cells(r,2).Value):objQty(key)=SafeDbl(wsO.Cells(r,7).Value):Next r
    Dim k As Variant,b As clsBIMLink
    For Each k In gSchedule.BIMLinks.Keys
        Set b=gSchedule.BIMLinks(k):key=b.ModelKey&"|"&b.ObjectID
        If objQty.Exists(key) Then
            Dim cur As Double
            If qty.Exists(b.TaskID) Then cur=SafeDbl(qty(b.TaskID)) Else cur=0#
            qty(b.TaskID)=cur+SafeDbl(objQty(key))
        End If
    Next k
    Dim outRow As Long:outRow=ws.Cells(ws.Rows.Count,1).End(xlUp).Row+2
    WriteRow ws,outRow,1,Array("TaskID","Activity ID","Linked Quantity"):outRow=outRow+1
    For Each k In qty.Keys
        Dim aid As String
        If gSchedule.Activities.Exists(CStr(k)) Then aid=gSchedule.Activities(CStr(k)).ActivityID Else aid=""
        WriteRow ws,outRow,1,Array(CStr(k),aid,qty(k)):outRow=outRow+1
    Next k
End Sub

Attribute VB_Name = "modCalendars"
Option Explicit

Public Function ActivityCalendar(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel, ByVal a As clsActivity) As clsCalendar
    If Len(a.CalendarID) > 0 Then
        If model.Calendars.Exists(a.CalendarID) Then Set ActivityCalendar = model.Calendars(a.CalendarID): Exit Function
    End If
    Dim c As New clsCalendar, h As Double
    h = SafeDbl(GetSetting("HoursPerDay", 8), 8)
    c.CalendarID = "__DEFAULT__": c.Name = "Default 5x8": c.HoursPerDay = h
    c.SetWeekdayHours 1, h: c.SetWeekdayHours 2, h: c.SetWeekdayHours 3, h: c.SetWeekdayHours 4, h: c.SetWeekdayHours 5, h
    c.SetWeekdayHours 6, 0: c.SetWeekdayHours 7, 0
    Set ActivityCalendar = c
End Function

Public Function AddWorkingHours(ByVal startDate As Date, ByVal hours As Double, ByVal cal As clsCalendar) As Date
    If Abs(hours) < 0.0000001 Then AddWorkingHours = startDate: Exit Function
    If hours > 0 Then
        AddWorkingHours = AddForward(startDate, hours, cal)
    Else
        AddWorkingHours = AddBackward(startDate, -hours, cal)
    End If
End Function

Private Function AddForward(ByVal startDate As Date, ByVal hours As Double, ByVal cal As clsCalendar) As Date
    Dim d As Date: d = startDate
    Dim remaining As Double: remaining = hours
    Dim dayH As Double, workStart As Date, workEnd As Date, avail As Double
    Do While remaining > 0.0000001
        dayH = cal.WorkingHoursOnDate(d)
        If dayH <= 0 Then
            d = DateAdd("d", 1, DateValue(d)) + TimeSerial(8, 0, 0)
        Else
            workStart = DateValue(d) + TimeSerial(8, 0, 0)
            workEnd = workStart + dayH / 24#
            If d < workStart Then d = workStart
            If d >= workEnd Then
                d = DateAdd("d", 1, DateValue(d)) + TimeSerial(8, 0, 0)
            Else
                avail = (workEnd - d) * 24#
                If remaining <= avail + 0.0000001 Then AddForward = d + remaining / 24#: Exit Function
                remaining = remaining - avail
                d = DateAdd("d", 1, DateValue(d)) + TimeSerial(8, 0, 0)
            End If
        End If
    Loop
    AddForward = d
End Function

Private Function AddBackward(ByVal startDate As Date, ByVal hours As Double, ByVal cal As clsCalendar) As Date
    Dim d As Date: d = startDate
    Dim remaining As Double: remaining = hours
    Dim dayH As Double, workStart As Date, workEnd As Date, avail As Double
    Do While remaining > 0.0000001
        dayH = cal.WorkingHoursOnDate(d)
        If dayH <= 0 Then
            d = DateAdd("d", -1, DateValue(d)) + TimeSerial(23, 59, 59)
        Else
            workStart = DateValue(d) + TimeSerial(8, 0, 0)
            workEnd = workStart + dayH / 24#
            If d > workEnd Then d = workEnd
            If d <= workStart Then
                d = DateAdd("d", -1, DateValue(d)) + TimeSerial(23, 59, 59)
            Else
                avail = (d - workStart) * 24#
                If remaining <= avail + 0.0000001 Then AddBackward = d - remaining / 24#: Exit Function
                remaining = remaining - avail
                d = DateAdd("d", -1, DateValue(d)) + TimeSerial(23, 59, 59)
            End If
        End If
    Loop
    AddBackward = d
End Function

Public Function WorkingHoursBetween(ByVal d1 As Date, ByVal d2 As Date, ByVal cal As clsCalendar) As Double
    If d1 = d2 Then Exit Function
    Dim sign As Double: sign = IIf(d2 > d1, 1#, -1#)
    Dim lo As Date, hi As Date
    If d2 > d1 Then lo = d1: hi = d2 Else lo = d2: hi = d1
    Dim d As Date: d = DateValue(lo)
    Dim total As Double, dayH As Double, ws As Date, we As Date, segStart As Date, segEnd As Date
    Do While d <= DateValue(hi)
        dayH = cal.WorkingHoursOnDate(d)
        If dayH > 0 Then
            ws = d + TimeSerial(8, 0, 0)
            we = ws + dayH / 24#
            segStart = IIf(lo > ws, lo, ws)
            segEnd = IIf(hi < we, hi, we)
            If segEnd > segStart Then total = total + (segEnd - segStart) * 24#
        End If
        d = DateAdd("d", 1, d)
    Loop
    WorkingHoursBetween = total * sign
End Function

Attribute VB_Name = "modCalendarUI"
Option Explicit

Public Sub RefreshCalendarsView(ByVal m As clsScheduleModel)
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = EnsureSheet(SH_CALENDARS)
    ws.Cells.Clear
    WriteRow ws, 1, 1, Array("Calendar ID", "Name", "Mon h", "Tue h", "Wed h", "Thu h", "Fri h", "Sat h", "Sun h", "Exception Date", "Exception Hours")
    ws.Rows(1).Font.Bold = True
    Dim r As Long: r = 2
    Dim k As Variant, c As clsCalendar
    For Each k In m.Calendars.Keys
        Set c = m.Calendars(k)
        WriteRow ws, r, 1, Array(c.CalendarID, c.Name, c.WorkingHoursOnDate(Date - Weekday(Date, vbMonday) + 1), c.WorkingHoursOnDate(Date - Weekday(Date, vbMonday) + 2), c.WorkingHoursOnDate(Date - Weekday(Date, vbMonday) + 3), c.WorkingHoursOnDate(Date - Weekday(Date, vbMonday) + 4), c.WorkingHoursOnDate(Date - Weekday(Date, vbMonday) + 5), c.WorkingHoursOnDate(Date - Weekday(Date, vbMonday) + 6), c.WorkingHoursOnDate(Date - Weekday(Date, vbMonday) + 7))
        r = r + 1
    Next k
    ws.Columns.AutoFit
End Sub

Public Sub SyncCalendarsFromSheet(ByVal m As clsScheduleModel)
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = EnsureSheet(SH_CALENDARS)
    If ws.Cells(1, 1).Value = "" Then Exit Sub
    Dim d As Object: Set d = Dict()
    Dim r As Long, lastRow As Long, c As clsCalendar, cid As String, exDate As Date
    lastRow = ws.Cells(ws.Rows.Count, 1).End(xlUp).Row
    For r = 2 To lastRow
        cid = Trim$(CStr(ws.Cells(r, 1).Value))
        If Len(cid) > 0 Then
            If d.Exists(cid) Then
                Set c = d(cid)
            Else
                Set c = New clsCalendar
                c.CalendarID = cid
                c.Name = CStr(ws.Cells(r, 2).Value)
                c.SetWeekdayHours 1, SafeDbl(ws.Cells(r, 3).Value)
                c.SetWeekdayHours 2, SafeDbl(ws.Cells(r, 4).Value)
                c.SetWeekdayHours 3, SafeDbl(ws.Cells(r, 5).Value)
                c.SetWeekdayHours 4, SafeDbl(ws.Cells(r, 6).Value)
                c.SetWeekdayHours 5, SafeDbl(ws.Cells(r, 7).Value)
                c.SetWeekdayHours 6, SafeDbl(ws.Cells(r, 8).Value)
                c.SetWeekdayHours 7, SafeDbl(ws.Cells(r, 9).Value)
                Set d(cid) = c
            End If
            If TryDate(ws.Cells(r, 10).Value, exDate) Then c.SetException exDate, SafeDbl(ws.Cells(r, 11).Value)
        End If
    Next r
    If d.Count > 0 Then Set m.Calendars = d
End Sub

Public Sub AddCalendarException()
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = EnsureSheet(SH_CALENDARS)
    Dim cid As String: cid = InputBox("Calendar ID:", "Calendar Exception")
    If Len(cid) = 0 Then Exit Sub
    Dim ds As String: ds = InputBox("Exception date (e.g. 25-Dec-2026):", "Calendar Exception")
    If Not IsDate(ds) Then MsgBox "Invalid date.", vbExclamation: Exit Sub
    Dim hs As String: hs = InputBox("Working hours on this date (0 = non-working):", "Calendar Exception", "0")
    If Not IsNumeric(hs) Then MsgBox "Invalid hours.", vbExclamation: Exit Sub
    Dim r As Long: r = ws.Cells(ws.Rows.Count, 1).End(xlUp).Row + 1
    ws.Cells(r, 1).Value = cid
    ws.Cells(r, 10).Value = CDate(ds)
    ws.Cells(r, 11).Value = CDbl(hs)
End Sub


========================================================================
FILE: src/modComparison.bas
========================================================================

Attribute VB_Name = "modComparison"
Option Explicit

Public Function CompareScheduleModels(ByVal previousModel As clsScheduleModel, ByVal currentModel As clsScheduleModel, ByVal previousName As String, ByVal currentName As String) As Collection
    On Error GoTo EH
    TraceEnter "modComparison", "CompareScheduleModels", "previous=" & previousName & "; current=" & currentName
    Dim changes As New Collection
    CompareActivities previousModel, currentModel, previousName, currentName, changes
    CompareRelationships previousModel, currentModel, previousName, currentName, changes
    CompareResources previousModel, currentModel, previousName, currentName, changes
    CompareCalendars previousModel, currentModel, previousName, currentName, changes
    CompareExtendedData previousModel, currentModel, previousName, currentName, changes
    TraceExit "modComparison", "CompareScheduleModels", "changes=" & CStr(changes.Count)
    Set CompareScheduleModels = changes
    Exit Function
EH:
    TraceError "modComparison", "CompareScheduleModels", Err.Number, Err.Description, Erl
    Err.Raise Err.Number, "modComparison.CompareScheduleModels", Err.Description
End Function

Private Sub CompareActivities(ByVal p As clsScheduleModel, ByVal c As clsScheduleModel, ByVal pn As String, ByVal cn As String, ByVal out As Collection)
    Dim k As Variant, a0 As clsActivity, a1 As clsActivity
    For Each k In c.Activities.Keys
        Set a1 = c.Activities(k)
        If Not p.Activities.Exists(CStr(k)) Then
            AddChange out, pn, cn, "Activity", CStr(k), a1, "Added", "Activity", "", a1.ActivityID, 0
        Else
            Set a0 = p.Activities(k)
            CompareActivityField out, pn, cn, a0, a1, "Activity Name", a0.Name, a1.Name
            CompareActivityField out, pn, cn, a0, a1, "WBS", a0.WBSID, a1.WBSID
            CompareActivityField out, pn, cn, a0, a1, "Activity Type", a0.ActivityType, a1.ActivityType
            CompareActivityField out, pn, cn, a0, a1, "Status", a0.Status, a1.Status
            CompareActivityField out, pn, cn, a0, a1, "Calendar", a0.CalendarID, a1.CalendarID
            CompareActivityNumber out, pn, cn, a0, a1, "Original Duration h", a0.OriginalDurationHours, a1.OriginalDurationHours
            CompareActivityNumber out, pn, cn, a0, a1, "Remaining Duration h", a0.RemainingDurationHours, a1.RemainingDurationHours
            CompareActivityNumber out, pn, cn, a0, a1, "% Complete", a0.PercentComplete, a1.PercentComplete
            CompareActivityDate out, pn, cn, a0, a1, "Planned Start", a0.PlannedStart, a0.HasPlannedStart, a1.PlannedStart, a1.HasPlannedStart
            CompareActivityDate out, pn, cn, a0, a1, "Planned Finish", a0.PlannedFinish, a0.HasPlannedFinish, a1.PlannedFinish, a1.HasPlannedFinish
            CompareActivityDate out, pn, cn, a0, a1, "Actual Start", a0.ActualStart, a0.HasActualStart, a1.ActualStart, a1.HasActualStart
            CompareActivityDate out, pn, cn, a0, a1, "Actual Finish", a0.ActualFinish, a0.HasActualFinish, a1.ActualFinish, a1.HasActualFinish
            CompareActivityDate out, pn, cn, a0, a1, "Early Start", a0.EarlyStart, a0.EarlyStart > 0, a1.EarlyStart, a1.EarlyStart > 0
            CompareActivityDate out, pn, cn, a0, a1, "Early Finish", a0.EarlyFinish, a0.EarlyFinish > 0, a1.EarlyFinish, a1.EarlyFinish > 0
            CompareActivityDate out, pn, cn, a0, a1, "Late Start", a0.LateStart, a0.LateStart > 0, a1.LateStart, a1.LateStart > 0
            CompareActivityDate out, pn, cn, a0, a1, "Late Finish", a0.LateFinish, a0.LateFinish > 0, a1.LateFinish, a1.LateFinish > 0
            CompareActivityNumber out, pn, cn, a0, a1, "Total Float h", a0.TotalFloatHours, a1.TotalFloatHours
            CompareActivityNumber out, pn, cn, a0, a1, "Free Float h", a0.FreeFloatHours, a1.FreeFloatHours
            CompareActivityField out, pn, cn, a0, a1, "Primary Constraint", a0.ConstraintType1, a1.ConstraintType1
            CompareActivityDate out, pn, cn, a0, a1, "Primary Constraint Date", a0.ConstraintDate1, a0.HasConstraintDate1, a1.ConstraintDate1, a1.HasConstraintDate1
            CompareActivityDate out, pn, cn, a0, a1, "Expected Finish", a0.ExpectedFinish, a0.HasExpectedFinish, a1.ExpectedFinish, a1.HasExpectedFinish
            CompareActivityDate out, pn, cn, a0, a1, "Suspend Date", a0.SuspendDate, a0.HasSuspendDate, a1.SuspendDate, a1.HasSuspendDate
            CompareActivityDate out, pn, cn, a0, a1, "Resume Date", a0.ResumeDate, a0.HasResumeDate, a1.ResumeDate, a1.HasResumeDate
        End If
    Next k
    For Each k In p.Activities.Keys
        If Not c.Activities.Exists(CStr(k)) Then
            Set a0 = p.Activities(k)
            AddChange out, pn, cn, "Activity", CStr(k), a0, "Deleted", "Activity", a0.ActivityID, "", 0
        End If
    Next k
End Sub

Private Sub CompareActivityField(ByVal out As Collection, ByVal pn As String, ByVal cn As String, ByVal a0 As clsActivity, ByVal a1 As clsActivity, ByVal fieldName As String, ByVal v0 As String, ByVal v1 As String)
    If StrComp(v0, v1, vbBinaryCompare) <> 0 Then AddChange out, pn, cn, "Activity", a1.TaskID, a1, "Changed", fieldName, v0, v1, 0
End Sub

Private Sub CompareActivityNumber(ByVal out As Collection, ByVal pn As String, ByVal cn As String, ByVal a0 As clsActivity, ByVal a1 As clsActivity, ByVal fieldName As String, ByVal v0 As Double, ByVal v1 As Double)
    If Abs(v0 - v1) > 0.000001 Then AddChange out, pn, cn, "Activity", a1.TaskID, a1, "Changed", fieldName, CStr(v0), CStr(v1), v1 - v0
End Sub

Private Sub CompareActivityDate(ByVal out As Collection, ByVal pn As String, ByVal cn As String, ByVal a0 As clsActivity, ByVal a1 As clsActivity, ByVal fieldName As String, ByVal d0 As Date, ByVal h0 As Boolean, ByVal d1 As Date, ByVal h1 As Boolean)
    If h0 <> h1 Then
        AddChange out, pn, cn, "Activity", a1.TaskID, a1, "Changed", fieldName, IIf(h0, CStr(d0), ""), IIf(h1, CStr(d1), ""), 0
    ElseIf h0 And h1 Then
        If Abs(CDbl(d0) - CDbl(d1)) > 0.000001 Then AddChange out, pn, cn, "Activity", a1.TaskID, a1, "Changed", fieldName, CStr(d0), CStr(d1), d1 - d0
    End If
End Sub

Private Sub CompareRelationships(ByVal p As clsScheduleModel, ByVal c As clsScheduleModel, ByVal pn As String, ByVal cn As String, ByVal out As Collection)
    Dim oldMap As Object: Set oldMap = RelationshipMap(p)
    Dim newMap As Object: Set newMap = RelationshipMap(c)
    Dim k As Variant, r As clsRelationship
    For Each k In newMap.Keys
        If Not oldMap.Exists(CStr(k)) Then
            Set r = newMap(k): AddGenericChange out, pn, cn, "Relationship", r.RelID, "Added", "Relationship", "", RelationshipSignature(r), 0
        End If
    Next k
    For Each k In oldMap.Keys
        If Not newMap.Exists(CStr(k)) Then
            Set r = oldMap(k): AddGenericChange out, pn, cn, "Relationship", r.RelID, "Deleted", "Relationship", RelationshipSignature(r), "", 0
        End If
    Next k
End Sub

Private Function RelationshipMap(ByVal m As clsScheduleModel) As Object
    Dim d As Object: Set d = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Dim k As Variant, r As clsRelationship
    For Each k In m.Relationships.Keys
        Set r = m.Relationships(k): Set d(RelationshipSignature(r)) = r
    Next k
    Set RelationshipMap = d
End Function

Private Function RelationshipSignature(ByVal r As clsRelationship) As String
    RelationshipSignature = r.PredTaskID & "|" & r.SuccTaskID & "|" & r.RelType & "|" & Format$(r.LagHours, "0.########")
End Function

Private Sub CompareResources(ByVal p As clsScheduleModel, ByVal c As clsScheduleModel, ByVal pn As String, ByVal cn As String, ByVal out As Collection)
    Dim k As Variant, r0 As clsResource, r1 As clsResource
    For Each k In c.Resources.Keys
        Set r1 = c.Resources(k)
        If Not p.Resources.Exists(CStr(k)) Then
            AddGenericChange out, pn, cn, "Resource", CStr(k), "Added", "Resource", "", r1.Name, 0
        Else
            Set r0 = p.Resources(k)
            If r0.Name <> r1.Name Then AddGenericChange out, pn, cn, "Resource", CStr(k), "Changed", "Name", r0.Name, r1.Name, 0
            If Abs(r0.PricePerUnit-r1.PricePerUnit)>0.000001 Then AddGenericChange out,pn,cn,"Resource",CStr(k),"Changed","Rate",CStr(r0.PricePerUnit),CStr(r1.PricePerUnit),r1.PricePerUnit-r0.PricePerUnit
            If Abs(r0.MaxUnitsPerHour-r1.MaxUnitsPerHour)>0.000001 Then AddGenericChange out,pn,cn,"Resource",CStr(k),"Changed","Max Units/hr",CStr(r0.MaxUnitsPerHour),CStr(r1.MaxUnitsPerHour),r1.MaxUnitsPerHour-r0.MaxUnitsPerHour
        End If
    Next k
    For Each k In p.Resources.Keys
        If Not c.Resources.Exists(CStr(k)) Then Set r0=p.Resources(k): AddGenericChange out,pn,cn,"Resource",CStr(k),"Deleted","Resource",r0.Name,"",0
    Next k
    CompareAssignments p,c,pn,cn,out
End Sub

Private Sub CompareAssignments(ByVal p As clsScheduleModel, ByVal c As clsScheduleModel, ByVal pn As String, ByVal cn As String, ByVal out As Collection)
    Dim k As Variant, a0 As clsAssignment, a1 As clsAssignment
    For Each k In c.Assignments.Keys
        Set a1=c.Assignments(k)
        If Not p.Assignments.Exists(CStr(k)) Then
            AddGenericChange out,pn,cn,"Assignment",CStr(k),"Added","Assignment","",a1.TaskID&"/"&a1.ResourceID,0
        Else
            Set a0=p.Assignments(k)
            CompareGenericNumber out,pn,cn,"Assignment",CStr(k),"Budget Units",a0.BudgetUnits,a1.BudgetUnits
            CompareGenericNumber out,pn,cn,"Assignment",CStr(k),"Actual Units",a0.ActualUnits,a1.ActualUnits
            CompareGenericNumber out,pn,cn,"Assignment",CStr(k),"Remaining Units",a0.RemainingUnits,a1.RemainingUnits
            CompareGenericNumber out,pn,cn,"Assignment",CStr(k),"Budget Cost",a0.BudgetCost,a1.BudgetCost
            CompareGenericNumber out,pn,cn,"Assignment",CStr(k),"Actual Cost",a0.ActualCost,a1.ActualCost
            CompareGenericNumber out,pn,cn,"Assignment",CStr(k),"Remaining Cost",a0.RemainingCost,a1.RemainingCost
        End If
    Next k
    For Each k In p.Assignments.Keys
        If Not c.Assignments.Exists(CStr(k)) Then AddGenericChange out,pn,cn,"Assignment",CStr(k),"Deleted","Assignment",CStr(k),"",0
    Next k
End Sub

Private Sub CompareGenericNumber(ByVal out As Collection, ByVal pn As String, ByVal cn As String, ByVal entityType As String, ByVal entityId As String, ByVal fieldName As String, ByVal v0 As Double, ByVal v1 As Double)
    If Abs(v0-v1)>0.000001 Then AddGenericChange out,pn,cn,entityType,entityId,"Changed",fieldName,CStr(v0),CStr(v1),v1-v0
End Sub

Private Sub CompareCalendars(ByVal p As clsScheduleModel, ByVal c As clsScheduleModel, ByVal pn As String, ByVal cn As String, ByVal out As Collection)
    Dim k As Variant, c0 As clsCalendar, c1 As clsCalendar, wd As Long
    For Each k In c.Calendars.Keys
        Set c1=c.Calendars(k)
        If Not p.Calendars.Exists(CStr(k)) Then
            AddGenericChange out,pn,cn,"Calendar",CStr(k),"Added","Calendar","",c1.Name,0
        Else
            Set c0=p.Calendars(k)
            If c0.Name<>c1.Name Then AddGenericChange out,pn,cn,"Calendar",CStr(k),"Changed","Name",c0.Name,c1.Name,0
            If Abs(c0.HoursPerDay-c1.HoursPerDay)>0.000001 Then AddGenericChange out,pn,cn,"Calendar",CStr(k),"Changed","Hours/Day",CStr(c0.HoursPerDay),CStr(c1.HoursPerDay),c1.HoursPerDay-c0.HoursPerDay
            For wd=1 To 7
                If Abs(c0.WorkingHoursOnDate(DateSerial(2026,8,24+wd-1))-c1.WorkingHoursOnDate(DateSerial(2026,8,24+wd-1)))>0.000001 Then AddGenericChange out,pn,cn,"Calendar",CStr(k),"Changed","Working Week Day "&wd,CStr(c0.WorkingHoursOnDate(DateSerial(2026,8,24+wd-1))),CStr(c1.WorkingHoursOnDate(DateSerial(2026,8,24+wd-1))),0
            Next wd
        End If
    Next k
    For Each k In p.Calendars.Keys
        If Not c.Calendars.Exists(CStr(k)) Then Set c0=p.Calendars(k): AddGenericChange out,pn,cn,"Calendar",CStr(k),"Deleted","Calendar",c0.Name,"",0
    Next k
End Sub

Private Sub CompareExtendedData(ByVal p As clsScheduleModel, ByVal c As clsScheduleModel, ByVal pn As String, ByVal cn As String, ByVal out As Collection)
    Dim oldMap As Object: Set oldMap=FlattenExtended(p)
    Dim newMap As Object: Set newMap=FlattenExtended(c)
    Dim k As Variant
    For Each k In newMap.Keys
        If Not oldMap.Exists(CStr(k)) Then AddGenericChange out,pn,cn,"XER Data",CStr(k),"Added","Raw Field","",SafeText(newMap(k)),0 ElseIf SafeText(oldMap(k))<>SafeText(newMap(k)) Then AddGenericChange out,pn,cn,"XER Data",CStr(k),"Changed","Raw Field",SafeText(oldMap(k)),SafeText(newMap(k)),0
    Next k
    For Each k In oldMap.Keys
        If Not newMap.Exists(CStr(k)) Then AddGenericChange out,pn,cn,"XER Data",CStr(k),"Deleted","Raw Field",SafeText(oldMap(k)),"",0
    Next k
End Sub

Private Function FlattenExtended(ByVal m As clsScheduleModel) As Object
    Dim d As Object: Set d=CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Dim k As Variant, x As clsExtendedRecord, f As Variant
    For Each k In m.ExtendedRecords.Keys
        Set x=m.ExtendedRecords(k)
        For Each f In x.FieldOrder
            d(x.TableName&"|"&x.RecordKey&"|"&CStr(f))=x.GetField(CStr(f))
        Next f
    Next k
    Set FlattenExtended=d
End Function

Private Sub AddChange(ByVal out As Collection, ByVal pn As String, ByVal cn As String, ByVal entityType As String, ByVal entityId As String, ByVal a As clsActivity, ByVal changeType As String, ByVal fieldName As String, ByVal oldValue As String, ByVal newValue As String, ByVal diff As Double)
    Dim ch As New clsChangeRecord
    ch.RevisionFrom=pn: ch.RevisionTo=cn: ch.EntityType=entityType: ch.EntityID=entityId
    ch.ActivityID=a.ActivityID: ch.ActivityName=a.Name: ch.WBSID=a.WBSID
    ch.ChangeType=changeType: ch.FieldName=fieldName: ch.PreviousValue=oldValue: ch.CurrentValue=newValue: ch.Difference=diff
    ch.Criticality=ClassifyChangeMateriality(ch)
    out.Add ch
End Sub

Private Sub AddGenericChange(ByVal out As Collection, ByVal pn As String, ByVal cn As String, ByVal entityType As String, ByVal entityId As String, ByVal changeType As String, ByVal fieldName As String, ByVal oldValue As String, ByVal newValue As String, ByVal diff As Double)
    Dim ch As New clsChangeRecord
    ch.RevisionFrom=pn: ch.RevisionTo=cn: ch.EntityType=entityType: ch.EntityID=entityId
    ch.ChangeType=changeType: ch.FieldName=fieldName: ch.PreviousValue=oldValue: ch.CurrentValue=newValue: ch.Difference=diff
    ch.Criticality=ClassifyChangeMateriality(ch)
    out.Add ch
End Sub

Public Sub WriteChangeRegister(ByVal changes As Collection)
    Dim ws As Worksheet
    Set ws = EnsureSheet(SH_CHANGE_REGISTER)

    Dim preserved As Object
    Set preserved = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Dim lastRow As Long, r As Long, key As String
    lastRow = ws.Cells(ws.Rows.Count, 1).End(xlUp).Row
    If lastRow >= 2 Then
        For r = 2 To lastRow
            key = CStr(ws.Cells(r, 3).Value) & "|" & CStr(ws.Cells(r, 4).Value) & "|" & CStr(ws.Cells(r, 9).Value)
            preserved(key) = Array(CStr(ws.Cells(r, 16).Value), CStr(ws.Cells(r, 17).Value), CStr(ws.Cells(r, 18).Value))
        Next r
    End If

    ws.Cells.Clear
    WriteRow ws, 1, 1, Array("From Revision", "To Revision", "Entity Type", "Entity ID", "Activity ID", "Activity Name", "WBS", "Change Type", "Field", "Previous Value", "Current Value", "Difference", "Impact", "Criticality", "Confidence", "User Comment", "Accepted/Rejected", "Responsible Party")
    ws.Rows(1).Font.Bold = True
    Dim rowNo As Long: rowNo = 2
    Dim ch As clsChangeRecord, saved As Variant
    For Each ch In changes
        key = ch.EntityType & "|" & ch.EntityID & "|" & ch.FieldName
        If preserved.Exists(key) Then
            saved = preserved(key)
            ch.UserComment = CStr(saved(0))
            ch.AcceptedRejected = CStr(saved(1))
            ch.ResponsibleParty = CStr(saved(2))
        End If
        WriteRow ws, rowNo, 1, Array(ch.RevisionFrom, ch.RevisionTo, ch.EntityType, ch.EntityID, ch.ActivityID, ch.ActivityName, ch.WBSID, ch.ChangeType, ch.FieldName, ch.PreviousValue, ch.CurrentValue, ch.Difference, ch.Impact, ch.Criticality, ch.Confidence, ch.UserComment, ch.AcceptedRejected, ch.ResponsibleParty)
        rowNo = rowNo + 1
    Next ch
    ws.Columns.AutoFit
End Sub

Attribute VB_Name = "modConversionAudit"
Option Explicit

Public Function BuildConversionAudit(ByVal m As clsScheduleModel,ByVal targetFormat As String) As Double
    TraceEnter "modConversionAudit", "BuildConversionAudit", TraceKV("targetFormat", targetFormat)
    Dim ws As Worksheet:Set ws=EnsureSheet(SH_CONVERSION):ws.Cells.Clear
    WriteRow ws,1,1,Array("Area","Score","Status","Notes"):ws.Rows(1).Font.Bold=True
    Dim score As Double:score=100
    Dim r As Long:r=2
    AuditRow ws,r,"Activities",100,"OK",m.Activities.Count&" records"
    AuditRow ws,r,"Relationships",100,"OK",m.Relationships.Count&" records"
    AuditRow ws,r,"Resources",100,"OK",m.Resources.Count&" resources"
    AuditRow ws,r,"Assignments",100,"OK",m.Assignments.Count&" assignments"
    Dim calScore As Double:calScore=IIf(m.Calendars.Count>0,95,85):score=score-(100-calScore)*0.1:AuditRow ws,r,"Calendars",calScore,IIf(calScore=100,"OK","Approximation"),"Calendar semantics vary between P6 and MSP"
    Dim bimScore As Double:bimScore=100:AuditRow ws,r,"BIM Links",bimScore,"OK",m.BIMLinks.Count&" stable element links"
    Dim extScore As Double
    If m.ExtendedRecords.Count = 0 Then
        extScore = 100
        AuditRow ws, r, "Extended / Custom Data", extScore, "OK", "No additional format-specific records loaded"
    ElseIf UCase$(m.SourceFormat) = UCase$(targetFormat) Or (UCase$(m.SourceFormat) = "XER" And UCase$(targetFormat) = "XER") Then
        extScore = 98
        score = score - 0.5
        AuditRow ws, r, "Extended / Custom Data", extScore, "Preserved", m.ExtendedRecords.Count & " extended records retained in native-format export"
    Else
        extScore = 70
        score = score - 4
        AuditRow ws, r, "Extended / Custom Data", extScore, "Review", m.ExtendedRecords.Count & " source-format-specific records may not map to the destination format"
    End If
    If UCase$(m.SourceFormat)<>UCase$(targetFormat) Then
        score=score-3
        AuditRow ws,r,"Cross-format",97,"Review","Format-specific codes, layouts and calculation settings may not map 1:1"
    Else
        AuditRow ws,r,"Native format",100,"OK","Highest-fidelity path"
    End If
    WriteRow ws,1,6,Array("Target",targetFormat):WriteRow ws,2,6,Array("Overall Fidelity",Round(score,1))
    ws.Columns.AutoFit:BuildConversionAudit=Round(score,1)
End Function

Private Sub AuditRow(ByVal ws As Worksheet,ByRef r As Long,ByVal area As String,ByVal score As Double,ByVal status As String,ByVal notes As String)
    WriteRow ws,r,1,Array(area,score,status,notes):r=r+1
End Sub


========================================================================
FILE: src/modCPM.bas
========================================================================

Attribute VB_Name = "modCPM"
Option Explicit

Public Sub CalculateCPM(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel)
    TraceEnter "modCPM", "CalculateCPM", "activities=" & CStr(model.Activities.Count) & "; relationships=" & CStr(model.Relationships.Count)
    Dim msg As String
    If Not model.Validate(msg) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 1000, , msg
    ValidateScheduleOptions model
    If HasCycle(model) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 1001, , "Relationship cycle detected."
    TracePoint "modCPM", "CalculateCPM", "Validation passed", "dataDate=" & IIf(model.HasDataDate, CStr(model.DataDate), "<none>")

    Dim ord As Collection: Set ord = TopologicalOrder(model)
    Dim projectStart As Date
    If model.HasDataDate Then projectStart = model.DataDate Else projectStart = Date

    Dim i As Long, id As String, a As clsActivity, cal As clsCalendar
    For i = 1 To ord.Count
        id = CStr(ord(i))
        Set a = model.Activities(id)
        Set cal = ActivityCalendar(model, a)
        a.EarlyStart = EarliestStart(model, a, projectStart)

        If a.HasActualFinish Then
            a.EarlyStart = IIf(a.HasActualStart, a.ActualStart, a.EarlyStart)
            a.EarlyFinish = a.ActualFinish
        ElseIf a.HasExpectedFinish And CBool(GetSetting("UseExpectedFinish", True)) Then
            a.EarlyFinish = a.ExpectedFinish
        Else
            a.EarlyFinish = AddWorkingHours(a.EarlyStart, RemainingHours(a), cal)
        End If
    Next i

    If CBool(GetSetting("EnableResourceLeveling", False)) Then LevelResources model, ord

    Dim projectFinish As Date: projectFinish = ProjectFinishDate(model)
    Dim finishBy As Date
    If TryDate(GetSetting("FinishBy", ""), finishBy) Then projectFinish = finishBy

    For i = ord.Count To 1 Step -1
        id = CStr(ord(i))
        Set a = model.Activities(id)
        Set cal = ActivityCalendar(model, a)
        a.LateFinish = LatestFinish(model, a, projectFinish)
        a.LateStart = AddWorkingHours(a.LateFinish, -RemainingHours(a), cal)
        a.TotalFloatHours = WorkingHoursBetween(a.EarlyFinish, a.LateFinish, cal)
        a.FreeFloatHours = FreeFloat(model, a)
    Next i
    TraceExit "modCPM", "CalculateCPM", "projectFinish=" & CStr(projectFinish)
End Sub

Private Sub ValidateScheduleOptions(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel)
    Dim strictSuspend As Boolean: strictSuspend = CBool(GetSetting("StrictSuspendResume", True))
    Dim k As Variant, a As clsActivity
    If strictSuspend Then
        For Each k In model.Activities.Keys
            Set a = model.Activities(k)
            If a.HasSuspendDate And Not a.HasResumeDate And Not a.HasActualFinish Then
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 1002, , "Activity " & a.ActivityID & " has a Suspend Date but no Resume Date."
            End If
            If a.HasSuspendDate And a.HasResumeDate Then
                If a.ResumeDate < a.SuspendDate Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 1003, , "Activity " & a.ActivityID & " resumes before it was suspended."
            End If
        Next k
    End If
End Sub

Private Function RemainingHours(ByVal a As clsActivity) As Double
    If a.HasActualFinish Or a.Status = STATUS_COMPLETE Then Exit Function
    If a.RemainingDurationHours > 0 Then RemainingHours = a.RemainingDurationHours Else RemainingHours = a.OriginalDurationHours
End Function

Private Function EarliestStart(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel, ByVal a As clsActivity, ByVal projectStart As Date) As Date
    Dim best As Date: best = projectStart
    If model.HasDataDate And a.HasActualStart And Not a.HasActualFinish Then best = model.DataDate

    Dim oosMode As String: oosMode = UCase$(SafeText(GetSetting("OutOfSequenceMode", "RETAINED LOGIC")))
    Dim ignoreRemainingLogic As Boolean
    ignoreRemainingLogic = (oosMode = "PROGRESS OVERRIDE" And a.HasActualStart And Not a.HasActualFinish)

    Dim k As Variant, r As clsRelationship, p As clsActivity, cand As Date
    If Not ignoreRemainingLogic Then
        For Each k In model.Relationships.Keys
            Set r = model.Relationships(k)
            If r.SuccTaskID = a.TaskID Then
                If r.IsExternal Then
                    cand = ExternalRelationshipDriver(model, a, r)
                ElseIf model.Activities.Exists(r.PredTaskID) Then
                    Set p = model.Activities(r.PredTaskID)
                    cand = RelationshipEarlyDriver(model, p, a, r, oosMode)
                End If
                If cand > best Then best = cand
            End If
        Next k
    End If

    If a.HasConstraintDate1 Then
        If IsStartConstraint(a.ConstraintType1) And a.ConstraintDate1 > best Then best = a.ConstraintDate1
    End If
    If a.HasResumeDate And a.ResumeDate > best Then best = a.ResumeDate
    If a.HasActualStart And oosMode = "ACTUAL DATES" And a.ActualStart > best Then best = a.ActualStart
    EarliestStart = best
End Function

Private Function RelationshipEarlyDriver(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel, ByVal p As clsActivity, ByVal s As clsActivity, ByVal r As clsRelationship, ByVal oosMode As String) As Date
    Dim predStart As Date, predFinish As Date
    predStart = p.EarlyStart: predFinish = p.EarlyFinish
    If oosMode = "ACTUAL DATES" Or p.HasActualFinish Then
        If p.HasActualStart Then predStart = p.ActualStart
        If p.HasActualFinish Then predFinish = p.ActualFinish
    End If

    Dim cal As clsCalendar: Set cal = RelationshipLagCalendar(model, p, s)
    Select Case r.RelType
        Case REL_SS
            RelationshipEarlyDriver = AddWorkingHours(predStart, r.LagHours, cal)
        Case REL_FF
            RelationshipEarlyDriver = AddWorkingHours(predFinish, r.LagHours - RemainingHours(s), cal)
        Case REL_SF
            RelationshipEarlyDriver = AddWorkingHours(predStart, r.LagHours - RemainingHours(s), cal)
        Case Else
            RelationshipEarlyDriver = AddWorkingHours(predFinish, r.LagHours, cal)
    End Select
End Function

Private Function ExternalRelationshipDriver(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel, ByVal s As clsActivity, ByVal r As clsRelationship) As Date
    Dim mode As String: mode = UCase$(SafeText(GetSetting("ExternalRelationshipMode", "USE STORED DATES")))
    If mode = "IGNORE" Then Exit Function
    If r.HasExternalPredDate Then
        ExternalRelationshipDriver = AddWorkingHours(r.ExternalPredDate, r.LagHours, ActivityCalendar(model, s))
    ElseIf mode = "USE STORED DATES" Then
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 1004, , "External predecessor " & r.PredTaskID & " has no stored external date."
    End If
End Function

Private Function RelationshipLagCalendar(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel, ByVal pred As clsActivity, ByVal succ As clsActivity) As clsCalendar
    Dim mode As String: mode = UCase$(SafeText(GetSetting("LagCalendarMode", "PREDECESSOR")))
    If mode = "SUCCESSOR" Then Set RelationshipLagCalendar = ActivityCalendar(model, succ): Exit Function
    If mode = "PROJECT" Then
        Dim cid As String: cid = SafeText(GetSetting("DefaultCalendarID", ""))
        If Len(cid) > 0 Then If model.Calendars.Exists(cid) Then Set RelationshipLagCalendar = model.Calendars(cid): Exit Function
    End If
    If mode = "24 HOUR" Or mode = "24H" Then
        Dim c As New clsCalendar: c.CalendarID = "__24H__": c.Name = "24 Hour": c.Is24Hour = True
        c.SetWeekdayHours 1, 24: c.SetWeekdayHours 2, 24: c.SetWeekdayHours 3, 24: c.SetWeekdayHours 4, 24: c.SetWeekdayHours 5, 24: c.SetWeekdayHours 6, 24: c.SetWeekdayHours 7, 24
        Set RelationshipLagCalendar = c: Exit Function
    End If
    Set RelationshipLagCalendar = ActivityCalendar(model, pred)
End Function

Private Function LatestFinish(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel, ByVal a As clsActivity, ByVal projectFinish As Date) As Date
    Dim best As Date: best = projectFinish
    Dim found As Boolean, k As Variant, r As clsRelationship, s As clsActivity, cand As Date, cal As clsCalendar
    Set cal = ActivityCalendar(model, a)
    For Each k In model.Relationships.Keys
        Set r = model.Relationships(k)
        If r.PredTaskID = a.TaskID And Not r.IsExternal Then
            If model.Activities.Exists(r.SuccTaskID) Then
                Set s = model.Activities(r.SuccTaskID)
                Select Case r.RelType
                    Case REL_FS: cand = AddWorkingHours(s.LateStart, -r.LagHours, cal)
                    Case REL_SS: cand = AddWorkingHours(s.LateStart, -r.LagHours + RemainingHours(a), cal)
                    Case REL_FF: cand = AddWorkingHours(s.LateFinish, -r.LagHours, cal)
                    Case REL_SF: cand = AddWorkingHours(s.LateFinish, -r.LagHours + RemainingHours(a), cal)
                End Select
                If Not found Or cand < best Then best = cand: found = True
            End If
        End If
    Next k
    If a.HasConstraintDate1 Then
        If IsFinishConstraint(a.ConstraintType1) And a.ConstraintDate1 < best Then best = a.ConstraintDate1
    End If
    LatestFinish = best
End Function

Private Function FreeFloat(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel, ByVal a As clsActivity) As Double
    Dim k As Variant, r As clsRelationship, s As clsActivity, v As Double, found As Boolean, minV As Double, cal As clsCalendar
    Set cal = ActivityCalendar(model, a)
    For Each k In model.Relationships.Keys
        Set r = model.Relationships(k)
        If r.PredTaskID = a.TaskID And Not r.IsExternal Then
            If model.Activities.Exists(r.SuccTaskID) Then
                Set s = model.Activities(r.SuccTaskID)
                v = WorkingHoursBetween(a.EarlyFinish, s.EarlyStart, cal) - r.LagHours
                If Not found Or v < minV Then minV = v: found = True
            End If
        End If
    Next k
    If found Then FreeFloat = minV Else FreeFloat = a.TotalFloatHours
End Function

Private Function ProjectFinishDate(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel) As Date
    Dim k As Variant, a As clsActivity
    For Each k In model.Activities.Keys
        Set a = model.Activities(k)
        If a.EarlyFinish > ProjectFinishDate Then ProjectFinishDate = a.EarlyFinish
    Next k
End Function

Private Function IsStartConstraint(ByVal t As String) As Boolean
    t = UCase$(t)
    IsStartConstraint = (InStr(t, "START") > 0 Or t = "CS_MSO" Or t = "CS_SNET")
End Function

Private Function IsFinishConstraint(ByVal t As String) As Boolean
    t = UCase$(t)
    IsFinishConstraint = (InStr(t, "FINISH") > 0 Or t = "CS_MFO" Or t = "CS_FNLT")
End Function

Public Function HasCycle(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel) As Boolean
    HasCycle = (TopologicalOrder(model).Count <> model.Activities.Count)
End Function

Public Function TopologicalOrder(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel) As Collection
    Dim indeg As Object: Set indeg = Dict()
    Dim adj As Object: Set adj = Dict()
    Dim k As Variant, r As clsRelationship, c As Collection
    For Each k In model.Activities.Keys
        indeg(CStr(k)) = 0
        Set c = New Collection: adj.Add CStr(k), c
    Next k
    For Each k In model.Relationships.Keys
        Set r = model.Relationships(k)
        If Not r.IsExternal Then
            If indeg.Exists(r.PredTaskID) And indeg.Exists(r.SuccTaskID) Then
                adj(r.PredTaskID).Add r.SuccTaskID
                indeg(r.SuccTaskID) = indeg(r.SuccTaskID) + 1
            End If
        End If
    Next k
    Dim q As New Collection, out As New Collection, id As Variant, n As Variant
    For Each k In indeg.Keys: If indeg(k) = 0 Then q.Add CStr(k)
    Next k
    Do While q.Count > 0
        id = q(1): q.Remove 1: out.Add CStr(id)
        For Each n In adj(CStr(id))
            indeg(CStr(n)) = indeg(CStr(n)) - 1
            If indeg(CStr(n)) = 0 Then q.Add CStr(n)
        Next n
    Loop
    Set TopologicalOrder = out
End Function


========================================================================
FILE: src/modCriticalIntelligence.bas
========================================================================

Attribute VB_Name = "modCriticalIntelligence"
Option Explicit

Public Function CriticalActivitySet(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel, Optional ByVal thresholdHours As Double = 0#) As Object
    Dim d As Object: Set d=CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Dim k As Variant, a As clsActivity
    For Each k In model.Activities.Keys
        Set a=model.Activities(k)
        If a.TotalFloatHours<=thresholdHours Then d(CStr(k))=True
    Next k
    Set CriticalActivitySet=d
End Function

Public Sub BuildCriticalPathReport(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel, Optional ByVal milestoneTaskId As String = "")
    On Error GoTo EH
    TraceEnter "modCriticalIntelligence","BuildCriticalPathReport",TraceKV("milestoneTaskId",milestoneTaskId)
    Dim ws As Worksheet:Set ws=EnsureSheet(SH_CRITICAL_PATH):ws.Cells.Clear
    WriteRow ws, 1, 1, Array("Rank","TaskID","Activity ID","Name","WBS","Early Start","Early Finish","Total Float h","Free Float h","Critical","Driving Preds")
    ws.Rows(1).Font.Bold=True
    Dim threshold As Double:threshold=SafeDbl(GetSetting("CriticalFloatThresholdHours",0),0)
    Dim relevant As Object:Set relevant=CriticalActivitySet(model,threshold)
    If Len(milestoneTaskId)>0 Then Set relevant=UpstreamSet(model,milestoneTaskId,relevant)
    Dim rowNo As Long:rowNo=2
    Dim k As Variant,a As clsActivity
    For Each k In model.Activities.Keys
        If relevant.Exists(CStr(k)) Then
            Set a=model.Activities(k)
            WriteRow ws, rowNo, 1, Array(rowNo-1,a.TaskID,a.ActivityID,a.Name,a.WBSID,a.EarlyStart,a.EarlyFinish,a.TotalFloatHours,a.FreeFloatHours,a.TotalFloatHours<=threshold,DrivingPredText(model,a.TaskID))
            rowNo=rowNo+1
        End If
    Next k
    ws.Columns.AutoFit
    TraceExit "modCriticalIntelligence","BuildCriticalPathReport","rows="&CStr(rowNo-2)
    Exit Sub
EH:
    LogAndShowError "modCriticalIntelligence","BuildCriticalPathReport","Critical-path analysis failed.",TraceKV("milestoneTaskId",milestoneTaskId)
End Sub

Public Function IsRelationshipDriving(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel, ByVal r As clsRelationship, Optional ByVal toleranceHours As Double = 0.1) As Boolean
    If Not model.Activities.Exists(r.PredTaskID) Or Not model.Activities.Exists(r.SuccTaskID) Then Exit Function
    Dim p As clsActivity:Set p=model.Activities(r.PredTaskID)
    Dim s As clsActivity:Set s=model.Activities(r.SuccTaskID)
    Dim cal As clsCalendar:Set cal=ActivityCalendar(model,p)
    Dim driverDate As Date
    Select Case r.RelType
        Case REL_SS: driverDate=AddWorkingHours(p.EarlyStart,r.LagHours,cal): IsRelationshipDriving=Abs(WorkingHoursBetween(driverDate,s.EarlyStart,cal))<=toleranceHours
        Case REL_FF: driverDate=AddWorkingHours(p.EarlyFinish,r.LagHours,cal): IsRelationshipDriving=Abs(WorkingHoursBetween(driverDate,s.EarlyFinish,cal))<=toleranceHours
        Case REL_SF: driverDate=AddWorkingHours(p.EarlyStart,r.LagHours,cal): IsRelationshipDriving=Abs(WorkingHoursBetween(driverDate,s.EarlyFinish,cal))<=toleranceHours
        Case Else: driverDate=AddWorkingHours(p.EarlyFinish,r.LagHours,cal): IsRelationshipDriving=Abs(WorkingHoursBetween(driverDate,s.EarlyStart,cal))<=toleranceHours
    End Select
End Function

Private Function DrivingPredText(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel,ByVal taskId As String) As String
    Dim s As String, k As Variant, r As clsRelationship, p As clsActivity, predLabel As String
    For Each k In model.Relationships.Keys
        Set r=model.Relationships(k)
        If r.SuccTaskID=taskId Then
            If IsRelationshipDriving(model,r) Then
                Set p = Nothing
                predLabel = r.PredTaskID
                If model.Activities.Exists(r.PredTaskID) Then
                    Set p = model.Activities(r.PredTaskID)
                    predLabel = p.ActivityID
                End If
                If Len(s) > 0 Then s = s & "; "
                s = s & predLabel & " " & r.RelType & " " & r.LagHours & "h"
            End If
        End If
    Next k
    DrivingPredText=s
End Function

Private Function UpstreamSet(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel,ByVal taskId As String,ByVal allowable As Object) As Object
    Dim d As Object:Set d=CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Dim q As New Collection:q.Add taskId
    Dim cur As String,k As Variant,r As clsRelationship
    Do While q.Count>0
        cur=CStr(q(1)):q.Remove 1
        If allowable.Exists(cur) And Not d.Exists(cur) Then
            d(cur)=True
            For Each k In model.Relationships.Keys
                Set r=model.Relationships(k)
                If r.SuccTaskID=cur Then q.Add r.PredTaskID
            Next k
        End If
    Loop
    Set UpstreamSet=d
End Function

Public Sub CompareCriticalPathMigration(ByVal oldModel As clsScheduleModel,ByVal newModel As clsScheduleModel,ByVal oldName As String,ByVal newName As String)
    On Error GoTo EH
    Dim ws As Worksheet:Set ws=EnsureSheet(SH_CRITICAL_MIGRATION):ws.Cells.Clear
    WriteRow ws, 1, 1, Array("TaskID","Activity ID","Name","Previous Critical","Current Critical","Migration","Previous TF h","Current TF h")
    ws.Rows(1).Font.Bold=True
    Dim threshold As Double:threshold=SafeDbl(GetSetting("CriticalFloatThresholdHours",0),0)
    Dim rowNo As Long, k As Variant, a0 As clsActivity, a1 As clsActivity, oldCrit As Boolean, newCrit As Boolean
    rowNo = 2
    For Each k In newModel.Activities.Keys
        Set a1=newModel.Activities(k):newCrit=a1.TotalFloatHours<=threshold
        If oldModel.Activities.Exists(CStr(k)) Then
            Set a0=oldModel.Activities(k):oldCrit=a0.TotalFloatHours<=threshold
            If oldCrit<>newCrit Then
                WriteRow ws, rowNo, 1, Array(a1.TaskID,a1.ActivityID,a1.Name,oldCrit,newCrit,IIf(newCrit,"Entered Critical Path","Left Critical Path"),a0.TotalFloatHours,a1.TotalFloatHours)
                rowNo=rowNo+1
            End If
        ElseIf newCrit Then
            WriteRow ws, rowNo, 1, Array(a1.TaskID,a1.ActivityID,a1.Name,False,True,"New Critical Activity","",a1.TotalFloatHours): rowNo=rowNo+1
        End If
    Next k
    ws.Columns.AutoFit
    Exit Sub
EH:
    LogAndShowError "modCriticalIntelligence","CompareCriticalPathMigration","Critical-path migration analysis failed.",oldName&" -> "&newName
End Sub


========================================================================
FILE: src/modDashboards.bas
