Attribute VB_Name = "modDashboards"
Option Explicit

Public Sub BuildWhatChangedDashboard(ByVal changes As Collection, ByVal previousModel As clsScheduleModel, ByVal currentModel As clsScheduleModel)
    On Error GoTo EH
    Dim ws As Worksheet
    Set ws = EnsureSheet(SH_WHAT_CHANGED)
    ws.Cells.Clear
    ws.Range("A1").Value = "WHAT CHANGED SINCE PREVIOUS REVISION"
    ws.Range("A1").Font.Bold = True
    ws.Range("A1").Font.Size = 16

    Dim counts As Object
    Set counts = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Dim ch As clsChangeRecord, key As String
    For Each ch In changes
        key = ch.EntityType & "|" & ch.ChangeType
        If counts.Exists(key) Then
            counts(key) = counts(key) + 1
        Else
            counts(key) = 1
        End If
    Next ch

    WriteRow ws, 3, 1, Array("Activities Added", CountD(counts, "Activity|Added"))
    WriteRow ws, 4, 1, Array("Activities Deleted", CountD(counts, "Activity|Deleted"))
    WriteRow ws, 5, 1, Array("Relationships Added", CountD(counts, "Relationship|Added"))
    WriteRow ws, 6, 1, Array("Relationships Deleted", CountD(counts, "Relationship|Deleted"))
    WriteRow ws, 7, 1, Array("Material/Critical Changes", CountMaterial(changes, "Material") + CountMaterial(changes, "Critical"))
    WriteRow ws, 8, 1, Array("Critical Activities Current", CountFloatBand(currentModel, -1E+20, SafeDbl(GetSetting("CriticalFloatThresholdHours", 0), 0)))
    WriteRow ws, 9, 1, Array("Near-Critical Activities Current", CountFloatBand(currentModel, SafeDbl(GetSetting("CriticalFloatThresholdHours", 0), 0) + 0.0001, SafeDbl(GetSetting("NearCriticalThresholdHours", 80), 80)))
    WriteRow ws, 10, 1, Array("Negative Float Current", CountFloatBand(currentModel, -1E+20, -0.0001))
    ws.Columns("A:B").AutoFit
    Exit Sub
EH:
    LogAndShowError "modDashboards", "BuildWhatChangedDashboard", "The What Changed dashboard could not be built."
End Sub

Public Sub BuildExecutiveDashboard(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel)
    On Error GoTo EH
    Dim ws As Worksheet
    Set ws = EnsureSheet(SH_EXEC_DASHBOARD)
    ws.Cells.Clear
    ws.Range("A1").Value = "EXECUTIVE SCHEDULE DASHBOARD"
    ws.Range("A1").Font.Bold = True
    ws.Range("A1").Font.Size = 16

    Dim finishD As Date, baseD As Variant
    finishD = ProjectFinish(model)
    baseD = PrimaryBaselineFinish(model)

    WriteRow ws, 3, 1, Array("Current Completion", finishD)
    WriteRow ws, 4, 1, Array("Baseline Completion", baseD)
    If IsDate(baseD) Then WriteRow ws, 5, 1, Array("Variance d", finishD - CDate(baseD)) Else WriteRow ws, 5, 1, Array("Variance d", "")
    WriteRow ws, 6, 1, Array("Critical Activities", CountFloatBand(model, -1E+20, SafeDbl(GetSetting("CriticalFloatThresholdHours", 0), 0)))
    WriteRow ws, 7, 1, Array("Near-Critical Activities", CountFloatBand(model, 0.0001, SafeDbl(GetSetting("NearCriticalThresholdHours", 80), 80)))
    WriteRow ws, 8, 1, Array("Negative Float Activities", CountFloatBand(model, -1E+20, -0.0001))
    WriteRow ws, 9, 1, Array("Average Progress %", AverageProgress(model)))
    WriteRow ws, 10, 1, Array("Resource Peak", PeakResourceDemand(model)))
    WriteRow ws, 11, 1, Array("Forecast Stability", GetSetting("LatestForecastStabilityScore", "")))
    WriteRow ws, 12, 1, Array("Schedule Health", ReadHealthScore())
    ws.Columns("A:B").AutoFit
    Exit Sub
EH:
    LogAndShowError "modDashboards", "BuildExecutiveDashboard", "The executive dashboard could not be built.", ScheduleTraceContext()
End Sub

Private Function CountD(ByVal d As Object, ByVal k As String) As Long
    If d.Exists(k) Then CountD = CLng(d(k))
End Function

Private Function CountMaterial(ByVal c As Collection, ByVal rating As String) As Long
    Dim ch As clsChangeRecord
    For Each ch In c
        If ch.Criticality = rating Then CountMaterial = CountMaterial + 1
    Next ch
End Function

Public Function CountFloatBand(ByVal m As clsScheduleModel, ByVal low As Double, ByVal high As Double) As Long
    Dim k As Variant, a As clsActivity
    For Each k In m.Activities.Keys
        Set a = m.Activities(k)
        If a.TotalFloatHours >= low And a.TotalFloatHours <= high Then CountFloatBand = CountFloatBand + 1
    Next k
End Function

Private Function ProjectFinish(ByVal m As clsScheduleModel) As Date
    Dim k As Variant, a As clsActivity, d As Date
    For Each k In m.Activities.Keys
        Set a = m.Activities(k)
        If a.HasPlannedFinish Then d = a.PlannedFinish Else d = a.EarlyFinish
        If d > ProjectFinish Then ProjectFinish = d
    Next k
End Function

Private Function PrimaryBaselineFinish(ByVal m As clsScheduleModel) As Variant
    Dim n As String
    n = SafeText(GetSetting("PrimaryBaselineName", ""))
    If Len(n) = 0 Then Exit Function
    If Not m.Baselines.Exists(n) Then Exit Function
    Dim b As clsBaseline, k As Variant, a As clsActivity, d As Date, maxD As Date
    Set b = m.Baselines(n)
    For Each k In b.Activities.Keys
        Set a = b.Activities(k)
        If a.HasPlannedFinish Then d = a.PlannedFinish Else d = a.EarlyFinish
        If d > maxD Then maxD = d
    Next k
    If maxD > 0 Then PrimaryBaselineFinish = maxD
End Function

Private Function AverageProgress(ByVal m As clsScheduleModel) As Double
    Dim k As Variant, a As clsActivity, total As Double
    If m.Activities.Count = 0 Then Exit Function
    For Each k In m.Activities.Keys
        Set a = m.Activities(k)
        total = total + a.PercentComplete
    Next k
    AverageProgress = total / m.Activities.Count
End Function

Private Function PeakResourceDemand(ByVal m As clsScheduleModel) As Double
    On Error GoTo SafeOut
    BuildResourceProfile m, "WEEK"
    Dim ws As Worksheet, lr As Long
    Set ws = EnsureSheet(SH_RESOURCE_PROFILE)
    lr = ws.Cells(ws.Rows.Count, 6).End(xlUp).Row
    If lr >= 2 Then PeakResourceDemand = WorksheetFunction.Max(ws.Range("F2:F" & lr))
SafeOut:
End Function

Private Function ReadHealthScore() As Variant
    Dim ws As Worksheet, f As Range
    Set ws = EnsureSheet(SH_SCHEDULE_HEALTH)
    Set f = ws.Columns(1).Find("Overall Health Score", LookAt:=xlWhole)
    If Not f Is Nothing Then ReadHealthScore = f.Offset(0, 1).Value
End Function


========================================================================
FILE: src/modDateMove.bas
========================================================================

Attribute VB_Name = "modDateMove"
Option Explicit

Public Sub ExplainDateMovement(ByVal previousModel As clsScheduleModel,ByVal currentModel As clsScheduleModel,ByVal taskId As String,ByVal previousName As String,ByVal currentName As String)
    On Error GoTo EH
    TraceEnter "modDateMove","ExplainDateMovement",TraceKV("taskId",taskId)
    If Not currentModel.Activities.Exists(taskId) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError+12100,,"Activity not found in current revision."
    Dim ws As Worksheet:Set ws=EnsureSheet(SH_DATE_MOVE):ws.Cells.Clear
    WriteRow ws, 1, 1, Array("Cause","Evidence","Estimated Contribution","Confidence","Notes")
    ws.Rows(1).Font.Bold=True
    Dim rowNo As Long:rowNo=2
    Dim cur As clsActivity:Set cur=currentModel.Activities(taskId)
    If Not previousModel.Activities.Exists(taskId) Then
        AddCause ws,rowNo,"Newly introduced activity","Activity did not exist in previous revision.",0,"Confirmed","No direct previous forecast exists."
        Exit Sub
    End If
    Dim prev As clsActivity:Set prev=previousModel.Activities(taskId)
    If prev.HasPlannedFinish And cur.HasPlannedFinish Then AddCause ws,rowNo,"Activity finish movement","Finish changed from "&prev.PlannedFinish&" to "&cur.PlannedFinish,cur.PlannedFinish-prev.PlannedFinish,"Confirmed","Observed movement."
    If Abs(cur.OriginalDurationHours-prev.OriginalDurationHours)>0.000001 Then AddCause ws,rowNo,"Duration change","Original duration "&prev.OriginalDurationHours&"h -> "&cur.OriginalDurationHours&"h",(cur.OriginalDurationHours-prev.OriginalDurationHours)/SafeDbl(GetSetting("HoursPerDay",8),8),"Confirmed","Duration change can alter downstream dates."
    If cur.CalendarID<>prev.CalendarID Then AddCause ws,rowNo,"Calendar change","Calendar "&prev.CalendarID&" -> "&cur.CalendarID,0,"Confirmed","Exact date contribution depends on calendar working periods."
    If cur.ConstraintType1<>prev.ConstraintType1 Or (cur.HasConstraintDate1<>prev.HasConstraintDate1) Or (cur.HasConstraintDate1 And prev.HasConstraintDate1 And cur.ConstraintDate1<>prev.ConstraintDate1) Then AddCause ws,rowNo,"Constraint change","Constraint configuration changed.",0,"Confirmed","Constraint may mask or override logical drivers."
    If cur.PercentComplete<>prev.PercentComplete Or cur.RemainingDurationHours<>prev.RemainingDurationHours Then AddCause ws,rowNo,"Progress / remaining work","Progress "&prev.PercentComplete&"% -> "&cur.PercentComplete&"%; Remaining "&prev.RemainingDurationHours&"h -> "&cur.RemainingDurationHours&"h",0,"Confirmed","Progress and remaining-duration changes can change forecast."
    CompareRelationshipCauses previousModel,currentModel,taskId,ws,rowNo
    ComparePredecessorMovement previousModel,currentModel,taskId,ws,rowNo
    If rowNo=2 Then AddCause ws,rowNo,"No direct cause identified","No material direct-field or immediate predecessor change was found.",0,"Possible","Investigate upstream logic, project settings and calendars."
    ws.Columns.AutoFit
    TraceExit "modDateMove","ExplainDateMovement","causes="&CStr(rowNo-2)
    Exit Sub
EH:
    LogAndShowError "modDateMove","ExplainDateMovement","Date-movement analysis failed.",TraceKV("taskId",taskId)
End Sub

Private Sub CompareRelationshipCauses(ByVal p As clsScheduleModel,ByVal c As clsScheduleModel,ByVal taskId As String,ByVal ws As Worksheet,ByRef rowNo As Long)
    Dim old As Object:Set old=PredSignatureSet(p,taskId)
    Dim newD As Object:Set newD=PredSignatureSet(c,taskId)
    Dim k As Variant
    For Each k In newD.Keys:If Not old.Exists(CStr(k)) Then AddCause ws,rowNo,"Relationship addition",CStr(k),0,"Confirmed","New incoming logic may delay the activity."
    Next k
    For Each k In old.Keys:If Not newD.Exists(CStr(k)) Then AddCause ws,rowNo,"Relationship deletion",CStr(k),0,"Confirmed","Removed incoming logic can release or reroute the activity."
    Next k
End Sub

Private Function PredSignatureSet(ByVal m As clsScheduleModel,ByVal taskId As String) As Object
    Dim d As Object:Set d=CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Dim k As Variant,r As clsRelationship
    For Each k In m.Relationships.Keys
        Set r=m.Relationships(k)
        If r.SuccTaskID=taskId Then d(r.PredTaskID&" -> "&taskId&" "&r.RelType&" "&r.LagHours&"h")=True
    Next k
    Set PredSignatureSet=d
End Function

Private Sub ComparePredecessorMovement(ByVal p As clsScheduleModel,ByVal c As clsScheduleModel,ByVal taskId As String,ByVal ws As Worksheet,ByRef rowNo As Long)
    Dim k As Variant,r As clsRelationship,p0 As clsActivity,p1 As clsActivity,delta As Double
    For Each k In c.Relationships.Keys
        Set r=c.Relationships(k)
        If r.SuccTaskID=taskId And p.Activities.Exists(r.PredTaskID) And c.Activities.Exists(r.PredTaskID) Then
            Set p0=p.Activities(r.PredTaskID):Set p1=c.Activities(r.PredTaskID)
            If p0.HasPlannedFinish And p1.HasPlannedFinish Then
                delta=p1.PlannedFinish-p0.PlannedFinish
                If Abs(delta)>0.000001 Then AddCause ws,rowNo,"Predecessor movement",p1.ActivityID&" finish moved "&Format$(delta,"0.0")&" calendar days",delta,IIf(IsRelationshipDriving(c,r),"Strongly indicated","Possible"),r.RelType&" "&r.LagHours&"h"
            End If
        End If
    Next k
End Sub

Private Sub AddCause(ByVal ws As Worksheet,ByRef rowNo As Long,ByVal cause As String,ByVal evidence As String,ByVal contribution As Double,ByVal confidence As String,ByVal notes As String)
    WriteRow ws, rowNo, 1, Array(cause,evidence,contribution,confidence,notes): rowNo=rowNo+1
End Sub


========================================================================
FILE: src/modDiagnostics.bas
========================================================================

Attribute VB_Name = "modDiagnostics"
Option Explicit
Public Sub RunScheduleDiagnostics(ByVal m As clsScheduleModel)
    TraceEnter "modDiagnostics", "RunScheduleDiagnostics", "activities=" & CStr(m.Activities.Count) & "; relationships=" & CStr(m.Relationships.Count)
    Dim ws As Worksheet:Set ws=Worksheets(SH_DIAGNOSTICS):ws.Cells.Clear:WriteRow ws,1,1,Array("Severity","Type","TaskID","Activity ID","Detail")
    Dim pred As Object, succ As Object, k As Variant, rel As clsRelationship, a As clsActivity, r As Long
    Set pred=Dict():Set succ=Dict():r=2
    For Each k In m.Activities.Keys:pred(CStr(k))=0:succ(CStr(k))=0:Next
    For Each k In m.Relationships.Keys
        Set rel=m.Relationships(k):If pred.Exists(rel.SuccTaskID) Then pred(rel.SuccTaskID)=pred(rel.SuccTaskID)+1
        If succ.Exists(rel.PredTaskID) Then succ(rel.PredTaskID)=succ(rel.PredTaskID)+1
        If rel.LagHours<0 Then AddD ws,r,"Warning","Negative Lag",rel.SuccTaskID,"",rel.LagHours&"h"
        If rel.LagHours>80 Then AddD ws,r,"Warning","High Lag",rel.SuccTaskID,"",rel.LagHours&"h"
    Next
    For Each k In m.Activities.Keys
        Set a=m.Activities(k)
        If pred(CStr(k))=0 Then AddD ws,r,"Warning","Open Start",a.TaskID,a.ActivityID,a.Name
        If succ(CStr(k))=0 Then AddD ws,r,"Warning","Open Finish",a.TaskID,a.ActivityID,a.Name
        If a.OriginalDurationHours>160 Then AddD ws,r,"Warning","Long Duration",a.TaskID,a.ActivityID,a.OriginalDurationHours&"h"
        If a.TotalFloatHours<0 Then AddD ws,r,"Critical","Negative Float",a.TaskID,a.ActivityID,a.TotalFloatHours&"h"
    Next
    ws.Rows(1).Font.Bold=True:ws.Columns.AutoFit
End Sub
Private Sub AddD(ByVal ws As Worksheet,ByRef r As Long,ByVal sev As String,ByVal typ As String,ByVal tid As String,ByVal aid As String,ByVal detail As String)
    WriteRow ws,r,1,Array(sev,typ,tid,aid,detail):r=r+1
End Sub

Public Sub ExplainSelectedActivity()
    If ActiveSheet.Name<>SH_ACTIVITIES Then MsgBox "Select an activity row first.",vbExclamation:Exit Sub
    Dim taskId As String:taskId=CStr(Cells(ActiveCell.Row,1).Value)
    If Not gSchedule.Activities.Exists(taskId) Then Exit Sub
    Dim a As clsActivity:Set a=gSchedule.Activities(taskId)
    Dim ws As Worksheet:Set ws=EnsureSheet(SH_DIAGNOSTICS)
    ws.Cells.Clear
    ws.Range("A1").Value="Calculation Explanation":ws.Range("A1").Font.Bold=True
    WriteRow ws,3,1,Array("TaskID",a.TaskID):WriteRow ws,4,1,Array("Activity",a.ActivityID&" - "&a.Name)
    WriteRow ws,5,1,Array("Early Start",a.EarlyStart):WriteRow ws,6,1,Array("Early Finish",a.EarlyFinish)
    WriteRow ws,7,1,Array("Late Start",a.LateStart):WriteRow ws,8,1,Array("Late Finish",a.LateFinish)
    WriteRow ws,9,1,Array("Total Float h",a.TotalFloatHours):WriteRow ws,10,1,Array("Calendar",a.CalendarID)
    WriteRow ws,12,1,Array("Predecessor","Type","Lag h","Pred ES","Pred EF","Approx Candidate Start")
    Dim r As Long, k As Variant, rel As clsRelationship, p As clsActivity, cand As Date
    r=13
    For Each k In gSchedule.Relationships.Keys
        Set rel=gSchedule.Relationships(k)
        If rel.SuccTaskID=taskId Then
            If rel.IsExternal Then
                WriteRow ws,r,1,Array(rel.PredTaskID,rel.RelType,rel.LagHours,"EXTERNAL",rel.ExternalPredDate,rel.ExternalPredDate)
            ElseIf gSchedule.Activities.Exists(rel.PredTaskID) Then
                Set p=gSchedule.Activities(rel.PredTaskID)
                cand=ApproxRelationshipCandidate(p,a,rel)
                WriteRow ws,r,1,Array(p.ActivityID,rel.RelType,rel.LagHours,p.EarlyStart,p.EarlyFinish,cand)
            End If
            r=r+1
        End If
    Next
    ws.Columns.AutoFit
    TraceExit "modDiagnostics", "RunScheduleDiagnostics", "rows=" & CStr(r - 1)
End Sub

Private Function ApproxRelationshipCandidate(ByVal p As clsActivity,ByVal s As clsActivity,ByVal rel As clsRelationship) As Date
    Select Case rel.RelType
        Case REL_SS:ApproxRelationshipCandidate=p.EarlyStart+rel.LagHours/24#
        Case REL_FF:ApproxRelationshipCandidate=p.EarlyFinish+(rel.LagHours-s.RemainingDurationHours)/24#
        Case REL_SF:ApproxRelationshipCandidate=p.EarlyStart+(rel.LagHours-s.RemainingDurationHours)/24#
        Case Else:ApproxRelationshipCandidate=p.EarlyFinish+rel.LagHours/24#
    End Select
End Function

Public Sub BuildFloatPathsForSelectedActivity()
    If ActiveSheet.Name<>SH_ACTIVITIES Then MsgBox "Select an activity row first.",vbExclamation:Exit Sub
    Dim taskId As String:taskId=CStr(Cells(ActiveCell.Row,1).Value)
    If Not gSchedule.Activities.Exists(taskId) Then Exit Sub
    Dim ws As Worksheet:Set ws=EnsureSheet(SH_DIAGNOSTICS):ws.Cells.Clear
    WriteRow ws,1,1,Array("Path","Score h","Terminal TaskID")
    Dim outRow As Long:outRow=2
    Dim visited As Object:Set visited=Dict()
    ExploreUpstreamPaths gSchedule,taskId,"",0,visited,ws,outRow,0
    If outRow>2 Then
        ws.Range("A1:C"&outRow-1).Sort Key1:=ws.Range("B2"),Order1:=xlAscending,Header:=xlYes
        Dim i As Long
        For i=2 To WorksheetFunction.Min(outRow-1,21):ws.Cells(i,1).Value=(i-1)&": "&ws.Cells(i,1).Value:Next i
    End If
    ws.Columns.AutoFit
End Sub

Private Sub ExploreUpstreamPaths(ByVal m As clsScheduleModel,ByVal taskId As String,ByVal suffix As String,ByVal score As Double,ByVal visited As Object,ByVal ws As Worksheet,ByRef outRow As Long,ByVal depth As Long)
    If depth>50 Or outRow>502 Then Exit Sub
    If visited.Exists(taskId) Then Exit Sub
    Dim localVisited As Object:Set localVisited=CloneStringDict(visited):localVisited(taskId)=True
    Dim a As clsActivity:Set a=m.Activities(taskId)
    Dim path As String
    path=a.ActivityID
    If Len(suffix)>0 Then path=path&" -> "&suffix
    Dim found As Boolean,k As Variant,rel As clsRelationship,p As clsActivity,gap As Double
    For Each k In m.Relationships.Keys
        Set rel=m.Relationships(k)
        If rel.SuccTaskID=taskId And Not rel.IsExternal And m.Activities.Exists(rel.PredTaskID) Then
            found=True:Set p=m.Activities(rel.PredTaskID)
            gap=Abs((a.EarlyStart-p.EarlyFinish)*24#-rel.LagHours)
            ExploreUpstreamPaths m,p.TaskID,path,score+gap,localVisited,ws,outRow,depth+1
        End If
    Next
    If Not found Then WriteRow ws,outRow,1,Array(path,score,taskId):outRow=outRow+1
End Sub

Private Function CloneStringDict(ByVal d As Object) As Object
    Dim x As Object, k As Variant
    Set x=Dict()
    For Each k In d.Keys:x(CStr(k))=d(k):Next
    Set CloneStringDict=x
End Function


========================================================================
FILE: src/modEVM.bas
========================================================================

Attribute VB_Name = "modEVM"
Option Explicit

Public Sub BuildEVMAndProductivity()
    TraceEnter "modEVM", "BuildEVMAndProductivity", ScheduleTraceContext()
    On Error GoTo fail
    If gSchedule Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4100, , "No schedule loaded."
    SyncModelFromSheets gSchedule

    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = EnsureSheet(SH_EVM)
    ws.Cells.Clear
    ws.Range("A1").Value = "Earned Value & Productivity"
    ws.Range("A1").Font.Bold = True
    ws.Range("A1").Font.Size = 16

    Dim asOfDate As Date
    If gSchedule.HasDataDate Then asOfDate = gSchedule.DataDate Else asOfDate = Date

    Dim BAC As Double, PV As Double, EV As Double, AC As Double
    Dim k As Variant, a As clsActivity, taskPV As Double, taskEV As Double, plannedPct As Double
    WriteRow ws,5,1,Array("TaskID", "Activity ID", "Name", "BAC", "Planned %", "PV", "Physical %", "EV", "AC", "SPI", "CPI", "EAC", "VAC", "TCPI")
    ws.Rows(5).Font.Bold = True
    Dim r As Long: r = 6

    For Each k In gSchedule.Activities.Keys
        Set a = gSchedule.Activities(k)
        plannedPct = PlannedPercentAtDate(a, asOfDate)
        taskPV = a.BudgetCost * plannedPct
        taskEV = a.BudgetCost * Clamp01(a.PercentComplete / 100#)
        BAC = BAC + a.BudgetCost
        PV = PV + taskPV
        EV = EV + taskEV
        AC = AC + a.ActualCost

        WriteRow ws,r,1,Array(a.TaskID, a.ActivityID, a.Name, a.BudgetCost, plannedPct, taskPV, a.PercentComplete / 100#, taskEV, a.ActualCost)
        If taskPV <> 0 Then ws.Cells(r, 10).Value = taskEV / taskPV
        If a.ActualCost <> 0 Then ws.Cells(r, 11).Value = taskEV / a.ActualCost
        If taskEV <> 0 And a.ActualCost <> 0 Then ws.Cells(r, 12).Value = a.BudgetCost / (taskEV / a.ActualCost)
        If ws.Cells(r, 12).Value <> "" Then ws.Cells(r, 13).Value = a.BudgetCost - CDbl(ws.Cells(r, 12).Value)
        If a.BudgetCost - a.ActualCost <> 0 Then ws.Cells(r, 14).Value = (a.BudgetCost - taskEV) / (a.BudgetCost - a.ActualCost)
        r = r + 1
    Next k

    Dim SPI As Variant, CPI As Variant, EAC As Variant, VAC As Variant, TCPI As Variant
    If PV <> 0 Then SPI = EV / PV
    If AC <> 0 Then CPI = EV / AC
    If AC <> 0 And EV <> 0 Then EAC = BAC / (EV / AC)
    If Not IsEmpty(EAC) Then VAC = BAC - CDbl(EAC)
    If BAC - AC <> 0 Then TCPI = (BAC - EV) / (BAC - AC)

    WriteRow ws,3,1,Array("As Of", "BAC", "PV", "EV", "AC", "SPI", "CPI", "EAC", "VAC", "TCPI")
    WriteRow ws,4,1,Array(asOfDate, BAC, PV, EV, AC, SPI, CPI, EAC, VAC, TCPI)
    ws.Range("A3:J3").Font.Bold = True

    BuildProductivitySection ws, r + 2
    ws.Columns.AutoFit
    TraceExit "modEVM", "BuildEVMAndProductivity", _
              "BAC=" & CStr(BAC) & "; PV=" & CStr(PV) & "; EV=" & CStr(EV) & "; AC=" & CStr(AC)
    Exit Sub
fail:
    LogAndShowError "modEVM", "BuildEVMAndProductivity", "EVM/productivity build failed.", ScheduleTraceContext()
End Sub

Private Function PlannedPercentAtDate(ByVal a As clsActivity, ByVal asOfDate As Date) As Double
    Dim s As Date, f As Date
    If a.HasPlannedStart Then s = a.PlannedStart Else s = a.EarlyStart
    If a.HasPlannedFinish Then f = a.PlannedFinish Else f = a.EarlyFinish
    If s = 0 Or f = 0 Then Exit Function
    If asOfDate <= s Then PlannedPercentAtDate = 0#: Exit Function
    If asOfDate >= f Then PlannedPercentAtDate = 1#: Exit Function
    If f = s Then PlannedPercentAtDate = 1#: Exit Function
    PlannedPercentAtDate = Clamp01((asOfDate - s) / (f - s))
End Function

Private Function Clamp01(ByVal x As Double) As Double
    If x < 0 Then Clamp01 = 0 ElseIf x > 1 Then Clamp01 = 1 Else Clamp01 = x
End Function

Private Sub BuildProductivitySection(ByVal ws As Worksheet, ByVal startRow As Long)
    ws.Cells(startRow, 1).Value = "Resource Productivity"
    ws.Cells(startRow, 1).Font.Bold = True
    WriteRow ws,startRow+1,1,Array("Resource ID", "Resource", "Budget Units", "Actual Units", "Remaining Units", "Budget Cost", "Actual Cost", "Unit Efficiency", "Cost Efficiency")
    ws.Rows(startRow + 1).Font.Bold = True

    Dim agg As Object: Set agg = Dict()
    Dim k As Variant, asn As clsAssignment, arr As Variant, rid As String
    For Each k In gSchedule.Assignments.Keys
        Set asn = gSchedule.Assignments(k)
        rid = asn.ResourceID
        If agg.Exists(rid) Then arr = agg(rid) Else arr = Array(0#, 0#, 0#, 0#, 0#)
        arr(0) = arr(0) + asn.BudgetUnits
        arr(1) = arr(1) + asn.ActualUnits
        arr(2) = arr(2) + asn.RemainingUnits
        arr(3) = arr(3) + asn.BudgetCost
        arr(4) = arr(4) + asn.ActualCost
        agg(rid) = arr
    Next k

    Dim r As Long: r = startRow + 2
    Dim nm As String
    For Each k In agg.Keys
        arr = agg(k): nm = CStr(k)
        If gSchedule.Resources.Exists(CStr(k)) Then nm = gSchedule.Resources(CStr(k)).Name
        WriteRow ws,r,1,Array(CStr(k), nm, arr(0), arr(1), arr(2), arr(3), arr(4))
        If arr(1) <> 0 Then ws.Cells(r, 8).Value = arr(0) / arr(1)
        If arr(4) <> 0 Then ws.Cells(r, 9).Value = arr(3) / arr(4)
        r = r + 1
    Next k
End Sub


========================================================================
FILE: src/modExtendedData.bas
========================================================================

Attribute VB_Name = "modExtendedData"
Option Explicit

Public Sub StoreExtendedRecord(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel, ByVal tableName As String, ByVal rec As Object)
    On Error GoTo EH
    Dim x As New clsExtendedRecord, f As Variant, key As String
    x.TableName = UCase$(tableName)
    key = ExtendedRecordKey(x.TableName, rec, model.ExtendedRecords.Count + 1)
    x.RecordKey = key
    For Each f In rec.Keys: x.SetField CStr(f), SafeText(rec(f)): Next f
    Set model.ExtendedRecords(x.TableName & "|" & key & "|" & CStr(model.ExtendedRecords.Count + 1)) = x
    Exit Sub
EH:
    TraceError "modExtendedData", "StoreExtendedRecord", Err.Number, Err.Description, Erl, _
               TraceKV("table", tableName)
    Err.Raise Err.Number, "modExtendedData.StoreExtendedRecord", Err.Description
End Sub

Private Function ExtendedRecordKey(ByVal tableName As String, ByVal rec As Object, ByVal ordinal As Long) As String
    Dim candidates As Variant, c As Variant
    candidates = Array("task_id", "wbs_id", "rsrc_id", "role_id", "actv_code_id", "actv_code_type_id", _
                       "memo_id", "memo_type_id", "udf_type_id", "fk_id", "risk_id", "issue_id", "proj_id")
    For Each c In candidates
        If rec.Exists(CStr(c)) Then
            If Len(SafeText(rec(CStr(c)))) > 0 Then ExtendedRecordKey = SafeText(rec(CStr(c))): Exit Function
        End If
    Next c
    ExtendedRecordKey = tableName & "_" & Format$(ordinal, "000000")
End Function

Public Function IsNormalizedCoreXERTable(ByVal tableName As String) As Boolean
    Select Case UCase$(tableName)
        Case "PROJECT", "PROJWBS", "TASK", "TASKPRED", "RSRC", "TASKRSRC", "CALENDAR"
            IsNormalizedCoreXERTable = True
    End Select
End Function

Public Sub RefreshExtendedDataView(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel)
    On Error GoTo EH
    TraceEnter "modExtendedData", "RefreshExtendedDataView", "records=" & CStr(model.ExtendedRecords.Count)
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = EnsureSheet(SH_EXTENDED)
    ws.Cells.Clear
    WriteRow ws, 1, 1, Array("Table", "Record Key", "Field", "Value")
    ws.Rows(1).Font.Bold = True
    Dim k As Variant, x As clsExtendedRecord, f As Variant, r As Long: r = 2
    For Each k In model.ExtendedRecords.Keys
        Set x = model.ExtendedRecords(k)
        For Each f In x.FieldOrder
            WriteRow ws, r, 1, Array(x.TableName, x.RecordKey, CStr(f), x.GetField(CStr(f)))
            r = r + 1
        Next f
    Next k
    ws.Columns("A:D").AutoFit
    ws.Columns("D").ColumnWidth = 60
    TraceExit "modExtendedData", "RefreshExtendedDataView", "rows=" & CStr(r - 1)
    Exit Sub
EH:
    LogAndShowError "modExtendedData", "RefreshExtendedDataView", "Extended XER data could not be displayed.", _
                    "records=" & CStr(model.ExtendedRecords.Count)
End Sub

Public Sub SyncExtendedDataFromSheet(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel)
    On Error GoTo EH
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = EnsureSheet(SH_EXTENDED)
    Dim lastRow As Long: lastRow = ws.Cells(ws.Rows.Count, 1).End(xlUp).Row
    Dim records As Object: Set records = Dict()
    Dim r As Long, tableName As String, recordKey As String, fieldName As String, composite As String, x As clsExtendedRecord
    For r = 2 To lastRow
        tableName = UCase$(Trim$(SafeText(ws.Cells(r, 1).Value)))
        recordKey = Trim$(SafeText(ws.Cells(r, 2).Value))
        fieldName = Trim$(SafeText(ws.Cells(r, 3).Value))
        If Len(tableName) > 0 And Len(recordKey) > 0 And Len(fieldName) > 0 Then
            composite = tableName & "|" & recordKey
            If Not records.Exists(composite) Then
                Set x = New clsExtendedRecord: x.TableName = tableName: x.RecordKey = recordKey
                Set records(composite) = x
            End If
            Set x = records(composite)
            x.SetField fieldName, SafeText(ws.Cells(r, 4).Value)
        End If
    Next r
    Set model.ExtendedRecords = Dict()
    Dim k As Variant, seq As Long: seq = 0
    For Each k In records.Keys
        seq = seq + 1: Set x = records(k)
        Set model.ExtendedRecords(x.TableName & "|" & x.RecordKey & "|" & CStr(seq)) = x
    Next k
    TracePoint "modExtendedData", "SyncExtendedDataFromSheet", "Extended records synchronized", _
               "records=" & CStr(model.ExtendedRecords.Count)
    Exit Sub
EH:
    TraceError "modExtendedData", "SyncExtendedDataFromSheet", Err.Number, Err.Description, Erl, _
               "row=" & CStr(r) & "; table=" & tableName & "; recordKey=" & recordKey
    Err.Raise Err.Number, "modExtendedData.SyncExtendedDataFromSheet", Err.Description
End Sub

Public Sub WriteExtendedXERTables(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel, ByVal f As Integer)
    Dim tables As Object: Set tables = Dict()
    Dim k As Variant, x As clsExtendedRecord, c As Collection
    For Each k In model.ExtendedRecords.Keys
        Set x = model.ExtendedRecords(k)
        If x.TableName <> "UDFTYPE" And x.TableName <> "UDFVALUE" Then
            If Left$(x.TableName, 4) <> "MSP_" And Not IsNormalizedCoreXERTable(x.TableName) Then
                If Not tables.Exists(x.TableName) Then Set c = New Collection: tables.Add x.TableName, c
                tables(x.TableName).Add x
            End If
        End If
    Next k
    Dim tableName As Variant
    For Each tableName In tables.Keys
        WriteOneExtendedTable CStr(tableName), tables(tableName), f
    Next tableName
End Sub

Private Sub WriteOneExtendedTable(ByVal tableName As String, ByVal records As Collection, ByVal f As Integer)
    If records.Count = 0 Then Exit Sub
    Dim fields As Object: Set fields = Dict()
    Dim fieldOrder As New Collection, x As clsExtendedRecord, i As Long, fld As Variant
    For i = 1 To records.Count
        Set x = records(i)
        For Each fld In x.FieldOrder
            If Not fields.Exists(CStr(fld)) Then fields(CStr(fld)) = True: fieldOrder.Add CStr(fld)
        Next fld
    Next i
    Print #f, "%T" & vbTab & tableName
    Dim line As String: line = "%F"
    For Each fld In fieldOrder: line = line & vbTab & CStr(fld): Next fld
    Print #f, line
    For i = 1 To records.Count
        Set x = records(i): line = "%R"
        For Each fld In fieldOrder: line = line & vbTab & EscapeXER(x.GetField(CStr(fld))): Next fld
        Print #f, line
    Next i
End Sub

Public Sub WriteCombinedUDFTables(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel, ByVal f As Integer)
    Dim types As New Collection, vals As New Collection, k As Variant, x As clsExtendedRecord
    For Each k In model.ExtendedRecords.Keys
        Set x = model.ExtendedRecords(k)
        If x.TableName = "UDFTYPE" Then types.Add x
        If x.TableName = "UDFVALUE" Then vals.Add x
    Next k

    Dim payloads As Object: Set payloads = BIMPayloadsByTask(model)
    If types.Count = 0 And vals.Count = 0 And payloads.Count = 0 Then Exit Sub

    Print #f, "%T" & vbTab & "UDFTYPE"
    Print #f, "%F" & vbTab & "udf_type_id" & vbTab & "table_name" & vbTab & "udf_type_name" & vbTab & "udf_type_label" & vbTab & "logical_data_type"
    Dim i As Long
    For i = 1 To types.Count
        Set x = types(i)
        If UCase$(Left$(x.GetField("udf_type_label"), 9)) <> "BIM_LINK_" Then
            Print #f, "%R" & vbTab & x.GetField("udf_type_id") & vbTab & x.GetField("table_name") & vbTab & _
                      EscapeXER(x.GetField("udf_type_name")) & vbTab & EscapeXER(x.GetField("udf_type_label")) & vbTab & x.GetField("logical_data_type")
        End If
    Next i
    Dim maxChunks As Long, cc As Long, task As Variant
    For Each task In payloads.Keys
        cc = (Len(CStr(payloads(task))) + 239) \ 240
        If cc > maxChunks Then maxChunks = cc
    Next task
    For i = 1 To maxChunks
        Print #f, "%R" & vbTab & (990000 + i) & vbTab & "TASK" & vbTab & "BIM_LINK_" & Format$(i, "00") & vbTab & "BIM_LINK_" & Format$(i, "00") & vbTab & "FT_TEXT"
    Next i

    Print #f, "%T" & vbTab & "UDFVALUE"
    Print #f, "%F" & vbTab & "udf_type_id" & vbTab & "fk_id" & vbTab & "udf_text"
    For i = 1 To vals.Count
        Set x = vals(i)
        Print #f, "%R" & vbTab & x.GetField("udf_type_id") & vbTab & x.GetField("fk_id") & vbTab & EscapeXER(x.GetField("udf_text"))
    Next i
    Dim payload As String
    For Each task In payloads.Keys
        payload = CStr(payloads(task)): cc = (Len(payload) + 239) \ 240
        For i = 1 To cc
            Print #f, "%R" & vbTab & (990000 + i) & vbTab & CStr(task) & vbTab & EscapeXER(Mid$(payload, (i - 1) * 240 + 1, 240))
        Next i
    Next task
End Sub

Private Function BIMPayloadsByTask(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel) As Object
    Dim d As Object, k As Variant, b As clsBIMLink, s As String
    Set d = Dict()
    For Each k In model.BIMLinks.Keys
        Set b = model.BIMLinks(k)
        If d.Exists(b.TaskID) Then s = CStr(d(b.TaskID)) Else s = ""
        If Len(s) > 0 Then s = s & ";"
        d(b.TaskID) = s & b.ModelKey & "~" & b.ObjectID
    Next k
    Set BIMPayloadsByTask = d
End Function


========================================================================

Attribute VB_Name = "modFiltersLayouts"
Option Explicit

Public Sub InitializeFiltersAndLayouts()
    Dim ws As Worksheet
    Set ws = EnsureSheet(SH_FILTERS)
    If ws.Cells(1, 1).Value = "" Then
        WriteRow ws,1,1,Array("Filter Name", "Connector", "Field", "Operator", "Value", "Enabled")
        ws.Rows(1).Font.Bold = True
        WriteRow ws,2,1,Array("Critical / Negative Float","AND","TF h","<=",0,True)
        WriteRow ws,3,1,Array("6 Week Lookahead","AND","Planned Start",">=",Date,True)
        WriteRow ws,4,1,Array("6 Week Lookahead","AND","Planned Start","<=",Date+42,True)
        WriteRow ws,5,1,Array("Incomplete","AND","Status","<>",STATUS_COMPLETE,True)
        ws.Columns.AutoFit
    End If

    Set ws = EnsureSheet(SH_LAYOUTS)
    If ws.Cells(1, 1).Value = "" Then
        WriteRow ws,1,1,Array("Layout", "Field", "Position", "Visible", "Width", "Sort Priority", "Sort Direction", "Group Field")
        ws.Rows(1).Font.Bold = True
        ws.Columns.AutoFit
    End If
End Sub

Public Sub ApplyNamedActivityFilter()
    Dim nm As String
    nm = InputBox("Filter name from the Filters sheet:", "Apply Activity Filter", SafeText(GetSetting("ActiveFilter", "")))
    If Len(nm) = 0 Then Exit Sub
    ApplyActivityFilter nm
End Sub

Public Sub ApplyActivityFilter(ByVal filterName As String)
    TraceEnter "modFiltersLayouts", "ApplyActivityFilter", TraceKV("filterName", filterName)
    On Error GoTo fail
    Dim wsA As Worksheet: Set wsA = Worksheets(SH_ACTIVITIES)
    Dim wsF As Worksheet: Set wsF = Worksheets(SH_FILTERS)
    Dim rules As Collection: Set rules = ReadFilterRules(wsF, filterName)
    If rules.Count = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4300, , "No enabled rules found for filter '" & filterName & "'."

    Dim lastRow As Long: lastRow = wsA.Cells(wsA.Rows.Count, 1).End(xlUp).Row
    Dim r As Long, passed As Boolean
    wsA.Rows.Hidden = False
    For r = 2 To lastRow
        passed = EvaluateRules(wsA, r, rules)
        wsA.Rows(r).Hidden = Not passed
    Next r
    SetSetting "ActiveFilter", filterName
    TraceExit "modFiltersLayouts", "ApplyActivityFilter", "rows=" & CStr(lastRow - 1)
    Exit Sub
fail:
    LogAndShowError "modFiltersLayouts", "ApplyActivityFilter", "The activity filter could not be applied.", _
                    TraceKV("filterName", filterName)
End Sub

Public Sub ClearActivityFilter()
    Worksheets(SH_ACTIVITIES).Rows.Hidden = False
    SetSetting "ActiveFilter", ""
End Sub

Private Function ReadFilterRules(ByVal ws As Worksheet, ByVal filterName As String) As Collection
    Dim c As New Collection, lastRow As Long, r As Long, d As Object
    lastRow = ws.Cells(ws.Rows.Count, 1).End(xlUp).Row
    For r = 2 To lastRow
        If StrComp(Trim$(CStr(ws.Cells(r, 1).Value)), filterName, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then
            If CBool(IIf(ws.Cells(r, 6).Value = "", True, ws.Cells(r, 6).Value)) Then
                Set d = Dict()
                d("Connector") = UCase$(Trim$(CStr(ws.Cells(r, 2).Value)))
                d("Field") = Trim$(CStr(ws.Cells(r, 3).Value))
                d("Operator") = UCase$(Trim$(CStr(ws.Cells(r, 4).Value)))
                d("Value") = ws.Cells(r, 5).Value
                c.Add d
            End If
        End If
    Next r
    Set ReadFilterRules = c
End Function

Private Function EvaluateRules(ByVal ws As Worksheet, ByVal rowNo As Long, ByVal rules As Collection) As Boolean
    Dim i As Long, result As Boolean, current As Boolean, connector As String, d As Object, col As Long
    For i = 1 To rules.Count
        Set d = rules(i)
        col = HeaderColumn(ws, CStr(d("Field")))
        If col = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4301, , "Filter field not found: " & d("Field")
        current = CompareValue(ws.Cells(rowNo, col).Value, CStr(d("Operator")), d("Value"))
        If i = 1 Then
            result = current
        Else
            connector = CStr(d("Connector"))
            If connector = "OR" Then result = result Or current Else result = result And current
        End If
    Next i
    EvaluateRules = result
End Function

Private Function CompareValue(ByVal actual As Variant, ByVal op As String, ByVal target As Variant) As Boolean
    Select Case op
        Case "=", "EQ": CompareValue = (UCase$(SafeText(actual)) = UCase$(SafeText(target)))
        Case "<>", "!=", "NE": CompareValue = (UCase$(SafeText(actual)) <> UCase$(SafeText(target)))
        Case "CONTAINS": CompareValue = (InStr(1, SafeText(actual), SafeText(target), vbTextCompare) > 0)
        Case "BEGINS": CompareValue = (Left$(UCase$(SafeText(actual)), Len(SafeText(target))) = UCase$(SafeText(target)))
        Case "BLANK": CompareValue = (Len(Trim$(SafeText(actual))) = 0)
        Case "NOT BLANK": CompareValue = (Len(Trim$(SafeText(actual))) > 0)
        Case ">", ">=", "<", "<="
            If IsDate(actual) And IsDate(target) Then
                CompareValue = NumericCompare(CDbl(CDate(actual)), op, CDbl(CDate(target)))
            ElseIf IsNumeric(actual) And IsNumeric(target) Then
                CompareValue = NumericCompare(CDbl(actual), op, CDbl(target))
            End If
    End Select
End Function

Private Function NumericCompare(ByVal a As Double, ByVal op As String, ByVal b As Double) As Boolean
    Select Case op
        Case ">": NumericCompare = a > b
        Case ">=": NumericCompare = a >= b
        Case "<": NumericCompare = a < b
        Case "<=": NumericCompare = a <= b
    End Select
End Function

Public Sub SaveCurrentActivityLayout()
    Dim nm As String: nm = InputBox("Layout name:", "Save Layout")
    If Len(nm) = 0 Then Exit Sub
    SaveActivityLayout nm
End Sub

Public Sub SaveActivityLayout(ByVal layoutName As String)
    Dim wsA As Worksheet: Set wsA = Worksheets(SH_ACTIVITIES)
    Dim wsL As Worksheet: Set wsL = Worksheets(SH_LAYOUTS)
    DeleteLayoutRows wsL, layoutName

    Dim c As Long, r As Long, nextRow As Long
    nextRow = wsL.Cells(wsL.Rows.Count, 1).End(xlUp).Row + 1
    For c = 1 To wsA.Cells(1, wsA.Columns.Count).End(xlToLeft).Column
        r = nextRow: nextRow = nextRow + 1
        wsL.Cells(r, 1).Value = layoutName
        wsL.Cells(r, 2).Value = CStr(wsA.Cells(1, c).Value)
        wsL.Cells(r, 3).Value = c
        wsL.Cells(r, 4).Value = Not wsA.Columns(c).Hidden
        wsL.Cells(r, 5).Value = wsA.Columns(c).ColumnWidth
        If c = 1 Then
            wsL.Cells(r, 6).Value = SafeLng(GetSetting("LayoutSortPriority", 0))
            wsL.Cells(r, 7).Value = SafeText(GetSetting("LayoutSortDirection", "ASC"))
            wsL.Cells(r, 8).Value = SafeText(GetSetting("LayoutGroupField", ""))
        End If
    Next c
    WriteAudit "SAVE", "Layout", layoutName, "Activities layout"
End Sub

Public Sub ApplyNamedActivityLayout()
    Dim nm As String: nm = InputBox("Layout name:", "Apply Layout")
    If Len(nm) = 0 Then Exit Sub
    ApplyActivityLayout nm
End Sub

Public Sub ApplyActivityLayout(ByVal layoutName As String)
    Dim wsA As Worksheet: Set wsA = Worksheets(SH_ACTIVITIES)
    Dim wsL As Worksheet: Set wsL = Worksheets(SH_LAYOUTS)
    Dim lastRow As Long: lastRow = wsL.Cells(wsL.Rows.Count, 1).End(xlUp).Row
    Dim r As Long, field As String, c As Long, found As Boolean, groupField As String
    For r = 2 To lastRow
        If StrComp(CStr(wsL.Cells(r, 1).Value), layoutName, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then
            found = True
            field = CStr(wsL.Cells(r, 2).Value)
            c = HeaderColumn(wsA, field)
            If c > 0 Then
                wsA.Columns(c).Hidden = Not CBool(wsL.Cells(r, 4).Value)
                If IsNumeric(wsL.Cells(r, 5).Value) Then wsA.Columns(c).ColumnWidth = CDbl(wsL.Cells(r, 5).Value)
            End If
            If Len(groupField) = 0 Then groupField = SafeText(wsL.Cells(r, 8).Value)
        End If
    Next r
    If Not found Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4302, , "Layout not found: " & layoutName
    If Len(groupField) > 0 Then ApplyGroupingBands wsA, groupField
    SetSetting "ActiveLayout", layoutName
End Sub

Private Sub ApplyGroupingBands(ByVal ws As Worksheet, ByVal groupField As String)
    Dim c As Long: c = HeaderColumn(ws, groupField)
    If c = 0 Then Exit Sub
    Dim lastRow As Long: lastRow = ws.Cells(ws.Rows.Count, 1).End(xlUp).Row
    Dim r As Long
    ws.Range(ws.Cells(2, 1), ws.Cells(lastRow, ws.Cells(1, ws.Columns.Count).End(xlToLeft).Column)).Borders(xlEdgeTop).LineStyle = xlNone
    For r = 3 To lastRow
        If CStr(ws.Cells(r, c).Value) <> CStr(ws.Cells(r - 1, c).Value) Then
            With ws.Range(ws.Cells(r, 1), ws.Cells(r, ws.Cells(1, ws.Columns.Count).End(xlToLeft).Column)).Borders(xlEdgeTop)
                .LineStyle = xlContinuous
                .Weight = xlMedium
            End With
        End If
    Next r
End Sub

Private Sub DeleteLayoutRows(ByVal ws As Worksheet, ByVal layoutName As String)
    Dim r As Long
    For r = ws.Cells(ws.Rows.Count, 1).End(xlUp).Row To 2 Step -1
        If StrComp(CStr(ws.Cells(r, 1).Value), layoutName, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then ws.Rows(r).Delete
    Next r
End Sub

Public Function HeaderColumn(ByVal ws As Worksheet, ByVal headerText As String) As Long
    Dim f As Range
    Set f = ws.Rows(1).Find(What:=headerText, LookAt:=xlWhole, MatchCase:=False)
    If Not f Is Nothing Then HeaderColumn = f.Column
End Function

Attribute VB_Name = "modForensicCommands"
Option Explicit

Public Sub CommandStoreRevision()
    On Error GoTo EH
    Dim nm As String, desc As String
    nm = InputBox("Revision name:", "Store Revision", "Rev " & Format$(Now, "yyyymmdd_hhnn"))
    If Len(Trim$(nm)) = 0 Then Exit Sub
    desc = InputBox("Optional description:", "Store Revision", "")
    StoreCurrentRevision nm, desc
    Exit Sub
EH:
    LogAndShowError "modForensicCommands", "CommandStoreRevision", "The revision could not be stored.", TraceKV("revisionName", nm)
End Sub

Public Sub CommandCompareRevisions()
    On Error GoTo EH
    EnsureRepository
    Dim prevName As String, curName As String
    prevName = InputBox("Previous revision name:", "Compare Revisions")
    If Len(prevName) = 0 Then Exit Sub
    curName = InputBox("Current revision name (leave blank for loaded schedule):", "Compare Revisions")
    Dim p As clsRevision, c As clsRevision, curModel As clsScheduleModel, curLabel As String
    Set p = GetRevisionByName(prevName)
    If p Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 12600, , "Previous revision not found: " & prevName
    If Len(curName) > 0 Then
        Set c = GetRevisionByName(curName)
        If c Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 12601, , "Current revision not found: " & curName
        Set curModel = c.Model
        curLabel = c.RevisionName
    Else
        Set curModel = gSchedule
        curLabel = "Current Loaded Schedule"
    End If
    Dim changes As Collection
    Set changes = CompareScheduleModels(p.Model, curModel, p.RevisionName, curLabel)
    WriteChangeRegister changes
    BuildWhatChangedDashboard changes, p.Model, curModel
    CompareCriticalPathMigration p.Model, curModel, p.RevisionName, curLabel
    EnsureSheet(SH_CHANGE_REGISTER).Activate
    Exit Sub
EH:
    LogAndShowError "modForensicCommands", "CommandCompareRevisions", "Revision comparison failed.", prevName & " -> " & curName
End Sub

Public Sub CommandWhyDateMoved()
    On Error GoTo EH
    Dim prevName As String, taskId As String
    prevName = InputBox("Previous revision name:", "Why Did My Date Move?")
    If Len(prevName) = 0 Then Exit Sub
    taskId = InputBox("Current TaskID:", "Why Did My Date Move?")
    If Len(taskId) = 0 Then Exit Sub
    Dim p As clsRevision
    Set p = GetRevisionByName(prevName)
    If p Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 12602, , "Previous revision not found."
    ExplainDateMovement p.Model, gSchedule, taskId, p.RevisionName, "Current Loaded Schedule"
    EnsureSheet(SH_DATE_MOVE).Activate
    Exit Sub
EH:
    LogAndShowError "modForensicCommands", "CommandWhyDateMoved", "Date-movement analysis failed.", TraceKV("taskId", taskId) & "; previous=" & prevName
End Sub

Public Sub CommandAnalyseSchedule()
    On Error GoTo EH
    TraceEnter "modForensicCommands", "CommandAnalyseSchedule", ScheduleTraceContext()
    If gSchedule Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 12603, , "No schedule loaded."
    CalculateCPM gSchedule
    RunScheduleDiagnostics gSchedule
    BuildScheduleHealth gSchedule
    BuildCriticalPathReport gSchedule
    BuildResourceOverloadAnalysis gSchedule
    BuildExecutiveDashboard gSchedule
    BuildWeeklyResourceCharts
    BuildEVMAndProductivity
    WriteAudit "ANALYSE", "Schedule", gSchedule.ProjectID, "Full deterministic analysis refreshed"
    TraceExit "modForensicCommands", "CommandAnalyseSchedule"
    Exit Sub
EH:
    LogAndShowError "modForensicCommands", "CommandAnalyseSchedule", "Schedule analysis failed.", ScheduleTraceContext()
End Sub

Public Sub CommandTimeMachine()
    Dim taskId As String
    taskId = InputBox("TaskID:", "Schedule Time Machine")
    If Len(taskId) > 0 Then
        BuildTimeMachine taskId
        EnsureSheet(SH_TIME_MACHINE).Activate
    End If
End Sub

    EnsureSheet(SH_FORECAST_STABILITY).Activate

    EnsureSheet(SH_NARRATIVE).Activate
End Sub

Public Sub CommandLookahead()

End Sub

        GlobalSearch q
        EnsureSheet(SH_SEARCH).Activate
    End If

    EnsureSheet(SH_EXEC_DASHBOARD).Activate

========================================================================
FILE: src/modGantt.bas
========================================================================

Attribute VB_Name = "modGantt"
Option Explicit

Public Sub BuildGanttView(Optional ByVal timescale As String = "WEEK")
    TraceEnter "modGantt", "BuildGanttView", TraceKV("timescale", timescale) & "; " & ScheduleTraceContext()
    On Error GoTo fail
    If gSchedule Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4000, , "No schedule loaded."
    SyncModelFromSheets gSchedule

    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = EnsureSheet(SH_GANTT)
    Application.ScreenUpdating = False
    ws.Cells.Clear
    DeleteAllShapes ws

    Dim minD As Date, maxD As Date
    DetermineGanttRange gSchedule, minD, maxD
    If minD = 0 Or maxD = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4001, , "No schedulable dates were found."

    timescale = UCase$(Trim$(timescale))
    If timescale <> "DAY" And timescale <> "WEEK" And timescale <> "MONTH" Then timescale = UCase$(SafeText(GetSetting("GanttTimescale", "WEEK")))
    If timescale <> "DAY" And timescale <> "WEEK" And timescale <> "MONTH" Then timescale = "WEEK"
    SetSetting "GanttTimescale", timescale

    WriteRow ws, 1, 1, Array("TaskID", "Activity ID", "Activity Name", "WBS", "TF h", "Status", "Bar")
    ws.Rows(1).Font.Bold = True
    ws.Rows(1).Interior.Color = RGB(217, 225, 242)

    Dim dateCol As Long: dateCol = 8
    Dim bucketStarts As Collection: Set bucketStarts = New Collection
    Dim d As Date: d = BucketStart(minD, timescale)
    Dim c As Long: c = dateCol
    Do While d <= maxD
        bucketStarts.Add d
        ws.Cells(1, c).Value = BucketCaption(d, timescale)
        ws.Cells(1, c).HorizontalAlignment = xlCenter
        ws.Columns(c).ColumnWidth = IIf(timescale = "DAY", 3#, IIf(timescale = "WEEK", 5#, 8#))
        d = NextBucket(d, timescale)
        c = c + 1
        If c > 16380 Then Exit Do
    Loop

    Dim bl As clsBaseline: Set bl = PrimaryBaseline(gSchedule)
    Dim rowNo As Long: rowNo = 2
    Dim k As Variant, a As clsActivity, ba As clsActivity
    For Each k In SortedActivityKeys(gSchedule)
        Set a = gSchedule.Activities(CStr(k))
        WriteRow ws, rowNo, 1, Array(a.TaskID, a.ActivityID, a.Name, a.WBSID, a.TotalFloatHours, a.Status, "Current")
        RenderCurrentBar ws, rowNo, dateCol, bucketStarts, timescale, a, gSchedule
        rowNo = rowNo + 1

        If CBool(GetSetting("GanttShowBaseline", True)) Then
            If Not bl Is Nothing Then
                If bl.Activities.Exists(a.TaskID) Then
                Set ba = bl.Activities(a.TaskID)
                WriteRow ws, rowNo, 1, Array(a.TaskID, a.ActivityID, a.Name, a.WBSID, "", "", "Baseline")
                ws.Rows(rowNo).Font.Size = 8
                    RenderBaselineBar ws, rowNo, dateCol, bucketStarts, timescale, ba
                    rowNo = rowNo + 1
                End If
            End If
        End If
    Next k

    If CBool(GetSetting("GanttShowDataDate", True)) Then ApplyDataDateMarker ws, dateCol, bucketStarts, timescale, rowNo - 1, gSchedule
    ws.Columns("A:G").AutoFit
    ws.Columns("C").ColumnWidth = 36
    ws.Activate
    ws.Range("A1").Select
    ActiveWindow.FreezePanes = False
    ws.Range("H2").Select
    ActiveWindow.FreezePanes = True
    ws.Range("A1").Select
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    TraceExit "modGantt", "BuildGanttView", "rows=" & CStr(rowNo - 1) & "; timescale=" & timescale
    Exit Sub
fail:
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    LogAndShowError "modGantt", "BuildGanttView", "The Gantt view could not be built.", _
                    "timescale=" & timescale & "; " & ScheduleTraceContext()
End Sub

Private Sub DetermineGanttRange(ByVal m As clsScheduleModel, ByRef minD As Date, ByRef maxD As Date)
    Dim k As Variant, a As clsActivity, bl As clsBaseline, ba As clsActivity
    For Each k In m.Activities.Keys
        Set a = m.Activities(k)
        ConsiderDate minD, maxD, EffectiveStart(a)
        ConsiderDate minD, maxD, EffectiveFinish(a)
    Next k
    Set bl = PrimaryBaseline(m)
    If Not bl Is Nothing Then
        For Each k In bl.Activities.Keys
            Set ba = bl.Activities(k)
            ConsiderDate minD, maxD, EffectiveStart(ba)
            ConsiderDate minD, maxD, EffectiveFinish(ba)
        Next k
    End If
    If minD > 0 Then minD = DateAdd("d", -7, minD)
    If maxD > 0 Then maxD = DateAdd("d", 14, maxD)
End Sub

Private Sub ConsiderDate(ByRef minD As Date, ByRef maxD As Date, ByVal d As Date)
    If d <= 0 Then Exit Sub
    If minD = 0 Or d < minD Then minD = d
    If maxD = 0 Or d > maxD Then maxD = d
End Sub

Private Function EffectiveStart(ByVal a As clsActivity) As Date
    If a.HasActualStart Then EffectiveStart = a.ActualStart: Exit Function
    If a.EarlyStart > 0 Then EffectiveStart = a.EarlyStart: Exit Function
    If a.HasPlannedStart Then EffectiveStart = a.PlannedStart
End Function

Private Function EffectiveFinish(ByVal a As clsActivity) As Date
    If a.HasActualFinish Then EffectiveFinish = a.ActualFinish: Exit Function
    If a.EarlyFinish > 0 Then EffectiveFinish = a.EarlyFinish: Exit Function
    If a.HasExpectedFinish Then EffectiveFinish = a.ExpectedFinish: Exit Function
    If a.HasPlannedFinish Then EffectiveFinish = a.PlannedFinish
End Function

Private Sub RenderCurrentBar(ByVal ws As Worksheet, ByVal rowNo As Long, ByVal startCol As Long, ByVal buckets As Collection, ByVal scale As String, ByVal a As clsActivity, ByVal m As clsScheduleModel)
    Dim s As Date, f As Date, i As Long, bs As Date, be As Date, cell As Range
    s = EffectiveStart(a): f = EffectiveFinish(a)
    If s = 0 Or f = 0 Then Exit Sub
    For i = 1 To buckets.Count
        bs = buckets(i): be = NextBucket(bs, scale)
        If PeriodIntersects(bs, be, s, f) Then
            Set cell = ws.Cells(rowNo, startCol + i - 1)
            If a.TotalFloatHours <= 0 Then
                cell.Interior.Color = GanttStyleColor("Critical", RGB(244, 176, 132))
            Else
                cell.Interior.Color = GanttStyleColor("Current", RGB(91, 155, 213))
            End If
        End If
        If CBool(GetSetting("GanttShowActual", True)) And a.HasActualStart Then
            Dim actualTo As Date
            If a.HasActualFinish Then actualTo = a.ActualFinish ElseIf m.HasDataDate Then actualTo = m.DataDate Else actualTo = a.ActualStart
            If actualTo >= a.ActualStart Then
                If PeriodIntersects(bs, be, a.ActualStart, actualTo) Then ws.Cells(rowNo, startCol + i - 1).Interior.Color = GanttStyleColor("Actual", RGB(112, 173, 71))
            End If
        End If
    Next i
    If a.OriginalDurationHours = 0 Or InStr(UCase$(a.ActivityType), "MILESTONE") > 0 Then
        Dim mc As Long: mc = BucketColumnForDate(buckets, scale, s, startCol)
        If mc > 0 Then
            With ws.Cells(rowNo, mc).Borders
                .Item(xlEdgeLeft).LineStyle = xlContinuous
                .Item(xlEdgeRight).LineStyle = xlContinuous
                .Item(xlEdgeTop).LineStyle = xlContinuous
                .Item(xlEdgeBottom).LineStyle = xlContinuous
            End With
            ws.Cells(rowNo, mc).Value = "◆"
            ws.Cells(rowNo, mc).HorizontalAlignment = xlCenter
        End If
    End If
End Sub

Private Sub RenderBaselineBar(ByVal ws As Worksheet, ByVal rowNo As Long, ByVal startCol As Long, ByVal buckets As Collection, ByVal scale As String, ByVal a As clsActivity)
    Dim s As Date, f As Date, i As Long, bs As Date, be As Date
    s = EffectiveStart(a): f = EffectiveFinish(a)
    If s = 0 Or f = 0 Then Exit Sub
    For i = 1 To buckets.Count
        bs = buckets(i): be = NextBucket(bs, scale)
        If PeriodIntersects(bs, be, s, f) Then ws.Cells(rowNo, startCol + i - 1).Interior.Color = GanttStyleColor("Baseline", RGB(166, 166, 166))
    Next i
End Sub

Private Sub ApplyDataDateMarker(ByVal ws As Worksheet, ByVal startCol As Long, ByVal buckets As Collection, ByVal scale As String, ByVal lastRow As Long, ByVal m As clsScheduleModel)
    If Not m.HasDataDate Then Exit Sub
    Dim c As Long: c = BucketColumnForDate(buckets, scale, m.DataDate, startCol)
    If c = 0 Then Exit Sub
    With ws.Range(ws.Cells(1, c), ws.Cells(lastRow, c)).Borders(xlEdgeLeft)
        .LineStyle = xlContinuous
        .Weight = xlMedium
        .Color = GanttStyleColor("DataDate", RGB(192, 0, 0))
    End With
    ws.Cells(1, c).AddComment "Data Date: " & Format$(m.DataDate, "dd-mmm-yyyy")
End Sub

Private Function PeriodIntersects(ByVal pStart As Date, ByVal pEnd As Date, ByVal aStart As Date, ByVal aEnd As Date) As Boolean
    PeriodIntersects = (aStart < pEnd And aEnd >= pStart)
End Function

Private Function BucketColumnForDate(ByVal buckets As Collection, ByVal scale As String, ByVal target As Date, ByVal startCol As Long) As Long
    Dim i As Long
    For i = 1 To buckets.Count
        If target >= buckets(i) And target < NextBucket(buckets(i), scale) Then BucketColumnForDate = startCol + i - 1: Exit Function
    Next i
End Function

Private Function BucketStart(ByVal d As Date, ByVal scale As String) As Date
    Select Case scale
        Case "DAY": BucketStart = DateValue(d)
        Case "MONTH": BucketStart = DateSerial(Year(d), Month(d), 1)
        Case Else: BucketStart = DateValue(d) - Weekday(DateValue(d), vbMonday) + 1
    End Select
End Function

Private Function NextBucket(ByVal d As Date, ByVal scale As String) As Date
    Select Case scale
        Case "DAY": NextBucket = DateAdd("d", 1, d)
        Case "MONTH": NextBucket = DateAdd("m", 1, d)
        Case Else: NextBucket = DateAdd("d", 7, d)
    End Select
End Function

Private Function BucketCaption(ByVal d As Date, ByVal scale As String) As String
    Select Case scale
        Case "DAY": BucketCaption = Format$(d, "dd-mmm")
        Case "MONTH": BucketCaption = Format$(d, "mmm-yy")
        Case Else: BucketCaption = "W/C " & Format$(d, "dd-mmm")
    End Select
End Function

Public Function PrimaryBaseline(ByVal m As clsScheduleModel) As clsBaseline
    Dim preferred As String: preferred = SafeText(GetSetting("PrimaryBaselineName", ""))
    If Len(preferred) > 0 Then If m.Baselines.Exists(preferred) Then Set PrimaryBaseline = m.Baselines(preferred): Exit Function
    Dim k As Variant, b As clsBaseline
    For Each k In m.Baselines.Keys
        Set b = m.Baselines(k)
        If UCase$(b.Role) = "PRIMARY" Then Set PrimaryBaseline = b: Exit Function
    Next k
End Function

Private Function SortedActivityKeys(ByVal m As clsScheduleModel) As Variant
    Dim arr() As String, i As Long, j As Long, t As String, k As Variant
    ReDim arr(0 To m.Activities.Count - 1)
    i = 0
    For Each k In m.Activities.Keys: arr(i) = CStr(k): i = i + 1: Next k
    For i = LBound(arr) To UBound(arr) - 1
        For j = i + 1 To UBound(arr)
            If UCase$(m.Activities(arr(j)).ActivityID) < UCase$(m.Activities(arr(i)).ActivityID) Then t = arr(i): arr(i) = arr(j): arr(j) = t
        Next j
    Next i
    SortedActivityKeys = arr
End Function

Private Sub DeleteAllShapes(ByVal ws As Worksheet)
    On Error Resume Next
    ws.Shapes.SelectAll
    Selection.Delete
    On Error GoTo 0
End Sub

Attribute VB_Name = "modGanttStyles"
Option Explicit

Public Sub InitializeGanttStyles()
    On Error GoTo EH
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = EnsureSheet(SH_BAR_STYLES)
    If ws.Cells(1, 1).Value = "" Then
        WriteRow ws, 1, 1, Array("Bar Type", "Red", "Green", "Blue", "Enabled", "Description")
        WriteRow ws, 2, 1, Array("Current", 91, 155, 213, True, "Current forecast")
        WriteRow ws, 3, 1, Array("Critical", 244, 176, 132, True, "Critical / zero float")
        WriteRow ws, 4, 1, Array("Actual", 112, 173, 71, True, "Actual/progress overlay")
        WriteRow ws, 5, 1, Array("Baseline", 166, 166, 166, True, "Primary baseline")
        WriteRow ws, 6, 1, Array("DataDate", 192, 0, 0, True, "Data Date marker")
        WriteRow ws, 7, 1, Array("Milestone", 68, 68, 68, True, "Milestone marker")
        ws.Rows(1).Font.Bold = True: ws.Columns.AutoFit
    End If
    Exit Sub
EH:
    TraceError "modGanttStyles", "InitializeGanttStyles", Err.Number, Err.Description, Erl
End Sub

Public Function GanttStyleColor(ByVal barType As String, ByVal fallbackColor As Long) As Long
    On Error GoTo SafeOut
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = EnsureSheet(SH_BAR_STYLES)
    Dim f As Range: Set f = ws.Columns(1).Find(What:=barType, LookAt:=xlWhole, MatchCase:=False)
    If f Is Nothing Then GoTo SafeOut
    If Not CBool(f.Offset(0, 4).Value) Then GanttStyleColor = fallbackColor: Exit Function
    GanttStyleColor = RGB(SafeLng(f.Offset(0, 1).Value), SafeLng(f.Offset(0, 2).Value), SafeLng(f.Offset(0, 3).Value))
    Exit Function
SafeOut:
    GanttStyleColor = fallbackColor
End Function

Public Function GanttStyleEnabled(ByVal barType As String, Optional ByVal fallback As Boolean = True) As Boolean
    On Error GoTo SafeOut
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = EnsureSheet(SH_BAR_STYLES)
    Dim f As Range: Set f = ws.Columns(1).Find(What:=barType, LookAt:=xlWhole, MatchCase:=False)
    If f Is Nothing Then GoTo SafeOut
    GanttStyleEnabled = CBool(f.Offset(0, 4).Value)
    Exit Function
SafeOut:
    GanttStyleEnabled = fallback
End Function


========================================================================
FILE: src/modGlobalSearch.bas
========================================================================

Attribute VB_Name = "modGlobalSearch"
Option Explicit

Public Sub GlobalSearch(ByVal searchText As String)
    On Error GoTo EH
    TraceEnter "modGlobalSearch","GlobalSearch",TraceKV("searchText",searchText)
    If gSchedule Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError+12300,,"No schedule loaded."
    Dim ws As Worksheet:Set ws=EnsureSheet(SH_SEARCH):ws.Cells.Clear
    WriteRow ws, 1, 1, Array("Entity Type","ID","Code/Name","Field","Value","Match")
    ws.Rows(1).Font.Bold=True
    Dim q As String, rowNo As Long, k As Variant, a As clsActivity, w As clsWBS, r As clsResource, x As clsExtendedRecord, f As Variant
    q = LCase$(Trim$(searchText))
    rowNo = 2
    For Each k In gSchedule.Activities.Keys:Set a=gSchedule.Activities(k):If ContainsAny(q,a.TaskID,a.ActivityID,a.Name,a.WBSID,a.CalendarID,a.ConstraintType1) Then SearchRow ws,rowNo,"Activity",a.TaskID,a.ActivityID&" - "&a.Name,"Activity",a.Name,searchText
    Next k
    For Each k In gSchedule.WBS.Keys:Set w=gSchedule.WBS(k):If ContainsAny(q,w.WBSID,w.Code,w.Name,w.Notes) Then SearchRow ws,rowNo,"WBS",w.WBSID,w.Code&" - "&w.Name,"WBS",w.Name,searchText
    Next k
    For Each k In gSchedule.Resources.Keys:Set r=gSchedule.Resources(k):If ContainsAny(q,r.ResourceID,r.ResourceCode,r.Name,r.ResourceType) Then SearchRow ws,rowNo,"Resource",r.ResourceID,r.ResourceCode&" - "&r.Name,"Resource",r.Name,searchText
    Next k
    For Each k In gSchedule.ExtendedRecords.Keys
        Set x=gSchedule.ExtendedRecords(k)
        For Each f In x.FieldOrder
            If InStr(1,LCase$(x.GetField(CStr(f))),q,vbTextCompare)>0 Then SearchRow ws,rowNo,x.TableName,x.RecordKey,x.TableName,CStr(f),x.GetField(CStr(f)),searchText
        Next f
    Next k
    ws.Columns.AutoFit
    TraceExit "modGlobalSearch","GlobalSearch","results="&CStr(rowNo-2)
    Exit Sub
EH:
    LogAndShowError "modGlobalSearch","GlobalSearch","Global search failed.",TraceKV("searchText",searchText)
End Sub

Private Function ContainsAny(ByVal q As String,ParamArray vals() As Variant) As Boolean
    Dim i As Long:If Len(q)=0 Then Exit Function
    For i=LBound(vals) To UBound(vals):If InStr(1,LCase$(SafeText(vals(i))),q,vbTextCompare)>0 Then ContainsAny=True:Exit Function
    Next i
End Function
Private Sub SearchRow(ByVal ws As Worksheet, ByRef rowNo As Long, ByVal entityType As String, ByVal id As String, ByVal label As String, ByVal fieldName As String, ByVal value As String, ByVal query As String)
    WriteRow ws, rowNo, 1, Array(entityType, id, label, fieldName, value, query)
    rowNo = rowNo + 1
End Sub

========================================================================

Attribute VB_Name = "modImportDiagnostics"
Option Explicit

Public Sub BuildImportDiagnosticReport(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel)
    On Error GoTo EH
    TraceEnter "modImportDiagnostics","BuildImportDiagnosticReport",ScheduleTraceContext()
    Dim ws As Worksheet:Set ws=EnsureSheet(SH_IMPORT_DIAGNOSTICS):ws.Cells.Clear
    WriteRow ws, 1, 1, Array("Severity","Check","Count / Value","Detail")
    ws.Rows(1).Font.Bold=True
    Dim rowNo As Long, msg As String
    rowNo = 2
    ImportDiag ws,rowNo,"Info","Source format",model.SourceFormat,model.SourcePath
    ImportDiag ws,rowNo,"Info","Project",model.ProjectID,model.ProjectName
    ImportDiag ws,rowNo,"Info","Activities",model.Activities.Count,""
    ImportDiag ws,rowNo,"Info","Relationships",model.Relationships.Count,""
    ImportDiag ws,rowNo,"Info","Resources",model.Resources.Count,""
    ImportDiag ws,rowNo,"Info","Assignments",model.Assignments.Count,""
    ImportDiag ws,rowNo,"Info","Calendars",model.Calendars.Count,""
    ImportDiag ws,rowNo,"Info","Extended/raw records",model.ExtendedRecords.Count,"Unknown/non-core XER tables are preserved generically."
    If model.Validate(msg) Then ImportDiag ws,rowNo,"Info","Structural validation","PASS","No orphan references detected by canonical model validator." Else ImportDiag ws,rowNo,"Error","Structural validation","FAIL",msg
    ImportDiag ws,rowNo,IIf(model.Activities.Count=0,"Error","Info"),"Missing activities",IIf(model.Activities.Count=0,1,0),IIf(model.Activities.Count=0,"No activities were imported.","")
    ImportDiag ws,rowNo,IIf(model.ProjectID="","Warning","Info"),"Missing project ID",IIf(model.ProjectID="",1,0),""
    ws.Columns.AutoFit
    TraceExit "modImportDiagnostics","BuildImportDiagnosticReport","rows="&CStr(rowNo-2)
    Exit Sub
EH:
    LogAndShowError "modImportDiagnostics","BuildImportDiagnosticReport","The import diagnostic report could not be generated."
End Sub
Private Sub ImportDiag(ByVal ws As Worksheet, ByRef rowNo As Long, ByVal severity As String, ByVal checkName As String, ByVal value As Variant, ByVal detail As String)
    WriteRow ws, rowNo, 1, Array(severity, checkName, value, detail)
    rowNo = rowNo + 1
End Sub

Public Function PreflightXERFile(ByVal filePath As String, ByRef diagnostic As String) As Boolean
    On Error GoTo EH
    If Len(Dir$(filePath)) = 0 Then diagnostic = "File does not exist.": Exit Function
    Dim maxMB As Double: maxMB = SafeDbl(GetSetting("MaxImportFileMB", 500), 500)
    If FileLen(filePath) > maxMB * 1024# * 1024# Then diagnostic = "File exceeds configured maximum import size of " & maxMB & " MB.": Exit Function
    Dim f As Integer, line As String, lineNo As Long, tableCount As Long, fieldCount As Long, recordCount As Long, malformed As Long, hasHeader As Boolean
    f = FreeFile: Open filePath For Input As #f
    Do While Not EOF(f)
        Line Input #f, line: lineNo = lineNo + 1
        If lineNo = 1 Then hasHeader = (InStr(1, line, "ERMHDR", vbTextCompare) > 0)
        If Left$(line, 2) = "%T" Then tableCount = tableCount + 1
        If Left$(line, 2) = "%F" Then fieldCount = fieldCount + 1
        If Left$(line, 2) = "%R" Then recordCount = recordCount + 1
        If Len(line) > 0 Then
            If Left$(line, 1) = "%" And Left$(line, 2) <> "%T" And Left$(line, 2) <> "%F" And Left$(line, 2) <> "%R" And Left$(line, 2) <> "%E" Then malformed = malformed + 1
        End If
    Loop
    Close #f
    diagnostic = "Header=" & CStr(hasHeader) & "; Tables=" & tableCount & "; Field rows=" & fieldCount & "; Records=" & recordCount & "; suspicious control rows=" & malformed
    PreflightXERFile = hasHeader And tableCount > 0 And fieldCount > 0
    Exit Function
EH:
    On Error Resume Next: Close #f: On Error GoTo 0
    diagnostic = "Preflight failed: " & Err.Description
    PreflightXERFile = False
End Function


========================================================================
FILE: src/modLeveling.bas
========================================================================

Attribute VB_Name = "modLeveling"
Option Explicit

Public Sub LevelResources(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel, ByVal ord As Collection)
    Dim usage As Object: Set usage = Dict()
    Dim i As Long, id As String, a As clsActivity, attempts As Long
    For i = 1 To ord.Count
        id = CStr(ord(i))
        Set a = model.Activities(id)
        If a.HasActualFinish Then GoTo nextActivity
        attempts = 0
        Do While Not FitsResourceCapacity(model, a, usage)
            attempts = attempts + 1
            If attempts > 3650 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 1100, , "Resource leveling exceeded 10 years while leveling " & a.ActivityID
            a.EarlyStart = DateAdd("d", 1, a.EarlyStart)
            a.EarlyFinish = DateAdd("d", 1, a.EarlyFinish)
        Loop
        ReserveResourceCapacity model, a, usage
nextActivity:
    Next i
End Sub

Private Function FitsResourceCapacity(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel, ByVal a As clsActivity, ByVal usage As Object) As Boolean
    FitsResourceCapacity = True
    Dim k As Variant, asn As clsAssignment, rs As clsResource, d As Date, endD As Date, dailyDemand As Double, cap As Double, key As String
    For Each k In model.Assignments.Keys
        Set asn = model.Assignments(k)
        If asn.TaskID = a.TaskID And model.Resources.Exists(asn.ResourceID) Then
            Set rs = model.Resources(asn.ResourceID)
            cap = ResourceDailyCapacity(rs)
            If cap > 0 Then
                dailyDemand = AssignmentDailyDemand(a, asn)
                d = DateValue(a.EarlyStart): endD = DateValue(a.EarlyFinish)
                Do While d <= endD
                    key = rs.ResourceID & "|" & Format$(d, "yyyy-mm-dd")
                    Dim used As Double
                    If usage.Exists(key) Then used = SafeDbl(usage(key)) Else used = 0#
                    If used + dailyDemand > cap + 0.000001 Then FitsResourceCapacity = False: Exit Function
                    d = DateAdd("d", 1, d)
                Loop
            End If
        End If
    Next k
End Function

Private Sub ReserveResourceCapacity(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel, ByVal a As clsActivity, ByVal usage As Object)
    Dim k As Variant, asn As clsAssignment, rs As clsResource, d As Date, endD As Date, dailyDemand As Double, key As String
    For Each k In model.Assignments.Keys
        Set asn = model.Assignments(k)
        If asn.TaskID = a.TaskID And model.Resources.Exists(asn.ResourceID) Then
            Set rs = model.Resources(asn.ResourceID)
            dailyDemand = AssignmentDailyDemand(a, asn)
            d = DateValue(a.EarlyStart): endD = DateValue(a.EarlyFinish)
            Do While d <= endD
                key = rs.ResourceID & "|" & Format$(d, "yyyy-mm-dd")
                If usage.Exists(key) Then usage(key) = CDbl(usage(key)) + dailyDemand Else usage(key) = dailyDemand
                d = DateAdd("d", 1, d)
            Loop
        End If
    Next k
End Sub

Private Function AssignmentDailyDemand(ByVal a As clsActivity, ByVal asn As clsAssignment) As Double
    Dim days As Long: days = DateDiff("d", DateValue(a.EarlyStart), DateValue(a.EarlyFinish)) + 1
    If days < 1 Then days = 1
    Dim qty As Double: qty = asn.RemainingUnits
    If qty <= 0 Then qty = asn.BudgetUnits
    AssignmentDailyDemand = qty / days
End Function

Private Function ResourceDailyCapacity(ByVal rs As clsResource) As Double
    ResourceDailyCapacity = rs.MaxUnitsPerHour * SafeDbl(GetSetting("HoursPerDay", 8), 8)
End Function


========================================================================
FILE: src/modLookahead.bas
========================================================================

Attribute VB_Name = "modLookahead"
Option Explicit

Public Sub BuildLookahead(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel,ByVal weeks As Long,Optional ByVal wbsFilter As String="",Optional ByVal statusFilter As String="")
    On Error GoTo EH
    TraceEnter "modLookahead","BuildLookahead","weeks="&weeks&"; wbs="&wbsFilter&"; status="&statusFilter
    Dim ws As Worksheet:Set ws=EnsureSheet(SH_LOOKAHEAD):ws.Cells.Clear
    WriteRow ws, 1, 1, Array("TaskID","Activity ID","Name","WBS","Status","Start","Finish","Remaining h","TF h","Calendar","Constraint","Resources","Critical","Weeks")
    ws.Rows(1).Font.Bold=True
    Dim anchor As Date:anchor=IIf(model.HasDataDate,model.DataDate,Date)
    Dim endD As Date:endD=DateAdd("d",weeks*7,anchor)
    Dim rowNo As Long, k As Variant, a As clsActivity, s As Date, f As Date
    rowNo = 2
    For Each k In model.Activities.Keys
        Set a=model.Activities(k)
        If Len(wbsFilter)>0 Then If a.WBSID<>wbsFilter Then GoTo NextA
        If Len(statusFilter)>0 Then If StrComp(a.Status,statusFilter,vbTextCompare)<>0 Then GoTo NextA
        If a.PercentComplete>=100 Then GoTo NextA
        s=IIf(a.HasPlannedStart,a.PlannedStart,a.EarlyStart):f=IIf(a.HasPlannedFinish,a.PlannedFinish,a.EarlyFinish)
        If (s>=anchor And s<=endD) Or (f>=anchor And f<=endD) Or (s<anchor And f>anchor) Then
            WriteRow ws, rowNo, 1, Array(a.TaskID,a.ActivityID,a.Name,a.WBSID,a.Status,s,f,a.RemainingDurationHours,a.TotalFloatHours,a.CalendarID,a.ConstraintType1,ActivityResourceText(model,a.TaskID),a.TotalFloatHours<=SafeDbl(GetSetting("CriticalFloatThresholdHours",0),0),weeks)
            rowNo=rowNo+1
        End If
NextA:
    Next k
    ws.Columns.AutoFit
    TraceExit "modLookahead","BuildLookahead","rows="&CStr(rowNo-2)
    Exit Sub
EH:
    LogAndShowError "modLookahead","BuildLookahead","The lookahead could not be generated.","weeks="&weeks
End Sub

Private Function ActivityResourceText(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel,ByVal taskId As String) As String
    Dim s As String,k As Variant,a As clsAssignment,r As clsResource
    For Each k In model.Assignments.Keys
        Set a=model.Assignments(k)
        If a.TaskID=taskId Then
            If Len(s)>0 Then s=s&"; "
            If model.Resources.Exists(a.ResourceID) Then Set r=model.Resources(a.ResourceID):s=s&r.Name Else s=s&a.ResourceID
        End If
    Next k
    ActivityResourceText=s
End Function
