========================================================================
FILE: src/modMateriality.bas
========================================================================

Attribute VB_Name = "modMateriality"
Option Explicit

Public Function ClassifyChangeMateriality(ByVal ch As clsChangeRecord) As String
    Dim dateThreshold As Double: dateThreshold=SafeDbl(GetSetting("MaterialDateChangeDays",5),5)
    Dim floatThreshold As Double: floatThreshold=SafeDbl(GetSetting("MaterialFloatChangeDays",10),10)*24#
    Dim lagThreshold As Double: lagThreshold=SafeDbl(GetSetting("MaterialLagChangeHours",40),40)
    Dim f As String: f=UCase$(ch.FieldName)
    If ch.ChangeType="Deleted" And (ch.EntityType="Relationship" Or ch.EntityType="Activity") Then ClassifyChangeMateriality="Critical": Exit Function
    If ch.ChangeType="Added" And InStr(f,"CONSTRAINT")>0 Then ClassifyChangeMateriality="Critical": Exit Function
    If InStr(f,"FINISH")>0 Or InStr(f,"START")>0 Then
        If Abs(ch.Difference)>=dateThreshold Then ClassifyChangeMateriality="Material" Else ClassifyChangeMateriality="Minor"
        Exit Function
    End If
    If InStr(f,"FLOAT")>0 Then
        If Abs(ch.Difference)>=floatThreshold Then ClassifyChangeMateriality="Material" Else ClassifyChangeMateriality="Minor"
        Exit Function
    End If
    If InStr(f,"LAG")>0 Then
        If Abs(ch.Difference)>=lagThreshold Then ClassifyChangeMateriality="Material" Else ClassifyChangeMateriality="Minor"
        Exit Function
    End If
    If ch.ChangeType="Added" Or ch.ChangeType="Deleted" Then ClassifyChangeMateriality="Material" Else ClassifyChangeMateriality="Informational"
End Function


========================================================================
FILE: src/modMSPXML.bas
========================================================================

Attribute VB_Name = "modMSPXML"
Option Explicit

Public Function ParseMSPXML(ByVal filePath As String) As clsScheduleModel
    TraceEnter "modMSPXML", "ParseMSPXML", TraceKV("filePath", filePath)
    Dim xml As Object: Set xml = CreateObject("MSXML2.DOMDocument.6.0")
    xml.async = False: xml.validateOnParse = False
    If Not xml.Load(filePath) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2100, , xml.parseError.reason

    Dim model As New clsScheduleModel, d As Date
    model.ProjectName = XText(xml, "//*[local-name()='Project']/*[local-name()='Name']")
    model.ProjectID = XText(xml, "//*[local-name()='Project']/*[local-name()='GUID']")
    If Len(model.ProjectID) = 0 Then model.ProjectID = "MSP"
    If TryDate(XText(xml, "//*[local-name()='Project']/*[local-name()='StatusDate']"), d) Then model.DataDate = d: model.HasDataDate = True

    ParseMSPExtendedAttributeDefinitions xml, model
    ParseMSPCalendars xml, model
    ParseMSPTasks xml, model
    ParseMSPResources xml, model
    ParseMSPAssignments xml, model

    model.SourceFormat = "MSPXML": model.SourcePath = filePath
    Set ParseMSPXML = model
End Function

Private Sub ParseMSPTasks(ByVal xml As Object, ByVal model As clsScheduleModel)
    Dim nodes As Object, n As Object, summaryByLevel As Object: Set summaryByLevel = Dict()
    Set nodes = xml.SelectNodes("//*[local-name()='Tasks']/*[local-name()='Task']")
    Dim uid As String, level As Long, wbsId As String, d As Date
    Dim primary As New clsBaseline: primary.Name = "MSP Baseline 0": primary.Role = "Primary": primary.ImportedOn = Now
    Dim hasBaseline As Boolean

    For Each n In nodes
        uid = Child(n, "UID"): level = SafeLng(Child(n, "OutlineLevel"), 1)
        If Child(n, "Summary") = "1" Then
            Dim w As New clsWBS
            w.WBSID = "WBS-" & uid
            w.Code = Child(n, "WBS"): If Len(w.Code) = 0 Then w.Code = Child(n, "OutlineNumber")
            w.Name = Child(n, "Name"): w.Seq = model.WBS.Count + 1
            If level > 1 Then If summaryByLevel.Exists(CStr(level - 1)) Then w.ParentWBSID = CStr(summaryByLevel(CStr(level - 1)))
            Set model.WBS(w.WBSID) = w
            summaryByLevel(CStr(level)) = w.WBSID
            RemoveHigherLevels summaryByLevel, level
        Else
            Dim a As New clsActivity
            a.TaskID = uid
            a.ActivityID = Child(n, "WBS"): If Len(a.ActivityID) = 0 Then a.ActivityID = "T" & uid
            a.Name = Child(n, "Name")
            If level > 1 Then If summaryByLevel.Exists(CStr(level - 1)) Then a.WBSID = CStr(summaryByLevel(CStr(level - 1)))
            a.CalendarID = Child(n, "CalendarUID")
            a.OriginalDurationHours = DurH(Child(n, "Duration"))
            a.RemainingDurationHours = DurH(Child(n, "RemainingDuration"))
            a.PercentComplete = SafeDbl(Child(n, "PercentComplete"))
            If TryDate(Child(n, "Start"), d) Then a.PlannedStart = d: a.HasPlannedStart = True
            If TryDate(Child(n, "Finish"), d) Then a.PlannedFinish = d: a.HasPlannedFinish = True
            If TryDate(Child(n, "ActualStart"), d) Then a.ActualStart = d: a.HasActualStart = True
            If TryDate(Child(n, "ActualFinish"), d) Then a.ActualFinish = d: a.HasActualFinish = True
            a.ActualCost = SafeDbl(StripCurrency(Child(n, "ActualCost")))
            a.BudgetCost = SafeDbl(StripCurrency(Child(n, "Cost")))
            a.ConstraintType1 = MSPConstraintToText(SafeLng(Child(n, "ConstraintType"), 0))
            If TryDate(Child(n, "ConstraintDate"), d) Then a.ConstraintDate1 = d: a.HasConstraintDate1 = True
            If a.HasActualFinish Then a.Status = STATUS_COMPLETE ElseIf a.HasActualStart Or a.PercentComplete > 0 Then a.Status = STATUS_IN_PROGRESS Else a.Status = STATUS_NOT_STARTED
            Set model.Activities(a.TaskID) = a
            ParseMSPPredecessors n, a, model
            ParseTaskBIM n, a, model
            ParseTaskExtendedAttributes n, a, model
            If ParseTaskBaseline0(n, a, primary) Then hasBaseline = True
        End If
    Next n
    If hasBaseline Then Set model.Baselines(primary.Name) = primary
End Sub

Private Sub ParseMSPPredecessors(ByVal taskNode As Object, ByVal a As clsActivity, ByVal model As clsScheduleModel)
    Dim p As Object, r As clsRelationship
    For Each p In taskNode.SelectNodes("./*[local-name()='PredecessorLink']")
        Set r = New clsRelationship
        r.RelID = a.TaskID & "-" & Child(p, "PredecessorUID") & "-" & CStr(model.Relationships.Count + 1)
        r.PredTaskID = Child(p, "PredecessorUID"): r.SuccTaskID = a.TaskID
        r.RelType = RelTypeText(SafeLng(Child(p, "Type"), 1))
        r.LagHours = SafeDbl(Child(p, "LinkLag")) / 600#
        Set model.Relationships(r.RelID) = r
    Next p
End Sub

Private Sub ParseTaskBIM(ByVal taskNode As Object, ByVal a As clsActivity, ByVal model As clsScheduleModel)
    Dim x As Object, value As String, refs As Variant, ref As Variant, pair As Variant, b As clsBIMLink
    For Each x In taskNode.SelectNodes("./*[local-name()='ExtendedAttribute']")
        value = Child(x, "Value")
        If InStr(value, "~") > 0 Then
            refs = Split(value, ";")
            For Each ref In refs
                pair = Split(CStr(ref), "~")
                If UBound(pair) >= 1 Then
                    Set b = New clsBIMLink
                    b.TaskID = a.TaskID: b.ModelKey = CStr(pair(0)): b.ObjectID = CStr(pair(1))
                    b.LinkID = b.TaskID & "|" & b.ModelKey & "|" & b.ObjectID
                    If Not model.BIMLinks.Exists(b.LinkID) Then Set model.BIMLinks(b.LinkID) = b
                End If
            Next ref
        End If
    Next x
End Sub


Private Sub ParseMSPExtendedAttributeDefinitions(ByVal xml As Object, ByVal model As clsScheduleModel)
    Dim n As Object, rec As Object
    For Each n In xml.SelectNodes("//*[local-name()='Project']/*[local-name()='ExtendedAttributes']/*[local-name()='ExtendedAttribute']")
        Set rec = Dict()
        rec("field_id") = Child(n, "FieldID")
        rec("field_name") = Child(n, "FieldName")
        rec("alias") = Child(n, "Alias")
        If UCase$(Child(n, "Alias")) <> "BIM_LINK_01" Then StoreExtendedRecord model, "MSP_EXTATTR_DEF", rec
    Next n
End Sub

Private Sub ParseTaskExtendedAttributes(ByVal taskNode As Object, ByVal a As clsActivity, ByVal model As clsScheduleModel)
    Dim n As Object, rec As Object, value As String
    For Each n In taskNode.SelectNodes("./*[local-name()='ExtendedAttribute']")
        value = Child(n, "Value")
        If InStr(value, "~") = 0 Then
            Set rec = Dict()
            rec("task_id") = a.TaskID
            rec("field_id") = Child(n, "FieldID")
            rec("value") = value
            rec("duration_value") = Child(n, "DurationValue")
            rec("date_value") = Child(n, "DateValue")
            rec("flag_value") = Child(n, "FlagValue")
            StoreExtendedRecord model, "MSP_TASK_EXTATTR", rec
        End If
    Next n
End Sub

Private Function ParseTaskBaseline0(ByVal taskNode As Object, ByVal a As clsActivity, ByVal baseline As clsBaseline) As Boolean
    Dim bNode As Object, n As Object, ba As clsActivity, d As Date
    For Each n In taskNode.SelectNodes("./*[local-name()='Baseline']")
        If SafeLng(Child(n, "Number"), -1) = 0 Then
            Set ba = a.Clone
            If TryDate(Child(n, "Start"), d) Then ba.PlannedStart = d: ba.HasPlannedStart = True
            If TryDate(Child(n, "Finish"), d) Then ba.PlannedFinish = d: ba.HasPlannedFinish = True
            If Len(Child(n, "Duration")) > 0 Then ba.OriginalDurationHours = DurH(Child(n, "Duration"))
            If Len(Child(n, "Cost")) > 0 Then ba.BudgetCost = SafeDbl(StripCurrency(Child(n, "Cost")))
            Set baseline.Activities(a.TaskID) = ba
            ParseTaskBaseline0 = True
            Exit Function
        End If
    Next n
End Function

Private Sub ParseMSPResources(ByVal xml As Object, ByVal model As clsScheduleModel)
    Dim n As Object, rs As clsResource
    For Each n In xml.SelectNodes("//*[local-name()='Resources']/*[local-name()='Resource']")
        If Child(n, "UID") <> "0" Then
            Set rs = New clsResource
            rs.ResourceID = Child(n, "UID"): rs.ResourceCode = Child(n, "ID"): rs.Name = Child(n, "Name")
            rs.ResourceType = Child(n, "Type"): rs.MaxUnitsPerHour = SafeDbl(Child(n, "MaxUnits"))
            rs.PricePerUnit = SafeDbl(StripCurrency(Child(n, "StandardRate"))): rs.CalendarID = Child(n, "CalendarUID")
            Set model.Resources(rs.ResourceID) = rs
        End If
    Next n
End Sub

Private Sub ParseMSPAssignments(ByVal xml As Object, ByVal model As clsScheduleModel)
    Dim n As Object, a As clsAssignment
    For Each n In xml.SelectNodes("//*[local-name()='Assignments']/*[local-name()='Assignment']")
        Set a = New clsAssignment
        a.AssignmentID = Child(n, "UID"): a.TaskID = Child(n, "TaskUID"): a.ResourceID = Child(n, "ResourceUID")
        a.BudgetUnits = DurH(Child(n, "Work")): a.ActualUnits = DurH(Child(n, "ActualWork")): a.RemainingUnits = DurH(Child(n, "RemainingWork"))
        a.BudgetCost = SafeDbl(StripCurrency(Child(n, "Cost"))): a.ActualCost = SafeDbl(StripCurrency(Child(n, "ActualCost"))): a.RemainingCost = SafeDbl(StripCurrency(Child(n, "RemainingCost")))
        Set model.Assignments(a.AssignmentID) = a
    Next n
End Sub

Private Sub ParseMSPCalendars(ByVal xml As Object, ByVal model As clsScheduleModel)
    Dim n As Object, c As clsCalendar, wd As Object, dayType As Long, hours As Double, wt As Object, fr As Date, toD As Date
    For Each n In xml.SelectNodes("//*[local-name()='Calendars']/*[local-name()='Calendar']")
        Set c = New clsCalendar
        c.CalendarID = Child(n, "UID"): c.Name = Child(n, "Name")
        For Each wd In n.SelectNodes("./*[local-name()='WeekDays']/*[local-name()='WeekDay']")
            dayType = SafeLng(Child(wd, "DayType"), 0): hours = 0
            For Each wt In wd.SelectNodes("./*[local-name()='WorkingTimes']/*[local-name()='WorkingTime']")
                If TryDate(Child(wt, "FromTime"), fr) And TryDate(Child(wt, "ToTime"), toD) Then hours = hours + (toD - fr) * 24#
            Next wt
            If dayType >= 2 And dayType <= 8 Then c.SetWeekdayHours dayType - 1, hours
        Next wd
        c.HoursPerDay = 8
        Set model.Calendars(c.CalendarID) = c
    Next n
End Sub

Private Sub RemoveHigherLevels(ByVal d As Object, ByVal level As Long)
    Dim k As Variant, doomed As New Collection
    For Each k In d.Keys: If SafeLng(k) > level Then doomed.Add CStr(k)
    Next k
    For Each k In doomed: d.Remove CStr(k): Next k
End Sub

Private Function XText(ByVal xml As Object, ByVal xp As String) As String
    Dim n As Object: Set n = xml.SelectSingleNode(xp)
    If Not n Is Nothing Then XText = n.Text
End Function
Private Function Child(ByVal n As Object, ByVal nm As String) As String
    Dim x As Object: Set x = n.SelectSingleNode("./*[local-name()='" & nm & "']")
    If Not x Is Nothing Then Child = x.Text
End Function
Private Function DurH(ByVal s As String) As Double
    If Len(s) = 0 Then Exit Function
    Dim re As Object, m As Object: Set re = CreateObject("VBScript.RegExp")
    re.Pattern = "P(?:(\d+)D)?(?:T(?:(\d+)H)?(?:(\d+)M)?(?:(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)S)?)?"
    If re.Test(s) Then
        Set m = re.Execute(s)(0)
        DurH = SafeDbl(m.SubMatches(0)) * 8# + SafeDbl(m.SubMatches(1)) + SafeDbl(m.SubMatches(2)) / 60# + SafeDbl(m.SubMatches(3)) / 3600#
    End If
End Function
Private Function RelTypeText(ByVal n As Long) As String
    Select Case n: Case 0: RelTypeText = "FF": Case 1: RelTypeText = "FS": Case 2: RelTypeText = "SF": Case 3: RelTypeText = "SS": Case Else: RelTypeText = "FS": End Select
End Function
Private Function RelMSP(ByVal t As String) As Long
    Select Case UCase$(t): Case "FF": RelMSP = 0: Case "FS": RelMSP = 1: Case "SF": RelMSP = 2: Case "SS": RelMSP = 3: Case Else: RelMSP = 1: End Select
End Function
Private Function StripCurrency(ByVal s As String) As String
    s = Replace(s, "$", ""): s = Replace(s, "€", ""): s = Replace(s, "£", ""): s = Replace(s, ",", "")
    StripCurrency = s
End Function
Private Function HoursToDuration(ByVal h As Double) As String
    HoursToDuration = "PT" & Replace(Format$(h, "0.########"), ",", ".") & "H"
End Function
Private Function MSPConstraintToText(ByVal n As Long) As String
    Select Case n
        Case 1: MSPConstraintToText = "As Soon As Possible"
        Case 2: MSPConstraintToText = "As Late As Possible"
        Case 3: MSPConstraintToText = "Must Start On"
        Case 4: MSPConstraintToText = "Must Finish On"
        Case 5: MSPConstraintToText = "Start No Earlier Than"
        Case 6: MSPConstraintToText = "Start No Later Than"
        Case 7: MSPConstraintToText = "Finish No Earlier Than"
        Case 8: MSPConstraintToText = "Finish No Later Than"
    End Select
End Function
Private Function TextConstraintToMSP(ByVal t As String) As Long
    t = UCase$(t)
    If InStr(t, "MUST START") > 0 Or t = "CS_MSO" Then TextConstraintToMSP = 3 ElseIf InStr(t, "MUST FINISH") > 0 Or t = "CS_MFO" Then TextConstraintToMSP = 4 ElseIf InStr(t, "START NO EARLIER") > 0 Or t = "CS_SNET" Then TextConstraintToMSP = 5 ElseIf InStr(t, "START NO LATER") > 0 Then TextConstraintToMSP = 6 ElseIf InStr(t, "FINISH NO EARLIER") > 0 Then TextConstraintToMSP = 7 ElseIf InStr(t, "FINISH NO LATER") > 0 Or t = "CS_FNLT" Then TextConstraintToMSP = 8 Else TextConstraintToMSP = 1
End Function

Public Sub SaveModelAsMSPXML(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel, ByVal filePath As String)
    TraceEnter "modMSPXML", "SaveModelAsMSPXML", TraceKV("filePath", filePath) & "; activities=" & CStr(model.Activities.Count)
    Dim msg As String
    If Not model.Validate(msg) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2200, , "MSP XML export blocked: " & msg
    Dim taskMap As Object: Set taskMap = NumericIdMap(model.Activities)
    Dim resourceMap As Object: Set resourceMap = NumericIdMap(model.Resources)
    Dim calendarMap As Object: Set calendarMap = NumericIdMap(model.Calendars)
    Dim f As Integer: f = FreeFile
    Open filePath For Output As #f
    Print #f, "<?xml version=""1.0"" encoding=""UTF-8""?>"
    Print #f, "<Project xmlns=""http://schemas.microsoft.com/project"">"
    Print #f, "<Name>" & XmlEscape(model.ProjectName) & "</Name>"
    If model.HasDataDate Then Print #f, "<StatusDate>" & IsoDate(model.DataDate) & "</StatusDate>"
    WriteMSPExtendedAttributeDefinitions model, f
    WriteMSPCalendars model, calendarMap, f
    WriteMSPTasks model, taskMap, calendarMap, f
    WriteMSPResources model, resourceMap, calendarMap, f
    WriteMSPAssignments model, taskMap, resourceMap, f
    Print #f, "</Project>"
    Close #f
    TraceExit "modMSPXML", "SaveModelAsMSPXML", TraceKV("filePath", filePath)
End Sub


Private Sub WriteMSPExtendedAttributeDefinitions(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel, ByVal f As Integer)
    Print #f, "<ExtendedAttributes>"
    Dim k As Variant, x As clsExtendedRecord
    For Each k In model.ExtendedRecords.Keys
        Set x = model.ExtendedRecords(k)
        If x.TableName = "MSP_EXTATTR_DEF" Then
            Print #f, "<ExtendedAttribute><FieldID>" & XmlEscape(x.GetField("field_id")) & "</FieldID>" & _
                      "<FieldName>" & XmlEscape(x.GetField("field_name")) & "</FieldName>" & _
                      "<Alias>" & XmlEscape(x.GetField("alias")) & "</Alias></ExtendedAttribute>"
        End If
    Next k
    Print #f, "<ExtendedAttribute><FieldID>188743731</FieldID><FieldName>Text1</FieldName><Alias>BIM_LINK_01</Alias></ExtendedAttribute>"
    Print #f, "</ExtendedAttributes>"
End Sub

Private Sub WriteTaskMSPExtendedAttributes(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel, ByVal taskId As String, ByVal f As Integer)
    Dim k As Variant, x As clsExtendedRecord
    For Each k In model.ExtendedRecords.Keys
        Set x = model.ExtendedRecords(k)
        If x.TableName = "MSP_TASK_EXTATTR" And x.GetField("task_id") = taskId Then
            Print #f, "<ExtendedAttribute><FieldID>" & XmlEscape(x.GetField("field_id")) & "</FieldID>"
            If Len(x.GetField("value")) > 0 Then Print #f, "<Value>" & XmlEscape(x.GetField("value")) & "</Value>"
            If Len(x.GetField("duration_value")) > 0 Then Print #f, "<DurationValue>" & XmlEscape(x.GetField("duration_value")) & "</DurationValue>"
            If Len(x.GetField("date_value")) > 0 Then Print #f, "<DateValue>" & XmlEscape(x.GetField("date_value")) & "</DateValue>"
            If Len(x.GetField("flag_value")) > 0 Then Print #f, "<FlagValue>" & XmlEscape(x.GetField("flag_value")) & "</FlagValue>"
            Print #f, "</ExtendedAttribute>"
        End If
    Next k
End Sub

Private Sub WriteMSPCalendars(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel, ByVal map As Object, ByVal f As Integer)
    Print #f, "<Calendars>"
    Dim k As Variant, c As clsCalendar, i As Long, dayH As Double, wd As Long
    For Each k In model.Calendars.Keys
        Set c = model.Calendars(k)
        Print #f, "<Calendar><UID>" & map(CStr(k)) & "</UID><Name>" & XmlEscape(c.Name) & "</Name><IsBaseCalendar>1</IsBaseCalendar><WeekDays>"
        For wd = 1 To 7
            dayH = c.WorkingHoursOnDate(Date - Weekday(Date, vbMonday) + wd)
            Print #f, "<WeekDay><DayType>" & (wd + 1) & "</DayType><DayWorking>" & IIf(dayH > 0, 1, 0) & "</DayWorking>"
            If dayH > 0 Then Print #f, "<WorkingTimes><WorkingTime><FromTime>08:00:00</FromTime><ToTime>" & Format$(TimeSerial(8, 0, 0) + dayH / 24#, "hh:nn:ss") & "</ToTime></WorkingTime></WorkingTimes>"
            Print #f, "</WeekDay>"
        Next wd
        Print #f, "</WeekDays></Calendar>"
    Next k
    Print #f, "</Calendars>"
End Sub

Private Sub WriteMSPTasks(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel, ByVal taskMap As Object, ByVal calendarMap As Object, ByVal f As Integer)
    Print #f, "<Tasks>"
    Dim k As Variant, a As clsActivity, r As clsRelationship, bl As clsBaseline, ba As clsActivity
    Set bl = PrimaryBaseline(model)
    For Each k In model.Activities.Keys
        Set a = model.Activities(k)
        Print #f, "<Task><UID>" & taskMap(CStr(k)) & "</UID><ID>" & taskMap(CStr(k)) & "</ID><Name>" & XmlEscape(a.Name) & "</Name><WBS>" & XmlEscape(a.ActivityID) & "</WBS><Summary>0</Summary>"
        Print #f, "<Duration>" & HoursToDuration(a.OriginalDurationHours) & "</Duration><RemainingDuration>" & HoursToDuration(a.RemainingDurationHours) & "</RemainingDuration><PercentComplete>" & a.PercentComplete & "</PercentComplete>"
        If a.HasPlannedStart Then Print #f, "<Start>" & IsoDate(a.PlannedStart) & "</Start>"
        If a.HasPlannedFinish Then Print #f, "<Finish>" & IsoDate(a.PlannedFinish) & "</Finish>"
        If a.HasActualStart Then Print #f, "<ActualStart>" & IsoDate(a.ActualStart) & "</ActualStart>"
        If a.HasActualFinish Then Print #f, "<ActualFinish>" & IsoDate(a.ActualFinish) & "</ActualFinish>"
        If Len(a.CalendarID) > 0 Then If calendarMap.Exists(a.CalendarID) Then Print #f, "<CalendarUID>" & calendarMap(a.CalendarID) & "</CalendarUID>"
        If Len(a.ConstraintType1) > 0 Then Print #f, "<ConstraintType>" & TextConstraintToMSP(a.ConstraintType1) & "</ConstraintType>"
        If a.HasConstraintDate1 Then Print #f, "<ConstraintDate>" & IsoDate(a.ConstraintDate1) & "</ConstraintDate>"
        Print #f, "<Cost>" & a.BudgetCost & "</Cost><ActualCost>" & a.ActualCost & "</ActualCost>"
        For Each r In model.Relationships.Items
            If r.SuccTaskID = a.TaskID And Not r.IsExternal Then If taskMap.Exists(r.PredTaskID) Then Print #f, "<PredecessorLink><PredecessorUID>" & taskMap(r.PredTaskID) & "</PredecessorUID><Type>" & RelMSP(r.RelType) & "</Type><LinkLag>" & CLng(r.LagHours * 600#) & "</LinkLag></PredecessorLink>"
        Next r
        Dim payload As String: payload = BIMPayloadForTask(model, a.TaskID)
        If Len(payload) > 0 Then Print #f, "<ExtendedAttribute><FieldID>188743731</FieldID><Value>" & XmlEscape(payload) & "</Value></ExtendedAttribute>"
        WriteTaskMSPExtendedAttributes model, a.TaskID, f
        If Not bl Is Nothing Then
            If bl.Activities.Exists(a.TaskID) Then
                Set ba = bl.Activities(a.TaskID)
                Print #f, "<Baseline><Number>0</Number>"
                If ba.HasPlannedStart Then Print #f, "<Start>" & IsoDate(ba.PlannedStart) & "</Start>"
                If ba.HasPlannedFinish Then Print #f, "<Finish>" & IsoDate(ba.PlannedFinish) & "</Finish>"
                Print #f, "<Duration>" & HoursToDuration(ba.OriginalDurationHours) & "</Duration><Cost>" & ba.BudgetCost & "</Cost></Baseline>"
            End If
        End If
        Print #f, "</Task>"
    Next k
    Print #f, "</Tasks>"
End Sub

Private Sub WriteMSPResources(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel, ByVal resourceMap As Object, ByVal calendarMap As Object, ByVal f As Integer)
    Print #f, "<Resources>"
    Dim k As Variant, r As clsResource
    For Each k In model.Resources.Keys
        Set r = model.Resources(k)
        Print #f, "<Resource><UID>" & resourceMap(CStr(k)) & "</UID><ID>" & resourceMap(CStr(k)) & "</ID><Name>" & XmlEscape(r.Name) & "</Name><MaxUnits>" & r.MaxUnitsPerHour & "</MaxUnits><StandardRate>" & r.PricePerUnit & "</StandardRate>"
        If Len(r.CalendarID) > 0 Then If calendarMap.Exists(r.CalendarID) Then Print #f, "<CalendarUID>" & calendarMap(r.CalendarID) & "</CalendarUID>"
        Print #f, "</Resource>"
    Next k
    Print #f, "</Resources>"
End Sub

Private Sub WriteMSPAssignments(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel, ByVal taskMap As Object, ByVal resourceMap As Object, ByVal f As Integer)
    Print #f, "<Assignments>"
    Dim k As Variant, a As clsAssignment, uid As Long: uid = 1
    For Each k In model.Assignments.Keys
        Set a = model.Assignments(k)
        If taskMap.Exists(a.TaskID) And resourceMap.Exists(a.ResourceID) Then
            Print #f, "<Assignment><UID>" & uid & "</UID><TaskUID>" & taskMap(a.TaskID) & "</TaskUID><ResourceUID>" & resourceMap(a.ResourceID) & "</ResourceUID><Work>" & HoursToDuration(a.BudgetUnits) & "</Work><ActualWork>" & HoursToDuration(a.ActualUnits) & "</ActualWork><RemainingWork>" & HoursToDuration(a.RemainingUnits) & "</RemainingWork><Cost>" & a.BudgetCost & "</Cost><ActualCost>" & a.ActualCost & "</ActualCost><RemainingCost>" & a.RemainingCost & "</RemainingCost></Assignment>"
            uid = uid + 1
        End If
    Next k
    Print #f, "</Assignments>"
End Sub

Private Function NumericIdMap(ByVal d As Object) As Object
    Dim m As Object: Set m = Dict()
    Dim k As Variant, i As Long: i = 1
    For Each k In d.Keys: m(CStr(k)) = i: i = i + 1: Next k
    Set NumericIdMap = m
End Function

Private Function BIMPayloadForTask(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel, ByVal taskId As String) As String
    Dim k As Variant, b As clsBIMLink, s As String
    For Each k In model.BIMLinks.Keys
        Set b = model.BIMLinks(k)
        If b.TaskID = taskId Then If Len(s) > 0 Then s = s & ";"
        If b.TaskID = taskId Then s = s & b.ModelKey & "~" & b.ObjectID
    Next k
    BIMPayloadForTask = s
End Function


========================================================================
FILE: src/modNarrative.bas
========================================================================

Attribute VB_Name = "modNarrative"
Option Explicit

Public Sub GenerateScheduleNarrative(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel, Optional ByVal changes As Collection)
    On Error GoTo EH
    TraceEnter "modNarrative", "GenerateScheduleNarrative", ScheduleTraceContext()
    Dim ws As Worksheet
    Set ws = EnsureSheet(SH_NARRATIVE)
    ws.Cells.Clear
    ws.Range("A1").Value = "DRAFT SCHEDULE NARRATIVE"
    ws.Range("A1").Font.Bold = True
    ws.Range("A1").Font.Size = 16
    Dim r As Long
    r = 3
    NarrativeSection ws, r, "Executive Summary", ExecutiveSummaryText(model, changes)
    NarrativeSection ws, r, "Schedule Status", ScheduleStatusText(model)
    NarrativeSection ws, r, "Critical Path", CriticalText(model)
    NarrativeSection ws, r, "Near-Critical Paths", NearCriticalText(model)
    NarrativeSection ws, r, "Key Schedule Changes", ChangeText(changes)
    NarrativeSection ws, r, "Progress", ProgressText(model)
    NarrativeSection ws, r, "Resource Trends", ResourceText(model)
    NarrativeSection ws, r, "Schedule Quality", HealthText()
    NarrativeSection ws, r, "Risks / Planner Attention", ConcernText(model)
    NarrativeSection ws, r, "Lookahead", LookaheadText(model, SafeLng(GetSetting("LookaheadWeeks", 6), 6))
    ws.Columns("A:A").ColumnWidth = 120
    ws.Columns("A:A").WrapText = True
    TraceExit "modNarrative", "GenerateScheduleNarrative", "sections=10"
    Exit Sub
EH:
    LogAndShowError "modNarrative", "GenerateScheduleNarrative", "The schedule narrative could not be generated.", ScheduleTraceContext()
End Sub

Private Sub NarrativeSection(ByVal ws As Worksheet, ByRef rowNo As Long, ByVal heading As String, ByVal body As String)
    ws.Cells(rowNo, 1).Value = heading
    ws.Cells(rowNo, 1).Font.Bold = True
    rowNo = rowNo + 1
    ws.Cells(rowNo, 1).Value = body
    rowNo = rowNo + 2
End Sub

Private Function ExecutiveSummaryText(ByVal m As clsScheduleModel, ByVal changes As Collection) As String
    Dim s As String
    s = "Current calculated completion is " & Format$(ProjectFinishDate(m), "dd-mmm-yyyy") & ". " & m.Activities.Count & " activities and " & m.Relationships.Count & " relationships are loaded."
    If Not changes Is Nothing Then s = s & " The comparison contains " & changes.Count & " recorded changes."
    ExecutiveSummaryText = s
End Function

Private Function ScheduleStatusText(ByVal m As clsScheduleModel) As String
    ScheduleStatusText = "Data Date: " & IIf(m.HasDataDate, Format$(m.DataDate, "dd-mmm-yyyy"), "not available") & ". Average activity progress: " & Format$(AverageActivityProgress(m), "0.0") & "%."
End Function

Private Function CriticalText(ByVal m As clsScheduleModel) As String
    CriticalText = CountFloatBand(m, -1E+20, SafeDbl(GetSetting("CriticalFloatThresholdHours", 0), 0)) & " activities are at or below the configured critical-float threshold. Use the Critical Path report for the underlying records and driving relationships."
End Function

Private Function NearCriticalText(ByVal m As clsScheduleModel) As String
    NearCriticalText = CountFloatBand(m, 0.0001, SafeDbl(GetSetting("NearCriticalThresholdHours", 80), 80)) & " activities are within the configured near-critical band."
End Function

Private Function ChangeText(ByVal changes As Collection) As String
    If changes Is Nothing Then
        ChangeText = "No comparison set was supplied."
    Else
        ChangeText = changes.Count & " changes were identified. Materiality is shown in the Change Register and should be reviewed before contractual interpretation."
    End If
End Function

Private Function ProgressText(ByVal m As clsScheduleModel) As String
    ProgressText = "Average recorded progress is " & Format$(AverageActivityProgress(m), "0.0") & "%. Progress QA findings are available in Diagnostics/Schedule Health."
End Function

Private Function ResourceText(ByVal m As clsScheduleModel) As String
    ResourceText = m.Resources.Count & " resources and " & m.Assignments.Count & " assignments are loaded. Histograms, S-curves and overload findings should be read from the resource views."
End Function

Private Function HealthText() As String
    Dim ws As Worksheet, f As Range
    Set ws = EnsureSheet(SH_SCHEDULE_HEALTH)
    Set f = ws.Columns(1).Find("Overall Health Score", LookAt:=xlWhole)
    If f Is Nothing Then
        HealthText = "Run Schedule Health to calculate the transparent QA score."
    Else
        HealthText = "Current schedule-health score is " & Format$(f.Offset(0, 1).Value, "0.0") & "/100. Drill into the Schedule Health sheet for contributing checks."
    End If
End Function

Private Function ConcernText(ByVal m As clsScheduleModel) As String
    ConcernText = "Primary attention areas: " & CountFloatBand(m, -1E+20, -0.0001) & " activities with negative float; " & CountHardConstraints(m) & " activities with hard constraints; " & CountProgressIssues(m) & " activities with questionable status/date combinations."
End Function

Private Function LookaheadText(ByVal m As clsScheduleModel, ByVal weeks As Long) As String
    LookaheadText = CountLookaheadActivities(m, weeks) & " incomplete activities fall within the next " & weeks & " weeks from the Data Date/current date."
End Function

Private Function ProjectFinishDate(ByVal m As clsScheduleModel) As Date
    Dim k As Variant, a As clsActivity, d As Date
    For Each k In m.Activities.Keys
        Set a = m.Activities(k)
        If a.HasPlannedFinish Then d = a.PlannedFinish Else d = a.EarlyFinish
        If d > ProjectFinishDate Then ProjectFinishDate = d
    Next k
End Function

Private Function AverageActivityProgress(ByVal m As clsScheduleModel) As Double
    Dim k As Variant, a As clsActivity, total As Double
    If m.Activities.Count = 0 Then Exit Function
    For Each k In m.Activities.Keys
        Set a = m.Activities(k)
        total = total + a.PercentComplete
    Next k
    AverageActivityProgress = total / m.Activities.Count
End Function

Private Function CountHardConstraints(ByVal m As clsScheduleModel) As Long
    Dim k As Variant, a As clsActivity, t As String
    For Each k In m.Activities.Keys
        Set a = m.Activities(k)
        t = UCase$(a.ConstraintType1)
        If InStr(t, "MANDATORY") > 0 Or InStr(t, "START ON") > 0 Or InStr(t, "FINISH ON") > 0 Then CountHardConstraints = CountHardConstraints + 1
    Next k
End Function

Private Function CountProgressIssues(ByVal m As clsScheduleModel) As Long
    Dim k As Variant, a As clsActivity
    For Each k In m.Activities.Keys
        Set a = m.Activities(k)
        If (a.PercentComplete > 0 And Not a.HasActualStart) Or (a.HasActualFinish And Not a.HasActualStart) Then CountProgressIssues = CountProgressIssues + 1
    Next k
End Function

Private Function CountLookaheadActivities(ByVal m As clsScheduleModel, ByVal weeks As Long) As Long
    Dim anchor As Date, k As Variant, a As clsActivity, d As Date
    If m.HasDataDate Then anchor = m.DataDate Else anchor = Date
    For Each k In m.Activities.Keys
        Set a = m.Activities(k)
        If a.PercentComplete < 100 Then
            If a.HasPlannedStart Then d = a.PlannedStart Else d = a.EarlyStart
            If d >= anchor And d <= DateAdd("d", weeks * 7, anchor) Then CountLookaheadActivities = CountLookaheadActivities + 1
        End If
    Next k
End Function


========================================================================
FILE: src/modNetworkDiagram.bas
========================================================================

Attribute VB_Name = "modNetworkDiagram"
Option Explicit

Public Sub BuildNetworkDiagram()
    On Error GoTo EH
    TraceEnter "modNetworkDiagram", "BuildNetworkDiagram", ScheduleTraceContext()
    If gSchedule Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 7600, , "No schedule is loaded."
    SyncModelFromSheets gSchedule
    If HasCycle(gSchedule) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 7601, , "Network diagram cannot be built while a relationship cycle exists."

    Dim scoped As Object: Set scoped = ScopedActivities(gSchedule)
    Dim maxActivities As Long: maxActivities = SafeLng(GetSetting("NetworkMaxActivities", 200), 200)
    If maxActivities < 10 Then maxActivities = 10
    If scoped.Count > maxActivities Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 7602, , _
        "Network scope contains " & scoped.Count & " activities. Limit is " & maxActivities & ". Set NetworkWBSID or increase NetworkMaxActivities."

    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = EnsureSheet(SH_NETWORK)
    Application.ScreenUpdating = False
    ws.Cells.Clear
    DeleteNetworkShapes ws
    ws.Range("A1").Value = "Logic Network Diagram"
    ws.Range("A2").Value = "Project: " & gSchedule.ProjectName
    ws.Range("A3").Value = "Scope WBS: " & SafeText(GetSetting("NetworkWBSID", "<all>"), "<all>")
    ws.Range("A1").Font.Bold = True: ws.Range("A1").Font.Size = 16

    Dim levels As Object: Set levels = ComputeNetworkLevels(gSchedule, scoped)
    Dim counts As Object: Set counts = Dict()
    Dim shapes As Object: Set shapes = Dict()
    Dim k As Variant, a As clsActivity, level As Long, rowIndex As Long
    Dim leftPos As Double, topPos As Double, shp As Shape, shapeName As String
    Const BOX_W As Double = 165
    Const BOX_H As Double = 68
    Const X_GAP As Double = 80
    Const Y_GAP As Double = 28

    For Each k In scoped.Keys
        Set a = gSchedule.Activities(CStr(k))
        level = SafeLng(levels(CStr(k)), 0)
        If Not counts.Exists(CStr(level)) Then counts(CStr(level)) = 0
        rowIndex = SafeLng(counts(CStr(level)))
        counts(CStr(level)) = rowIndex + 1
        leftPos = 20 + level * (BOX_W + X_GAP)
        topPos = 70 + rowIndex * (BOX_H + Y_GAP)
        Set shp = ws.Shapes.AddShape(msoShapeRoundedRectangle, leftPos, topPos, BOX_W, BOX_H)
        shapeName = SafeShapeName("NET_" & a.TaskID)
        shp.Name = shapeName
        shp.TextFrame2.TextRange.Text = a.ActivityID & vbCrLf & Left$(a.Name, 45) & vbCrLf & _
                                      "ES " & FormatSafeDate(a.EarlyStart) & " | EF " & FormatSafeDate(a.EarlyFinish) & vbCrLf & _
                                      "TF " & Format$(a.TotalFloatHours, "0.0") & "h"
        shp.TextFrame2.TextRange.Font.Size = 8
        shp.Line.ForeColor.RGB = RGB(90, 90, 90)
        If a.TotalFloatHours <= 0 Then
            shp.Fill.ForeColor.RGB = GanttStyleColor("Critical", RGB(244, 176, 132))
        ElseIf a.HasActualFinish Then
            shp.Fill.ForeColor.RGB = GanttStyleColor("Actual", RGB(112, 173, 71))
        Else
            shp.Fill.ForeColor.RGB = GanttStyleColor("Current", RGB(91, 155, 213))
        End If
        shapes(CStr(k)) = shapeName
    Next k

    Dim r As clsRelationship, conn As Shape
    For Each k In gSchedule.Relationships.Keys
        Set r = gSchedule.Relationships(k)
        If Not r.IsExternal Then
            If shapes.Exists(r.PredTaskID) And shapes.Exists(r.SuccTaskID) Then
                Set conn = ws.Shapes.AddConnector(msoConnectorElbow, 0, 0, 10, 10)
                With conn.ConnectorFormat
                    .BeginConnect ws.Shapes(CStr(shapes(r.PredTaskID))), 4
                    .EndConnect ws.Shapes(CStr(shapes(r.SuccTaskID))), 2
                End With
                conn.Line.EndArrowheadStyle = msoArrowheadTriangle
                conn.Line.ForeColor.RGB = RGB(100, 100, 100)
                conn.Name = SafeShapeName("REL_" & r.RelID)
            End If
        End If
    Next k

    ws.Activate
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    TraceExit "modNetworkDiagram", "BuildNetworkDiagram", "activities=" & CStr(scoped.Count) & "; relationships=" & CStr(gSchedule.Relationships.Count)
    Exit Sub
EH:
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    LogAndShowError "modNetworkDiagram", "BuildNetworkDiagram", "The network diagram could not be built.", _
                    "NetworkWBSID=" & SafeText(GetSetting("NetworkWBSID", "")) & "; " & ScheduleTraceContext()
End Sub

Private Function ScopedActivities(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel) As Object
    Dim d As Object, wbsId As String
    Set d = Dict()
    wbsId = Trim$(SafeText(GetSetting("NetworkWBSID", "")))
    Dim descendants As Object
    If Len(wbsId) > 0 Then Set descendants = WBSDescendantSet(model, wbsId)
    Dim k As Variant, a As clsActivity
    For Each k In model.Activities.Keys
        Set a = model.Activities(k)
        If Len(wbsId) = 0 Then
            d(CStr(k)) = True
        ElseIf descendants.Exists(a.WBSID) Then
            d(CStr(k)) = True
        End If
    Next k
    Set ScopedActivities = d
End Function

Private Function WBSDescendantSet(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel, ByVal rootId As String) As Object
    Dim d As Object: Set d = Dict(): d(rootId) = True
    Dim changed As Boolean, k As Variant, w As clsWBS
    Do
        changed = False
        For Each k In model.WBS.Keys
            Set w = model.WBS(k)
            If d.Exists(w.ParentWBSID) And Not d.Exists(w.WBSID) Then d(w.WBSID) = True: changed = True
        Next k
    Loop While changed
    Set WBSDescendantSet = d
End Function

Private Function ComputeNetworkLevels(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel, ByVal scoped As Object) As Object
    Dim levels As Object, order As Collection
    Set levels = Dict()
    Set order = TopologicalOrder(model)
    Dim i As Long, id As String, k As Variant, r As clsRelationship, best As Long
    For i = 1 To order.Count
        id = CStr(order(i))
        If scoped.Exists(id) Then
            best = 0
            For Each k In model.Relationships.Keys
                Set r = model.Relationships(k)
                If Not r.IsExternal And r.SuccTaskID = id And scoped.Exists(r.PredTaskID) Then
                    If levels.Exists(r.PredTaskID) Then If SafeLng(levels(r.PredTaskID)) + 1 > best Then best = SafeLng(levels(r.PredTaskID)) + 1
                End If
            Next k
            levels(id) = best
        End If
    Next i
    Set ComputeNetworkLevels = levels
End Function

Private Function SafeShapeName(ByVal rawName As String) As String
    Dim s As String: s = rawName
    s = Replace(s, " ", "_"): s = Replace(s, "/", "_"): s = Replace(s, "\\", "_")
    s = Replace(s, ":", "_"): s = Replace(s, "[", "_"): s = Replace(s, "]", "_")
    If Len(s) > 200 Then s = Left$(s, 200)
    SafeShapeName = s
End Function

Private Function FormatSafeDate(ByVal d As Date) As String
    If d > 0 Then FormatSafeDate = Format$(d, "dd-mmm-yy") Else FormatSafeDate = "-"
End Function

Private Sub DeleteNetworkShapes(ByVal ws As Worksheet)
    Dim i As Long
    For i = ws.Shapes.Count To 1 Step -1: ws.Shapes(i).Delete: Next i
End Sub

Attribute VB_Name = "modP6ExtendedViews"
Option Explicit

Public Sub RefreshP6ExtendedViews(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel)
    On Error GoTo EH
    TraceEnter "modP6ExtendedViews", "RefreshP6ExtendedViews", "records=" & CStr(model.ExtendedRecords.Count)
    RefreshActivityCodesView model
    RefreshRolesView model
    RefreshNotebooksView model
    RefreshEPSView model
    RefreshOBSView model
    RefreshRisksIssuesView model
    TraceExit "modP6ExtendedViews", "RefreshP6ExtendedViews"
    Exit Sub
EH:
    LogAndShowError "modP6ExtendedViews", "RefreshP6ExtendedViews", "P6 extended views could not be refreshed.", _
                    "extendedRecords=" & CStr(model.ExtendedRecords.Count)
End Sub

Private Sub RefreshActivityCodesView(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel)
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = EnsureSheet(SH_ACTIVITY_CODES): ws.Cells.Clear
    WriteRow ws, 1, 1, Array("TaskID", "Activity ID", "Activity Name", "Code Type ID", "Code Type", "Code ID", "Code Value")
    ws.Rows(1).Font.Bold = True
    Dim types As Object, codes As Object
    Set types = Dict()
    Set codes = Dict()
    Dim k As Variant, x As clsExtendedRecord, id As String, typ As String, nm As String
    For Each k In model.ExtendedRecords.Keys
        Set x = model.ExtendedRecords(k)
        Select Case x.TableName
            Case "ACTVTYPE"
                id = FirstNonBlank(x, Array("actv_code_type_id", "actv_type_id"))
                nm = FirstNonBlank(x, Array("actv_code_type", "actv_code_type_name", "actv_type_name"))
                If Len(id) > 0 Then types(id) = nm
            Case "ACTVCODE"
                id = FirstNonBlank(x, Array("actv_code_id", "actv_id"))
                typ = FirstNonBlank(x, Array("actv_code_type_id", "actv_type_id"))
                nm = FirstNonBlank(x, Array("actv_code_name", "actv_code_short_name", "short_name"))
                If Len(id) > 0 Then codes(id) = typ & "|" & nm
        End Select
    Next k
    Dim r As Long, taskId As String, codeId As String, parts As Variant, a As clsActivity
    r = 2
    For Each k In model.ExtendedRecords.Keys
        Set x = model.ExtendedRecords(k)
        If x.TableName = "TASKACTV" Then
            taskId = FirstNonBlank(x, Array("task_id", "fk_id"))
            codeId = FirstNonBlank(x, Array("actv_code_id", "actv_id"))
            typ = FirstNonBlank(x, Array("actv_code_type_id", "actv_type_id"))
            nm = ""
            If codes.Exists(codeId) Then
                parts = Split(CStr(codes(codeId)), "|")
                If Len(typ) = 0 Then typ = CStr(parts(0))
                If UBound(parts) >= 1 Then nm = CStr(parts(1))
            End If
            WriteRow ws, r, 1, Array(taskId, ActivityCode(model, taskId), ActivityName(model, taskId), typ, DictText(types, typ), codeId, nm)
            r = r + 1
        End If
    Next k
    ws.Columns.AutoFit
End Sub

Private Sub RefreshRolesView(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel)
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = EnsureSheet(SH_ROLES): ws.Cells.Clear
    WriteRow ws, 1, 1, Array("Role ID", "Role Code", "Role Name", "Assigned Activities", "Assigned Units", "Assigned Cost")
    ws.Rows(1).Font.Bold = True
    Dim roleNames As Object, roleCodes As Object
    Set roleNames = Dict()
    Set roleCodes = Dict()
    Dim k As Variant, x As clsExtendedRecord, roleId As String
    For Each k In model.ExtendedRecords.Keys
        Set x = model.ExtendedRecords(k)
        If x.TableName = "ROLE" Or x.TableName = "ROLES" Then
            roleId = FirstNonBlank(x, Array("role_id"))
            If Len(roleId) > 0 Then
                roleCodes(roleId) = FirstNonBlank(x, Array("role_short_name", "role_code"))
                roleNames(roleId) = FirstNonBlank(x, Array("role_name"))
            End If
        End If
    Next k
    Dim countD As Object, unitsD As Object, costD As Object, asn As clsAssignment
    Set countD = Dict()
    Set unitsD = Dict()
    Set costD = Dict()
    For Each k In model.Assignments.Keys
        Set asn = model.Assignments(k): roleId = asn.RoleID
        If Len(roleId) > 0 Then
            countD(roleId) = DictDbl(countD, roleId) + 1
            unitsD(roleId) = DictDbl(unitsD, roleId) + asn.BudgetUnits
            costD(roleId) = DictDbl(costD, roleId) + asn.BudgetCost
            If Not roleNames.Exists(roleId) Then roleNames(roleId) = ""
        End If
    Next k
    Dim r As Long: r = 2
    For Each k In roleNames.Keys
        roleId = CStr(k)
        WriteRow ws, r, 1, Array(roleId, DictText(roleCodes, roleId), roleNames(roleId), _
            DictDbl(countD, roleId), DictDbl(unitsD, roleId), DictDbl(costD, roleId))
        r = r + 1
    Next k
    ws.Columns.AutoFit
End Sub

Private Sub RefreshNotebooksView(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel)
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = EnsureSheet(SH_NOTEBOOKS): ws.Cells.Clear
    WriteRow ws, 1, 1, Array("TaskID", "Activity ID", "Activity Name", "Topic Type ID", "Topic", "Text")
    ws.Rows(1).Font.Bold = True
    Dim topics As Object, k As Variant, x As clsExtendedRecord, id As String
    Set topics = Dict()
    For Each k In model.ExtendedRecords.Keys
        Set x = model.ExtendedRecords(k)
        If x.TableName = "MEMOTYPE" Then
            id = FirstNonBlank(x, Array("memo_type_id"))
            If Len(id) > 0 Then topics(id) = FirstNonBlank(x, Array("memo_type", "memo_type_name", "memo_type_label"))
        End If
    Next k
    Dim r As Long, taskId As String, topicId As String, txt As String
    r = 2
    For Each k In model.ExtendedRecords.Keys
        Set x = model.ExtendedRecords(k)
        If x.TableName = "TASKMEMO" Or x.TableName = "TASKNOTE" Then
            taskId = FirstNonBlank(x, Array("task_id", "fk_id"))
            topicId = FirstNonBlank(x, Array("memo_type_id"))
            txt = FirstNonBlank(x, Array("task_memo", "memo_text", "note_text", "memo"))
            WriteRow ws, r, 1, Array(taskId, ActivityCode(model, taskId), ActivityName(model, taskId), topicId, DictText(topics, topicId), txt)
            r = r + 1
        End If
    Next k
    ws.Columns("A:F").AutoFit: ws.Columns("F").ColumnWidth = 70: ws.Columns("F").WrapText = True
End Sub


Private Sub RefreshEPSView(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel)
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = EnsureSheet(SH_EPS): ws.Cells.Clear
    WriteRow ws, 1, 1, Array("EPS ID", "Parent EPS ID", "Code", "Name", "Sequence")
    ws.Rows(1).Font.Bold = True
    Dim r As Long, k As Variant, x As clsExtendedRecord
    r = 2
    For Each k In model.ExtendedRecords.Keys
        Set x = model.ExtendedRecords(k)
        If x.TableName = "EPS" Then
            WriteRow ws, r, 1, Array(FirstNonBlank(x, Array("eps_id")), FirstNonBlank(x, Array("parent_eps_id")), _
                FirstNonBlank(x, Array("eps_short_name", "eps_code")), FirstNonBlank(x, Array("eps_name")), _
                FirstNonBlank(x, Array("seq_num")))
            r = r + 1
        End If
    Next k
    ws.Columns.AutoFit
End Sub

Private Sub RefreshOBSView(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel)
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = EnsureSheet(SH_OBS): ws.Cells.Clear
    WriteRow ws, 1, 1, Array("OBS ID", "Parent OBS ID", "Name", "Description", "Sequence")
    ws.Rows(1).Font.Bold = True
    Dim r As Long, k As Variant, x As clsExtendedRecord
    r = 2
    For Each k In model.ExtendedRecords.Keys
        Set x = model.ExtendedRecords(k)
        If x.TableName = "OBS" Then
            WriteRow ws, r, 1, Array(FirstNonBlank(x, Array("obs_id")), FirstNonBlank(x, Array("parent_obs_id")), _
                FirstNonBlank(x, Array("obs_name")), FirstNonBlank(x, Array("obs_descr", "obs_description")), _
                FirstNonBlank(x, Array("seq_num")))
            r = r + 1
        End If
    Next k
    ws.Columns.AutoFit
End Sub

Private Sub RefreshRisksIssuesView(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel)
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = EnsureSheet(SH_RISKS_ISSUES): ws.Cells.Clear
    WriteRow ws, 1, 1, Array("Source Table", "ID", "Project ID", "Task ID", "Name/Title", "Status", "Owner", "Probability", "Impact", "Description")
    ws.Rows(1).Font.Bold = True
    Dim r As Long, k As Variant, x As clsExtendedRecord
    r = 2
    For Each k In model.ExtendedRecords.Keys
        Set x = model.ExtendedRecords(k)
        If InStr(1, x.TableName, "RISK", vbTextCompare) > 0 Or InStr(1, x.TableName, "ISSUE", vbTextCompare) > 0 Then
            WriteRow ws, r, 1, Array(x.TableName, FirstNonBlank(x, Array("risk_id", "issue_id", "id")), _
                FirstNonBlank(x, Array("proj_id")), FirstNonBlank(x, Array("task_id")), _
                FirstNonBlank(x, Array("risk_name", "issue_name", "risk_title", "issue_title", "name")), _
                FirstNonBlank(x, Array("status_code", "risk_status", "issue_status")), _
                FirstNonBlank(x, Array("owner_id", "responsible_id", "obs_id")), _
                FirstNonBlank(x, Array("probability", "probability_pct", "prob_pct")), _
                FirstNonBlank(x, Array("impact", "impact_value", "impact_score")), _
                FirstNonBlank(x, Array("risk_descr", "issue_descr", "description")))
            r = r + 1
        End If
    Next k
    ws.Columns("A:J").AutoFit: ws.Columns("J").ColumnWidth = 60: ws.Columns("J").WrapText = True
End Sub

Private Function FirstNonBlank(ByVal x As clsExtendedRecord, ByVal fields As Variant) As String
    Dim f As Variant, v As String
    For Each f In fields
        v = x.GetField(CStr(f))
        If Len(v) > 0 Then FirstNonBlank = v: Exit Function
    Next f
End Function


Private Function DictText(ByVal d As Object, ByVal key As String) As String
    If d.Exists(key) Then DictText = SafeText(d(key))
End Function

Private Function DictDbl(ByVal d As Object, ByVal key As String) As Double
    If d.Exists(key) Then DictDbl = SafeDbl(d(key))
End Function
Private Function ActivityCode(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel, ByVal taskId As String) As String
    If model.Activities.Exists(taskId) Then ActivityCode = model.Activities(taskId).ActivityID
End Function
Private Function ActivityName(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel, ByVal taskId As String) As String
    If model.Activities.Exists(taskId) Then ActivityName = model.Activities(taskId).Name
End Function


========================================================================
FILE: src/modProgress.bas
========================================================================

Attribute VB_Name = "modProgress"
Option Explicit

Public Sub ImportProgressCSV()
    TraceEnter "modProgress", "ImportProgressCSV", ScheduleTraceContext()
    On Error GoTo fail
    If gSchedule Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4400, , "No schedule loaded."
    SyncModelFromSheets gSchedule
    CreateHistorySnapshot "Before progress import"

    Dim p As String: p = ChooseOpen("Import progress CSV", "*.csv")
    If Len(p) = 0 Then Exit Sub

    Dim f As Integer: f = FreeFile
    Dim line As String, headers As Variant, vals As Variant, rowNo As Long
    Open p For Input As #f
    If EOF(f) Then Close #f: Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4401, , "Progress CSV is empty."
    Line Input #f, line
    headers = ParseCSVLine(line)
    Dim map As Object: Set map = HeaderMap(headers)
    If Not map.Exists("ACTIVITYID") And Not map.Exists("TASKID") Then Close #f: Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4402, , "CSV must include ActivityID or TaskID."

    Dim byActivity As Object: Set byActivity = Dict()
    Dim k As Variant, a As clsActivity
    For Each k In gSchedule.Activities.Keys
        Set a = gSchedule.Activities(k)
        If Len(a.ActivityID) > 0 Then byActivity(UCase$(a.ActivityID)) = a.TaskID
    Next k

    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = EnsureSheet(SH_PROGRESS)
    ws.Cells.Clear
    WriteRow ws, 1, 1, Array("CSV Row", "Identifier", "Status", "Message", "Old %", "New %")
    ws.Rows(1).Font.Bold = True
    Dim logRow As Long: logRow = 2
    rowNo = 1

    Do While Not EOF(f)
        Line Input #f, line: rowNo = rowNo + 1
        If Len(Trim$(line)) > 0 Then
            vals = ParseCSVLine(line)
            Dim taskId As String, identifier As String
            If map.Exists("TASKID") Then taskId = FieldValue(vals, map("TASKID"))
            If Len(taskId) = 0 And map.Exists("ACTIVITYID") Then
                identifier = FieldValue(vals, map("ACTIVITYID"))
                If byActivity.Exists(UCase$(identifier)) Then taskId = byActivity(UCase$(identifier))
            End If
            If Len(identifier) = 0 Then identifier = taskId

            If Len(taskId) = 0 Or Not gSchedule.Activities.Exists(taskId) Then
                LogProgress ws, logRow, rowNo, identifier, "Rejected", "Activity/task not found", "", ""
            Else
                Set a = gSchedule.Activities(taskId)
                Dim oldPct As Double: oldPct = a.PercentComplete
                ApplyProgressFields a, vals, map
                LogProgress ws, logRow, rowNo, identifier, "Applied", "OK", oldPct, a.PercentComplete
            End If
        End If
    Loop
    Close #f

    ValidateProgress gSchedule, ws, logRow
    RefreshAllViews gSchedule
    WriteAudit "IMPORT", "Progress", gSchedule.ProjectID, p
    ws.Columns.AutoFit
    TraceExit "modProgress", "ImportProgressCSV", "rowsProcessed=" & CStr(rowNo) & "; " & TraceKV("path", p)
    Exit Sub
fail:
    On Error Resume Next
    Close #f
    On Error GoTo 0
    LogAndShowError "modProgress", "ImportProgressCSV", "Progress import failed.", _
                    TraceKV("path", p) & "; rowNo=" & CStr(rowNo)
End Sub

Private Sub ApplyProgressFields(ByVal a As clsActivity, ByVal vals As Variant, ByVal map As Object)
    Dim d As Date, s As String
    If map.Exists("PERCENTCOMPLETE") Then a.PercentComplete = SafeDbl(FieldValue(vals, map("PERCENTCOMPLETE")), a.PercentComplete)
    If map.Exists("REMAININGDURATIONHOURS") Then a.RemainingDurationHours = SafeDbl(FieldValue(vals, map("REMAININGDURATIONHOURS")), a.RemainingDurationHours)
    If map.Exists("ACTUALSTART") Then
        s = FieldValue(vals, map("ACTUALSTART"))
        If Len(s) > 0 Then If TryDate(s, d) Then a.ActualStart = d: a.HasActualStart = True
    End If
    If map.Exists("ACTUALFINISH") Then
        s = FieldValue(vals, map("ACTUALFINISH"))
        If Len(s) > 0 Then If TryDate(s, d) Then a.ActualFinish = d: a.HasActualFinish = True
    End If
    If a.HasActualFinish Then
        a.Status = STATUS_COMPLETE: a.PercentComplete = 100: a.RemainingDurationHours = 0
    ElseIf a.HasActualStart Or a.PercentComplete > 0 Then
        a.Status = STATUS_IN_PROGRESS
    Else
        a.Status = STATUS_NOT_STARTED
    End If
End Sub

Private Sub ValidateProgress(ByVal m As clsScheduleModel, ByVal ws As Worksheet, ByRef logRow As Long)
    Dim k As Variant, a As clsActivity
    For Each k In m.Activities.Keys
        Set a = m.Activities(k)
        If a.PercentComplete < 0 Or a.PercentComplete > 100 Then LogProgress ws, logRow, 0, a.ActivityID, "Warning", "% Complete outside 0-100", "", a.PercentComplete
        If a.HasActualFinish And Not a.HasActualStart Then LogProgress ws, logRow, 0, a.ActivityID, "Warning", "Actual Finish without Actual Start", "", ""
        If a.HasActualStart And a.HasActualFinish Then If a.ActualFinish < a.ActualStart Then LogProgress ws, logRow, 0, a.ActivityID, "Warning", "Actual Finish before Actual Start", "", ""
    Next k
End Sub

Private Sub LogProgress(ByVal ws As Worksheet, ByRef r As Long, ByVal csvRow As Long, ByVal identifier As String, ByVal status As String, ByVal msg As String, ByVal oldPct As Variant, ByVal newPct As Variant)
    WriteRow ws, r, 1, Array(csvRow, identifier, status, msg, oldPct, newPct)
    r = r + 1
End Sub

Private Function HeaderMap(ByVal headers As Variant) As Object
    Dim d As Object: Set d = Dict()
    Dim i As Long
    For i = LBound(headers) To UBound(headers): d(UCase$(Replace(Trim$(CStr(headers(i))), " ", ""))) = i: Next i
    Set HeaderMap = d
End Function

Private Function FieldValue(ByVal vals As Variant, ByVal idx As Long) As String
    If idx >= LBound(vals) And idx <= UBound(vals) Then FieldValue = Trim$(CStr(vals(idx)))
End Function

Private Function ParseCSVLine(ByVal line As String) As Variant
    Dim out As Collection: Set out = New Collection
    Dim i As Long, ch As String, cur As String, inQuotes As Boolean
    For i = 1 To Len(line)
        ch = Mid$(line, i, 1)
        If ch = """" Then
            If inQuotes And i < Len(line) And Mid$(line, i + 1, 1) = """" Then cur = cur & """": i = i + 1 Else inQuotes = Not inQuotes
        ElseIf ch = "," And Not inQuotes Then
            out.Add cur: cur = ""
        Else
            cur = cur & ch
        End If
    Next i
    out.Add cur
    Dim arr() As String: ReDim arr(0 To out.Count - 1)
    For i = 1 To out.Count: arr(i - 1) = CStr(out(i)): Next i
    ParseCSVLine = arr
End Function


========================================================================
FILE: src/modPublicAPI.bas
========================================================================

Attribute VB_Name = "modPublicAPI"
Option Explicit

Public Sub CommandImportSchedule()
    On Error GoTo EH
    TraceEnter "modPublicAPI", "CommandImportSchedule"
    Dim p As String
    p = ChooseScheduleFile()
    TracePoint "modPublicAPI", "CommandImportSchedule", "File selected", TraceKV("path", p)
    If Len(p) = 0 Then Exit Sub
    Select Case LCase$(Mid$(p, InStrRev(p, ".") + 1))
        Case "xer": ImportScheduleFromXERPath p
        Case "xml": ImportScheduleFromMSPXMLPath p
        Case Else: Err.Raise vbObjectError + 9100, , "Unsupported schedule file type."
    End Select
    TraceExit "modPublicAPI", "CommandImportSchedule", ScheduleTraceContext()
    Exit Sub
EH:
    LogAndShowError "modPublicAPI", "CommandImportSchedule", "The schedule could not be imported.", TraceKV("path", p)
End Sub

Public Sub CommandSaveSchedule()
    On Error GoTo EH
    TraceEnter "modPublicAPI", "CommandSaveSchedule", ScheduleTraceContext()
    If gSchedule Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 9101, , "No schedule is loaded."
    Dim choice As VbMsgBoxResult
    choice = MsgBox("Save as Primavera P6 XER?" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
                    "Yes = XER" & vbCrLf & "No = Microsoft Project XML" & vbCrLf & "Cancel = do nothing", _
                    vbYesNoCancel + vbQuestion, "Save Schedule")
    TracePoint "modPublicAPI", "CommandSaveSchedule", "Format selected", TraceKV("choice", choice)
    If choice = vbYes Then ExportCurrentAsXER
    If choice = vbNo Then ExportCurrentAsMSPXML
    TraceExit "modPublicAPI", "CommandSaveSchedule", ScheduleTraceContext()
    Exit Sub
EH:
    LogAndShowError "modPublicAPI", "CommandSaveSchedule", "The schedule could not be saved.", ScheduleTraceContext()
End Sub

End Sub

Public Sub CommandLoadBaseline()
    On Error GoTo EH
    TraceEnter "modPublicAPI", "CommandLoadBaseline", ScheduleTraceContext()
    Dim p As String
    p = ChooseScheduleFile()
    If Len(p) = 0 Then Exit Sub
    If LCase$(Right$(p, 4)) = ".xer" Then
        LoadBaselineFromXERPath p
    ElseIf LCase$(Right$(p, 4)) = ".xml" Then
        LoadBaselineFromMSPXMLPath p
    Else
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 9102, , "Unsupported baseline file type."
    End If
    TraceExit "modPublicAPI", "CommandLoadBaseline", TraceKV("path", p)
    Exit Sub
EH:
    LogAndShowError "modPublicAPI", "CommandLoadBaseline", "The baseline could not be loaded.", TraceKV("path", p)
End Sub

Private Function ChooseScheduleFile() As String
    On Error GoTo EH
    TraceEnter "modPublicAPI", "ChooseScheduleFile"
    Dim fd As Object
    Set fd = Application.FileDialog(3)
    With fd
        .Title = "Open Schedule"
        .AllowMultiSelect = False
        .Filters.Clear
        .Filters.Add "Supported schedules", "*.xer;*.xml"
        .Filters.Add "Primavera P6 XER", "*.xer"
        .Filters.Add "Microsoft Project XML", "*.xml"
        If .Show = -1 Then ChooseScheduleFile = .SelectedItems(1)
    End With
    TraceExit "modPublicAPI", "ChooseScheduleFile", TraceKV("result", ChooseScheduleFile)
    Exit Function
EH:
    TraceError "modPublicAPI", "ChooseScheduleFile", Err.Number, Err.Description, Erl
    Err.Raise Err.Number, "modPublicAPI.ChooseScheduleFile", Err.Description
End Function

========================================================================
FILE: src/modRawData.bas
========================================================================

Attribute VB_Name = "modRawData"
Option Explicit

Public Sub StoreRawRecord(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel, ByVal tableName As String, ByVal rec As Object)
    Dim x As New clsExtendedRecord
    x.TableName = tableName
    x.RecordKey = RawRecordKey(tableName, rec, model.RawRecords.Count + 1)
    Dim k As Variant
    For Each k In rec.Keys
        x.SetField CStr(k), SafeText(rec(k))
    Next k
    Set model.RawRecords(tableName & "|" & x.RecordKey & "|" & CStr(model.RawRecords.Count + 1)) = x
End Sub

Private Function RawRecordKey(ByVal tableName As String, ByVal rec As Object, ByVal fallbackNo As Long) As String
    Dim candidates As Variant, c As Variant
    candidates = Array("task_id", "task_pred_id", "proj_id", "wbs_id", "rsrc_id", "taskrsrc_id", "clndr_id", "udf_type_id", "risk_id", "obs_id", "memo_id")
    For Each c In candidates
        If rec.Exists(CStr(c)) Then
            If Len(SafeText(rec(CStr(c)))) > 0 Then
                RawRecordKey = SafeText(rec(CStr(c)))
                Exit Function
            End If
        End If
    Next c
    RawRecordKey = tableName & "_" & CStr(fallbackNo)
End Function

Public Sub RefreshRawDataView(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel)
    On Error GoTo EH
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = EnsureSheet(SH_RAW_DATA)
    ws.Cells.Clear
    WriteRow ws, 1, 1, Array("Table", "Record Key", "Field", "Original Value")
    ws.Rows(1).Font.Bold = True
    Dim rowNo As Long: rowNo = 2
    Dim k As Variant, x As clsExtendedRecord, f As Variant
    For Each k In model.RawRecords.Keys
        Set x = model.RawRecords(k)
        For Each f In x.FieldOrder
            WriteRow ws, rowNo, 1, Array(x.TableName, x.RecordKey, CStr(f), x.GetField(CStr(f)))
            rowNo = rowNo + 1
        Next f
    Next k
    ws.Columns.AutoFit
    Exit Sub
EH:
    LogAndShowError "modRawData", "RefreshRawDataView", "Raw XER data could not be displayed."
End Sub

Attribute VB_Name = "modReports"
Option Explicit

Public Sub GenerateReportPack()
    On Error GoTo EH
    TraceEnter "modReports","GenerateReportPack",ScheduleTraceContext()
    If gSchedule Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError+12400,,"No schedule loaded."
    RunScheduleDiagnostics gSchedule
    BuildScheduleHealth gSchedule
    BuildCriticalPathReport gSchedule
    BuildResourceOverloadAnalysis gSchedule
    BuildExecutiveDashboard gSchedule
    BuildLookahead gSchedule,SafeLng(GetSetting("LookaheadWeeks",6),6)
    GenerateScheduleNarrative gSchedule
    BuildWeeklyResourceCharts
    BuildEVMAndProductivity
    WriteAudit "REPORT","Report Pack",gSchedule.ProjectID,"Core schedule report pack refreshed"
    TraceExit "modReports","GenerateReportPack"
    Exit Sub
EH:
    LogAndShowError "modReports","GenerateReportPack","The report pack could not be generated.",ScheduleTraceContext()
End Sub

Public Sub ExportAnalysisWorkbook(ByVal filePath As String)
    On Error GoTo EH
    TraceEnter "modReports","ExportAnalysisWorkbook",TraceKV("filePath",filePath)
    Dim wb As Workbook:Set wb=Workbooks.Add(xlWBATWorksheet)
    Dim names As Variant:names=Array(SH_EXEC_DASHBOARD,SH_ACTIVITIES,SH_CHANGE_REGISTER,SH_RELATIONSHIPS,SH_CRITICAL_PATH,SH_SCHEDULE_HEALTH,SH_CALENDARS,SH_RESOURCES,SH_MILESTONE_TREND,SH_PROGRESS,SH_LOOKAHEAD,SH_NARRATIVE)
    Dim n As Variant,srcWs As Worksheet,newWs As Worksheet
    For Each n In names
        On Error Resume Next:Set srcWs=ThisWorkbook.Worksheets(CStr(n)):On Error GoTo EH
        If Not srcWs Is Nothing Then
            Set newWs=wb.Worksheets.Add(After:=wb.Worksheets(wb.Worksheets.Count)):newWs.Name=Left$(CStr(n),31)
            srcWs.UsedRange.Copy Destination:=newWs.Range("A1")
            newWs.Cells.EntireColumn.AutoFit
        End If
        Set srcWs=Nothing
    Next n
    Application.DisplayAlerts=False:If wb.Worksheets.Count>1 And wb.Worksheets(1).UsedRange.Cells.Count=1 And wb.Worksheets(1).Range("A1").Value="" Then wb.Worksheets(1).Delete
    Application.DisplayAlerts=True
    wb.SaveAs filePath,xlOpenXMLWorkbook:wb.Close False
    TraceExit "modReports","ExportAnalysisWorkbook",TraceKV("filePath",filePath)
    Exit Sub
EH:
    On Error Resume Next:Application.DisplayAlerts=True:If Not wb Is Nothing Then wb.Close False:On Error GoTo 0
    LogAndShowError "modReports","ExportAnalysisWorkbook","The analysis workbook could not be exported.",TraceKV("filePath",filePath)
End Sub

Public Sub ExportReportPackPDF(ByVal filePath As String)
    On Error GoTo EH
    TraceEnter "modReports", "ExportReportPackPDF", TraceKV("filePath", filePath)
    Dim names As Variant
    names = Array(SH_EXEC_DASHBOARD, SH_WHAT_CHANGED, SH_CRITICAL_PATH, SH_SCHEDULE_HEALTH, SH_RESOURCE_PROFILE, SH_EVM, SH_LOOKAHEAD, SH_NARRATIVE)
    Dim temp As Workbook: Set temp = Workbooks.Add(xlWBATWorksheet)
    Dim n As Variant, srcWs As Worksheet, copied As Boolean
    For Each n In names
        Set srcWs = Nothing
        On Error Resume Next
        Set srcWs = ThisWorkbook.Worksheets(CStr(n))
        On Error GoTo EH
        If Not srcWs Is Nothing Then
            srcWs.Copy After:=temp.Worksheets(temp.Worksheets.Count)
            copied = True
        End If
    Next n
    If Not copied Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 12401, , "No report sheets are available for PDF export."
    Application.DisplayAlerts = False
    If temp.Worksheets(1).UsedRange.Cells.Count = 1 And temp.Worksheets(1).Range("A1").Value = "" Then temp.Worksheets(1).Delete
    Application.DisplayAlerts = True
    temp.ExportAsFixedFormat Type:=xlTypePDF, Filename:=filePath, Quality:=xlQualityStandard, IncludeDocProperties:=True, IgnorePrintAreas:=False, OpenAfterPublish:=False
    temp.Close False
    TraceExit "modReports", "ExportReportPackPDF", TraceKV("filePath", filePath)
    Exit Sub
EH:
    On Error Resume Next
    Application.DisplayAlerts = True
    If Not temp Is Nothing Then temp.Close False
    On Error GoTo 0
    LogAndShowError "modReports", "ExportReportPackPDF", "The report PDF could not be exported.", TraceKV("filePath", filePath)
End Sub


========================================================================

Attribute VB_Name = "modRepository"
Option Explicit

Public gRepository As Object

Public Sub EnsureRepository()
    If gRepository Is Nothing Then Set gRepository = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
End Sub

Public Function StoreCurrentRevision(ByVal revisionName As String, Optional ByVal description As String = "") As clsRevision
    On Error GoTo EH
    TraceEnter "modRepository", "StoreCurrentRevision", TraceKV("revisionName", revisionName) & "; " & ScheduleTraceContext()
    If gSchedule Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 12000, , "No schedule is loaded."
    If Len(Trim$(revisionName)) = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 12001, , "Revision name is required."
    EnsureRepository

    Dim rev As New clsRevision
    rev.RevisionID = Format$(Now, "yyyymmddhhnnss") & "_" & CStr(gRepository.Count + 1)
    rev.RevisionName = revisionName
    rev.Description = description
    rev.ImportedOn = Now
    rev.SourcePath = gSchedule.SourcePath
    rev.SourceFormat = gSchedule.SourceFormat
    Set rev.Model = CloneScheduleModel(gSchedule)
    Set gRepository(rev.RevisionID) = rev
    RefreshRepositorySheet
    WriteAudit "STORE", "Revision", rev.RevisionID, revisionName
    TraceExit "modRepository", "StoreCurrentRevision", TraceKV("revisionId", rev.RevisionID)
    Set StoreCurrentRevision = rev
    Exit Function
EH:
    LogAndShowError "modRepository", "StoreCurrentRevision", "The schedule revision could not be stored.", TraceKV("revisionName", revisionName)
End Function

Public Function GetRevisionByName(ByVal revisionName As String) As clsRevision
    EnsureRepository
    Dim k As Variant, r As clsRevision
    For Each k In gRepository.Keys
        Set r = gRepository(k)
        If StrComp(r.RevisionName, revisionName, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then
            Set GetRevisionByName = r
            Exit Function
        End If
    Next k
End Function

Public Function CloneScheduleModel(ByVal source As clsScheduleModel) As clsScheduleModel
    Dim target As New clsScheduleModel
    target.ProjectID = source.ProjectID
    target.ProjectName = source.ProjectName
    target.SourceFormat = source.SourceFormat
    target.SourcePath = source.SourcePath
    target.DataDate = source.DataDate
    target.HasDataDate = source.HasDataDate

    Dim k As Variant
    Dim a As clsActivity, r As clsRelationship, w As clsWBS, rs As clsResource, asn As clsAssignment
    Dim cal As clsCalendar, b As clsBIMLink, x As clsExtendedRecord

    For Each k In source.Activities.Keys
        Set a = source.Activities(k)
        Set target.Activities(CStr(k)) = a.Clone
    Next k
    For Each k In source.Relationships.Keys
        Set r = source.Relationships(k)
        Dim nr As New clsRelationship
        nr.RelID = r.RelID: nr.PredTaskID = r.PredTaskID: nr.SuccTaskID = r.SuccTaskID
        nr.RelType = r.RelType: nr.LagHours = r.LagHours: nr.IsExternal = r.IsExternal
        nr.ExternalProjectID = r.ExternalProjectID: nr.ExternalPredDate = r.ExternalPredDate: nr.HasExternalPredDate = r.HasExternalPredDate
        Set target.Relationships(CStr(k)) = nr
    Next k
    For Each k In source.WBS.Keys
        Set w = source.WBS(k)
        Dim nw As New clsWBS
        nw.WBSID = w.WBSID: nw.ParentWBSID = w.ParentWBSID: nw.Code = w.Code: nw.Name = w.Name
        nw.Seq = w.Seq: nw.OBSID = w.OBSID: nw.EVPercent = w.EVPercent: nw.Notes = w.Notes
        Set target.WBS(CStr(k)) = nw
    Next k
    For Each k In source.Resources.Keys
        Set rs = source.Resources(k)
        Dim nrs As New clsResource
        nrs.ResourceID = rs.ResourceID: nrs.ResourceCode = rs.ResourceCode: nrs.Name = rs.Name
        nrs.ResourceType = rs.ResourceType: nrs.MaxUnitsPerHour = rs.MaxUnitsPerHour
        nrs.PricePerUnit = rs.PricePerUnit: nrs.CalendarID = rs.CalendarID
        Set target.Resources(CStr(k)) = nrs
    Next k
    For Each k In source.Assignments.Keys
        Set asn = source.Assignments(k)
        Dim na As New clsAssignment
        na.AssignmentID = asn.AssignmentID: na.TaskID = asn.TaskID: na.ResourceID = asn.ResourceID
        na.RoleID = asn.RoleID: na.CurveID = asn.CurveID: na.BudgetUnits = asn.BudgetUnits
        na.ActualUnits = asn.ActualUnits: na.RemainingUnits = asn.RemainingUnits
        na.BudgetCost = asn.BudgetCost: na.ActualCost = asn.ActualCost: na.RemainingCost = asn.RemainingCost
        Set target.Assignments(CStr(k)) = na
    Next k
    For Each k In source.Calendars.Keys
        Set cal = source.Calendars(k)
        Set target.Calendars(CStr(k)) = cal.Clone
    Next k
    Dim bl As clsBaseline, nbl As clsBaseline, bk As Variant, ba As clsActivity
    For Each k In source.Baselines.Keys
        Set bl = source.Baselines(k)
        Set nbl = New clsBaseline
        nbl.Name = bl.Name: nbl.Role = bl.Role: nbl.SourcePath = bl.SourcePath: nbl.ImportedOn = bl.ImportedOn
        For Each bk In bl.Activities.Keys
            Set ba = bl.Activities(bk)
            Set nbl.Activities(CStr(bk)) = ba.Clone
        Next bk
        Set target.Baselines(CStr(k)) = nbl
    Next k

    For Each k In source.BIMLinks.Keys
        Set b = source.BIMLinks(k)
        Dim nb As New clsBIMLink
        nb.LinkID = b.LinkID: nb.TaskID = b.TaskID: nb.ModelKey = b.ModelKey: nb.ObjectID = b.ObjectID
        nb.ObjectName = b.ObjectName: nb.Category = b.Category: nb.Level = b.Level: nb.SystemName = b.SystemName
        nb.Quantity = b.Quantity: nb.QuantityUnit = b.QuantityUnit
        Set target.BIMLinks(CStr(k)) = nb
    Next k
    For Each k In source.ExtendedRecords.Keys
        Set x = source.ExtendedRecords(k)
        Set target.ExtendedRecords(CStr(k)) = x.Clone
    Next k
    For Each k In source.RawRecords.Keys
        Set x = source.RawRecords(k)
        Set target.RawRecords(CStr(k)) = x.Clone
    Next k
    Set CloneScheduleModel = target
End Function

Public Sub RefreshRepositorySheet()
    EnsureRepository
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = EnsureSheet(SH_REPOSITORY)
    ws.Cells.Clear
    WriteRow ws, 1, 1, Array("Revision ID", "Revision Name", "Description", "Imported On", "Project ID", "Project", "Format", "Source Path", "Data Date", "Activities")
    ws.Rows(1).Font.Bold = True
    Dim rowNo As Long: rowNo = 2
    Dim k As Variant, r As clsRevision
    For Each k In gRepository.Keys
        Set r = gRepository(k)
        WriteRow ws, rowNo, 1, Array(r.RevisionID, r.RevisionName, r.Description, r.ImportedOn, r.Model.ProjectID, r.Model.ProjectName, r.SourceFormat, r.SourcePath, IIf(r.Model.HasDataDate, r.Model.DataDate, ""), r.Model.Activities.Count)
        rowNo = rowNo + 1
    Next k
    ws.Columns.AutoFit
End Sub

Attribute VB_Name = "modResourceCharts"
Option Explicit

Public Sub BuildWeeklyResourceCharts()
    TraceEnter "modResourceCharts", "BuildWeeklyResourceCharts", ScheduleTraceContext()
    BuildResourceCharts "WEEK"
End Sub

Public Sub BuildMonthlyResourceCharts()
    TraceEnter "modResourceCharts", "BuildMonthlyResourceCharts", ScheduleTraceContext()
    BuildResourceCharts "MONTH"
End Sub

Public Sub BuildResourceCharts(ByVal bucket As String)
    TraceEnter "modResourceCharts", "BuildResourceCharts", "bucket=" & bucket & "; " & ScheduleTraceContext()
    On Error GoTo fail
    If gSchedule Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4200, , "No schedule loaded."
    SyncModelFromSheets gSchedule
    BuildResourceProfile gSchedule, bucket

    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = EnsureSheet(SH_RESOURCE_PROFILE)
    AggregateProfileForCharts ws
    DeleteCharts ws

    Dim lastRow As Long: lastRow = ws.Cells(ws.Rows.Count, 9).End(xlUp).Row
    If lastRow < 3 Then Exit Sub

    Dim co As ChartObject
    Set co = ws.ChartObjects.Add(Left:=ws.Range("Q2").Left, Top:=ws.Range("Q2").Top, Width:=650, Height:=280)
    co.Name = "chtResourceHistogram"
    With co.Chart
        .ChartType = xlColumnClustered
        .SetSourceData ws.Range("I1:L" & lastRow)
        .HasTitle = True
        .ChartTitle.Text = "Resource Histogram - " & bucket
        .Legend.Position = xlLegendPositionBottom
    End With

    Set co = ws.ChartObjects.Add(Left:=ws.Range("Q20").Left, Top:=ws.Range("Q20").Top, Width:=650, Height:=280)
    co.Name = "chtResourceSCurve"
    With co.Chart
        .ChartType = xlLine
        .SetSourceData ws.Range("I1:I" & lastRow & ",M1:O" & lastRow)
        .HasTitle = True
        .ChartTitle.Text = "Cumulative Resource S-Curves"
        .Legend.Position = xlLegendPositionBottom
    End With
    TraceExit "modResourceCharts", "BuildResourceCharts", "bucket=" & bucket & "; rows=" & CStr(lastRow)
    Exit Sub
fail:
    LogAndShowError "modResourceCharts", "BuildResourceCharts", "The resource histogram/S-curve could not be built.", _
                    "bucket=" & bucket & "; " & ScheduleTraceContext()
End Sub

Private Sub AggregateProfileForCharts(ByVal ws As Worksheet)
    Dim agg As Object: Set agg = Dict()
    Dim r As Long, lastRow As Long, key As String, arr As Variant
    lastRow = ws.Cells(ws.Rows.Count, 1).End(xlUp).Row
    For r = 2 To lastRow
        key = CStr(ws.Cells(r, 1).Value)
        If Len(key) > 0 Then
            If agg.Exists(key) Then arr = agg(key) Else arr = Array(0#, 0#, 0#)
            arr(0) = arr(0) + SafeDbl(ws.Cells(r, 3).Value)
            arr(1) = arr(1) + SafeDbl(ws.Cells(r, 4).Value)
            arr(2) = arr(2) + SafeDbl(ws.Cells(r, 5).Value)
            agg(key) = arr
        End If
    Next r

    ws.Range("I:O").Clear
    WriteRow ws, 1, 9, Array("Period", "Budget Units", "Actual Units", "Remaining Units", "Cum Budget", "Cum Actual", "Cum Forecast")
    ws.Range("I1:O1").Font.Bold = True

    Dim keys As Variant: keys = SortedTextKeys(agg)
    Dim i As Long, outRow As Long: outRow = 2
    Dim cb As Double, ca As Double, cf As Double
    If IsArray(keys) Then
        For i = LBound(keys) To UBound(keys)
            key = CStr(keys(i)): arr = agg(key)
            cb = cb + arr(0): ca = ca + arr(1): cf = cf + arr(1) + arr(2)
            WriteRow ws, outRow, 9, Array(key, arr(0), arr(1), arr(2), cb, ca, cf)
            outRow = outRow + 1
        Next i
    End If
    ws.Columns("I:O").AutoFit
End Sub

Private Function SortedTextKeys(ByVal d As Object) As Variant
    If d.Count = 0 Then SortedTextKeys = Array(): Exit Function
    Dim arr() As String, i As Long, j As Long, t As String, k As Variant
    ReDim arr(0 To d.Count - 1)
    For Each k In d.Keys: arr(i) = CStr(k): i = i + 1: Next k
    For i = LBound(arr) To UBound(arr) - 1
        For j = i + 1 To UBound(arr)
            If arr(j) < arr(i) Then t = arr(i): arr(i) = arr(j): arr(j) = t
        Next j
    Next i
    SortedTextKeys = arr
End Function

Private Sub DeleteCharts(ByVal ws As Worksheet)
    Dim i As Long
    For i = ws.ChartObjects.Count To 1 Step -1
        ws.ChartObjects(i).Delete
    Next i
End Sub
