Private Sub BuildTutorialSheet()
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = Worksheets(SH_TUTORIAL)
    ws.Cells.Clear
    ws.Range("A1").Value = "Excel Schedule Studio Tutorial"
    ws.Range("A1").Font.Size = 18: ws.Range("A1").Font.Bold = True
    Dim lines As Variant, i As Long
    lines = Array( _
        "1. Import: use ImportXERFile or ImportMSPXMLFile. Both load into one canonical VBA schedule model.", _
        "2. Edit: Activities, WBS, Relationships, Resources, Assignments, Calendars and BIM Links are editable sheets.", _
        "3. Commit: ApplyWorksheetEdits validates sheet edits before calculation/export.", _
        "4. F9: ScheduleCurrentProject performs CPM using FS/SS/FF/SF, lag, calendars, progress options, constraints and optional resource leveling.", _
        "5. Gantt: BuildGanttView creates current, baseline, actual/progress and Data Date bars using worksheet cells for scalability.", _
        "6. Baselines: load XER or MSP XML baselines; set PrimaryBaselineName on Settings for Gantt/variance use.", _
        "7. Resources: Assignments is Excel-friendly; Resource Profiles produces histogram and cumulative S-curve charts.", _
        "8. EVM: BuildEVMAndProductivity calculates PV, EV, AC, BAC, SPI, CPI, EAC, VAC and TCPI plus resource productivity.", _
        "9. Progress: ImportProgressCSV accepts ActivityID/TaskID, ActualStart, ActualFinish, RemainingDurationHours and PercentComplete.", _
        "10. Filters/Layouts: configure named rules on Filters; save/apply column visibility and grouping from Layouts.", _
        "11. Scenarios/History: snapshots copy the editable schedule sheets into VeryHidden recovery sheets and can be restored.", _
        "12. BIM: BIM Objects stores model metadata; BIM Links stores TaskID + ModelKey + stable ObjectID. Use stable GUIDs, not transient viewer IDs.", _
        "13. Navisworks: Excel does not render GLB/NWD safely; use OpenSelectedBIMModel to launch the local model externally while Excel retains links.", _
        "14. Export: ExportCurrentAsXER / ExportCurrentAsMSPXML run structural validation and conversion-audit reporting first.", _
        "15. Network Diagram: BuildNetworkDiagram renders a logic network for the current scope; use NetworkWBSID/NetworkMaxActivities in Settings.", _
        "16. Extended XER Data: Extended Data preserves/editably exposes non-core XER tables such as codes, roles, notebooks, risks and other version-specific records.", _
        "17. Testing: RunAllSelfTests writes an Excel-side regression report to Tests.")
    For i = LBound(lines) To UBound(lines): ws.Cells(i + 3, 1).Value = lines(i): Next i
    ws.Columns("A").ColumnWidth = 120: ws.Range("A1:A40").WrapText = True
End Sub

Public Sub RefreshAllViews(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel)
    WriteActivities model
    WriteWBS model
    WriteRelationships model
    WriteResources model
    WriteAssignments model
    WriteBIM model
    RefreshCalendarsView model
    RefreshExtendedDataView model
    RefreshRawDataView model
    RefreshP6ExtendedViews model
    InitializeGanttStyles
End Sub

Private Sub WriteActivities(ByVal m As clsScheduleModel)
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = Worksheets(SH_ACTIVITIES): ws.Cells.Clear
    WriteRow ws, 1, 1, Array("TaskID", "Activity ID", "Name", "WBS ID", "Calendar ID", "Type", "Status", "Original h", "Remaining h", "% Complete", "Planned Start", "Planned Finish", "Actual Start", "Actual Finish", "Expected Finish", "Suspend Date", "Resume Date", "Constraint Type", "Constraint Date", "Early Start", "Early Finish", "Late Start", "Late Finish", "TF h", "FF h", "Budget Cost", "Actual Cost")
    Dim r As Long: r = 2
    Dim k As Variant, a As clsActivity
    For Each k In m.Activities.Keys
        Set a = m.Activities(k)
        WriteRow ws, r, 1, Array(a.TaskID, a.ActivityID, a.Name, a.WBSID, a.CalendarID, a.ActivityType, a.Status, a.OriginalDurationHours, a.RemainingDurationHours, a.PercentComplete)
        If a.HasPlannedStart Then ws.Cells(r, 11) = a.PlannedStart
        If a.HasPlannedFinish Then ws.Cells(r, 12) = a.PlannedFinish
        If a.HasActualStart Then ws.Cells(r, 13) = a.ActualStart
        If a.HasActualFinish Then ws.Cells(r, 14) = a.ActualFinish
        If a.HasExpectedFinish Then ws.Cells(r, 15) = a.ExpectedFinish
        If a.HasSuspendDate Then ws.Cells(r, 16) = a.SuspendDate
        If a.HasResumeDate Then ws.Cells(r, 17) = a.ResumeDate
        ws.Cells(r, 18) = a.ConstraintType1
        If a.HasConstraintDate1 Then ws.Cells(r, 19) = a.ConstraintDate1
        If a.EarlyStart > 0 Then ws.Cells(r, 20) = a.EarlyStart
        If a.EarlyFinish > 0 Then ws.Cells(r, 21) = a.EarlyFinish
        If a.LateStart > 0 Then ws.Cells(r, 22) = a.LateStart
        If a.LateFinish > 0 Then ws.Cells(r, 23) = a.LateFinish
        ws.Cells(r, 24) = a.TotalFloatHours: ws.Cells(r, 25) = a.FreeFloatHours
        ws.Cells(r, 26) = a.BudgetCost: ws.Cells(r, 27) = a.ActualCost
        r = r + 1
    Next
    FormatDataSheet ws
End Sub

Private Sub WriteWBS(ByVal m As clsScheduleModel)
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = Worksheets(SH_WBS): ws.Cells.Clear
    WriteRow ws, 1, 1, Array("WBS ID", "Parent", "Code", "Name", "Seq", "OBS", "EV %", "Notes")
    Dim r As Long, k As Variant, w As clsWBS
    r = 2
    For Each k In m.WBS.Keys
        Set w = m.WBS(k): WriteRow ws, r, 1, Array(w.WBSID, w.ParentWBSID, w.Code, w.Name, w.Seq, w.OBSID, w.EVPercent, w.Notes): r = r + 1
    Next
    FormatDataSheet ws
End Sub

Private Sub WriteRelationships(ByVal m As clsScheduleModel)
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = Worksheets(SH_RELATIONSHIPS): ws.Cells.Clear
    WriteRow ws, 1, 1, Array("Rel ID", "Pred TaskID", "Succ TaskID", "Type", "Lag h", "External", "External Project", "External Pred Date")
    Dim r As Long, k As Variant, x As clsRelationship
    r = 2
    For Each k In m.Relationships.Keys
        Set x = m.Relationships(k)
        WriteRow ws, r, 1, Array(x.RelID, x.PredTaskID, x.SuccTaskID, x.RelType, x.LagHours, x.IsExternal, x.ExternalProjectID)
        If x.HasExternalPredDate Then ws.Cells(r, 8).Value = x.ExternalPredDate
        r = r + 1
    Next
    FormatDataSheet ws
End Sub

Private Sub WriteResources(ByVal m As clsScheduleModel)
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = Worksheets(SH_RESOURCES): ws.Cells.Clear
    WriteRow ws, 1, 1, Array("Resource ID", "Code", "Name", "Type", "Max Units/hr", "Rate", "Calendar ID")
    Dim r As Long, k As Variant, x As clsResource
    r = 2
    For Each k In m.Resources.Keys
        Set x = m.Resources(k): WriteRow ws, r, 1, Array(x.ResourceID, x.ResourceCode, x.Name, x.ResourceType, x.MaxUnitsPerHour, x.PricePerUnit, x.CalendarID): r = r + 1
    Next
    FormatDataSheet ws
End Sub

Private Sub WriteAssignments(ByVal m As clsScheduleModel)
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = Worksheets(SH_ASSIGNMENTS): ws.Cells.Clear
    WriteRow ws, 1, 1, Array("Assignment ID", "TaskID", "Resource ID", "Role", "Curve", "Budget Units", "Actual Units", "Remaining Units", "Budget Cost", "Actual Cost", "Remaining Cost")
    Dim r As Long, k As Variant, x As clsAssignment
    r = 2
    For Each k In m.Assignments.Keys
        Set x = m.Assignments(k): WriteRow ws, r, 1, Array(x.AssignmentID, x.TaskID, x.ResourceID, x.RoleID, x.CurveID, x.BudgetUnits, x.ActualUnits, x.RemainingUnits, x.BudgetCost, x.ActualCost, x.RemainingCost): r = r + 1
    Next
    FormatDataSheet ws
End Sub

Private Sub WriteBIM(ByVal m As clsScheduleModel)
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = Worksheets(SH_BIM): ws.Cells.Clear
    WriteRow ws, 1, 1, Array("Link ID", "TaskID", "Model Key", "Object ID", "Name", "Category", "Level", "System", "Quantity", "Unit")
    Dim r As Long, k As Variant, x As clsBIMLink
    r = 2
    For Each k In m.BIMLinks.Keys
        Set x = m.BIMLinks(k): WriteRow ws, r, 1, Array(x.LinkID, x.TaskID, x.ModelKey, x.ObjectID, x.ObjectName, x.Category, x.Level, x.SystemName, x.Quantity, x.QuantityUnit): r = r + 1
    Next
    FormatDataSheet ws
End Sub

Private Sub FormatDataSheet(ByVal ws As Worksheet)
    ws.Rows(1).Font.Bold = True
    ws.Rows(1).Interior.Color = RGB(217, 225, 242)
    ws.Rows(1).AutoFilter
    ws.Columns.AutoFit
End Sub


========================================================================
FILE: src/modWBSTools.bas
========================================================================

Attribute VB_Name = "modWBSTools"
Option Explicit

Public Sub AddWBSFromPrompt()
    If gSchedule Is Nothing Then Exit Sub
    Dim id As String,parent As String,code As String,nm As String
    id=InputBox("New WBS ID:","Add WBS"):If Len(id)=0 Then Exit Sub
    parent=InputBox("Parent WBS ID (blank for root):","Add WBS")
    code=InputBox("WBS Code:","Add WBS"):nm=InputBox("WBS Name:","Add WBS")
    AddWBSNode gSchedule,id,parent,code,nm:RefreshAllViews gSchedule
End Sub

Public Sub AddWBSNode(ByVal m As clsScheduleModel,ByVal id As String,ByVal parent As String,ByVal code As String,ByVal nm As String)
    If m.WBS.Exists(id) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError+4600,,"WBS already exists."
    If Len(parent)>0 Then If Not m.WBS.Exists(parent) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError+4601,,"Parent WBS not found."
    Dim w As New clsWBS:w.WBSID=id:w.ParentWBSID=parent:w.Code=code:w.Name=nm:w.Seq=m.WBS.Count+1:Set m.WBS(id)=w
    WriteAudit "ADD","WBS",id,code&" - "&nm
End Sub

Public Sub MoveSelectedActivityToWBS()
    If ActiveSheet.Name<>SH_ACTIVITIES Then MsgBox "Select an activity row first.",vbExclamation:Exit Sub
    Dim taskId As String:taskId=CStr(Cells(ActiveCell.Row,1).Value)
    Dim wbsId As String:wbsId=InputBox("Target WBS ID:","Move Activity")
    If Len(wbsId)=0 Then Exit Sub
    If Not gSchedule.Activities.Exists(taskId) Or Not gSchedule.WBS.Exists(wbsId) Then MsgBox "Task or WBS not found.",vbExclamation:Exit Sub
    gSchedule.Activities(taskId).WBSID=wbsId:RefreshAllViews gSchedule
End Sub

Public Sub DeleteSelectedActivity()
    If ActiveSheet.Name<>SH_ACTIVITIES Then MsgBox "Select an activity row first.",vbExclamation:Exit Sub
    Dim taskId As String:taskId=CStr(Cells(ActiveCell.Row,1).Value)
    If Len(taskId)=0 Then Exit Sub
    If MsgBox("Delete activity and dependent relationships/resources/BIM links?",vbYesNo+vbExclamation)<>vbYes Then Exit Sub
    DeleteActivitySafe gSchedule,taskId:RefreshAllViews gSchedule
End Sub

Public Sub DeleteActivitySafe(ByVal m As clsScheduleModel,ByVal taskId As String)
    Dim doomed As New Collection,k As Variant,r As clsRelationship,a As clsAssignment,b As clsBIMLink
    For Each k In m.Relationships.Keys:Set r=m.Relationships(k):If r.PredTaskID=taskId Or r.SuccTaskID=taskId Then doomed.Add CStr(k)
    Next
    For Each k In doomed:m.Relationships.Remove CStr(k):Next
    Set doomed=New Collection
    For Each k In m.Assignments.Keys:Set a=m.Assignments(k):If a.TaskID=taskId Then doomed.Add CStr(k)
    Next
    For Each k In doomed:m.Assignments.Remove CStr(k):Next
    Set doomed=New Collection
    For Each k In m.BIMLinks.Keys:Set b=m.BIMLinks(k):If b.TaskID=taskId Then doomed.Add CStr(k)
    Next
    For Each k In doomed:m.BIMLinks.Remove CStr(k):Next
    If m.Activities.Exists(taskId) Then m.Activities.Remove taskId
    WriteAudit "DELETE","Activity",taskId,"Cascade cleanup"
End Sub

Public Sub DeleteSelectedWBS()
    If ActiveSheet.Name<>SH_WBS Then MsgBox "Select a WBS row first.",vbExclamation:Exit Sub
    Dim id As String:id=CStr(Cells(ActiveCell.Row,1).Value):If Len(id)=0 Then Exit Sub
    Dim resp As VbMsgBoxResult:resp=MsgBox("YES = delete subtree and its activities."&vbCrLf&"NO = remove only this WBS and move direct contents to its parent."&vbCrLf&"CANCEL = abort.",vbYesNoCancel+vbExclamation,"Delete WBS")
    If resp=vbCancel Then Exit Sub
    DeleteWBSNodeSafe gSchedule,id,(resp=vbNo):RefreshAllViews gSchedule
End Sub

Private Sub DeleteWBSNodeSafe(ByVal m As clsScheduleModel,ByVal id As String,ByVal preserve As Boolean)
    If Not m.WBS.Exists(id) Then Exit Sub
    Dim root As clsWBS, parent As String, k As Variant, w As clsWBS, a As clsActivity
    Set root=m.WBS(id)
    parent=root.ParentWBSID
    If preserve Then
        For Each k In m.WBS.Keys:Set w=m.WBS(k):If w.ParentWBSID=id Then w.ParentWBSID=parent
        Next
        For Each k In m.Activities.Keys:Set a=m.Activities(k):If a.WBSID=id Then a.WBSID=parent
        Next
        m.WBS.Remove id
    Else
        Dim d As Object, tasks As New Collection
        Set d=DescendantSet(m,id)
        For Each k In m.Activities.Keys:Set a=m.Activities(k):If d.Exists(a.WBSID) Then tasks.Add a.TaskID
        Next
        For Each k In tasks:DeleteActivitySafe m,CStr(k):Next
        For Each k In d.Keys:If m.WBS.Exists(CStr(k)) Then m.WBS.Remove CStr(k)
        Next
    End If
End Sub

Private Function DescendantSet(ByVal m As clsScheduleModel,ByVal rootId As String) As Object
    Dim d As Object:Set d=Dict():d(rootId)=True
    Dim changed As Boolean,k As Variant,w As clsWBS
    Do
        changed=False
        For Each k In m.WBS.Keys:Set w=m.WBS(k):If d.Exists(w.ParentWBSID) And Not d.Exists(w.WBSID) Then d(w.WBSID)=True:changed=True
        Next
    Loop While changed
    Set DescendantSet=d
End Function


========================================================================
FILE: src/modXER.bas
========================================================================

Attribute VB_Name = "modXER"
Option Explicit
Private mUDFTypes As Object
Private mBIMChunks As Object

Public Function ParseXER(ByVal filePath As String) As clsScheduleModel
    TraceEnter "modXER", "ParseXER", TraceKV("filePath", filePath)
    Dim f As Integer, line As String, tbl As String, vals As Variant, headers As Object, rec As Object, fields As Variant, i As Long
    Dim model As New clsScheduleModel: Set headers=Dict()
    Set mUDFTypes=Dict():Set mBIMChunks=Dict()
    f=FreeFile: Open filePath For Input As #f
    Do While Not EOF(f)
        Line Input #f,line
        If Left$(line,2)="%T" Then vals=Split(line,vbTab): If UBound(vals)>=1 Then tbl=CStr(vals(1))
        If Left$(line,2)="%F" Then
            vals=Split(line,vbTab)
            If UBound(vals)>=1 Then
                ReDim fields(1 To UBound(vals))
                For i=1 To UBound(vals): fields(i)=CStr(vals(i)): Next
                headers(tbl)=fields
            End If
        ElseIf Left$(line,2)="%R" And headers.Exists(tbl) Then
            vals=Split(line,vbTab): fields=headers(tbl): Set rec=Dict()
            For i=LBound(fields) To UBound(fields)
                If i<=UBound(vals) Then rec(CStr(fields(i)))=vals(i) Else rec(CStr(fields(i)))=""
            Next
            StoreRawRecord model, tbl, rec
            ApplyRecord model,tbl,rec
        End If
    Loop
    Close #f
    FinalizeExternalRelationships model
    FinalizeBIMUDFLinks model
    AggregateActivityCosts model
    model.SourceFormat="XER": model.SourcePath=filePath
    TraceExit "modXER", "ParseXER", "activities=" & CStr(model.Activities.Count) & "; relationships=" & CStr(model.Relationships.Count) & "; resources=" & CStr(model.Resources.Count)
    Set ParseXER=model
End Function

Private Sub ApplyRecord(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel,ByVal tbl As String,ByVal rec As Object)
    Dim d As Date
    Select Case UCase$(tbl)
        Case "PROJECT"
            If rec.Exists("proj_id") Then model.ProjectID=SafeText(rec("proj_id"))
            If rec.Exists("proj_short_name") Then model.ProjectName=SafeText(rec("proj_short_name"))
            If rec.Exists("last_recalc_date") Then If TryDate(rec("last_recalc_date"),d) Then model.DataDate=d:model.HasDataDate=True
        Case "PROJWBS"
            Dim w As New clsWBS
            w.WBSID=SafeText(rec("wbs_id")): If rec.Exists("parent_wbs_id") Then w.ParentWBSID=SafeText(rec("parent_wbs_id"))
            If rec.Exists("wbs_short_name") Then w.Code=SafeText(rec("wbs_short_name"))
            If rec.Exists("wbs_name") Then w.Name=SafeText(rec("wbs_name"))
            If rec.Exists("seq_num") Then w.Seq=SafeLng(rec("seq_num"))
            Set model.WBS(w.WBSID)=w
        Case "TASK"
            Dim a As New clsActivity
            a.TaskID=SafeText(rec("task_id"))
            If rec.Exists("task_code") Then a.ActivityID=SafeText(rec("task_code"))
            If rec.Exists("task_name") Then a.Name=SafeText(rec("task_name"))
            If rec.Exists("wbs_id") Then a.WBSID=SafeText(rec("wbs_id"))
            If rec.Exists("clndr_id") Then a.CalendarID=SafeText(rec("clndr_id"))
            If rec.Exists("task_type") Then a.ActivityType=SafeText(rec("task_type"))
            If rec.Exists("status_code") Then a.Status=SafeText(rec("status_code"))
            If rec.Exists("target_drtn_hr_cnt") Then a.OriginalDurationHours=SafeDbl(rec("target_drtn_hr_cnt"))
            If rec.Exists("remain_drtn_hr_cnt") Then a.RemainingDurationHours=SafeDbl(rec("remain_drtn_hr_cnt"))
            If rec.Exists("phys_complete_pct") Then a.PercentComplete=SafeDbl(rec("phys_complete_pct"))
            If rec.Exists("target_start_date") Then If TryDate(rec("target_start_date"),d) Then a.PlannedStart=d:a.HasPlannedStart=True
            If rec.Exists("target_end_date") Then If TryDate(rec("target_end_date"),d) Then a.PlannedFinish=d:a.HasPlannedFinish=True
            If rec.Exists("act_start_date") Then If TryDate(rec("act_start_date"),d) Then a.ActualStart=d:a.HasActualStart=True
            If rec.Exists("act_end_date") Then If TryDate(rec("act_end_date"),d) Then a.ActualFinish=d:a.HasActualFinish=True
            If rec.Exists("expect_end_date") Then If TryDate(rec("expect_end_date"),d) Then a.ExpectedFinish=d:a.HasExpectedFinish=True
            If rec.Exists("suspend_date") Then If TryDate(rec("suspend_date"),d) Then a.SuspendDate=d:a.HasSuspendDate=True
            If rec.Exists("resume_date") Then If TryDate(rec("resume_date"),d) Then a.ResumeDate=d:a.HasResumeDate=True
            If rec.Exists("cstr_type") Then a.ConstraintType1=SafeText(rec("cstr_type"))
            If rec.Exists("cstr_date") Then If TryDate(rec("cstr_date"),d) Then a.ConstraintDate1=d:a.HasConstraintDate1=True
            Set model.Activities(a.TaskID)=a
        Case "TASKPRED"
            Dim r As New clsRelationship
            r.RelID=SafeText(rec("task_pred_id")): r.PredTaskID=SafeText(rec("pred_task_id")): r.SuccTaskID=SafeText(rec("task_id"))
            r.RelType=Replace(UCase$(SafeText(rec("pred_type"))),"PR_","")
            If rec.Exists("lag_hr_cnt") Then r.LagHours=SafeDbl(rec("lag_hr_cnt"))
            If rec.Exists("pred_proj_id") Then r.ExternalProjectID=SafeText(rec("pred_proj_id"))
            Set model.Relationships(r.RelID)=r
        Case "RSRC"
            Dim rs As New clsResource
            rs.ResourceID=SafeText(rec("rsrc_id"))
            If rec.Exists("rsrc_short_name") Then rs.ResourceCode=SafeText(rec("rsrc_short_name"))
            If rec.Exists("rsrc_name") Then rs.Name=SafeText(rec("rsrc_name"))
            If rec.Exists("rsrc_type") Then rs.ResourceType=SafeText(rec("rsrc_type"))
            If rec.Exists("max_qty_per_hr") Then rs.MaxUnitsPerHour=SafeDbl(rec("max_qty_per_hr"))
            If rec.Exists("cost_per_qty") Then rs.PricePerUnit=SafeDbl(rec("cost_per_qty"))
            Set model.Resources(rs.ResourceID)=rs
        Case "TASKRSRC"
            Dim asn As New clsAssignment
            asn.AssignmentID=SafeText(rec("taskrsrc_id")): asn.TaskID=SafeText(rec("task_id")): asn.ResourceID=SafeText(rec("rsrc_id"))
            If rec.Exists("target_qty") Then asn.BudgetUnits=SafeDbl(rec("target_qty"))
            If rec.Exists("act_reg_qty") Then asn.ActualUnits=SafeDbl(rec("act_reg_qty"))
            If rec.Exists("remain_qty") Then asn.RemainingUnits=SafeDbl(rec("remain_qty"))
            If rec.Exists("target_cost") Then asn.BudgetCost=SafeDbl(rec("target_cost"))
            If rec.Exists("act_reg_cost") Then asn.ActualCost=SafeDbl(rec("act_reg_cost"))
            If rec.Exists("remain_cost") Then asn.RemainingCost=SafeDbl(rec("remain_cost"))
            Set model.Assignments(asn.AssignmentID)=asn
        Case "CALENDAR"
            Dim cal As New clsCalendar
            cal.CalendarID=SafeText(rec("clndr_id"))
            If rec.Exists("clndr_name") Then cal.Name=SafeText(rec("clndr_name"))
            If rec.Exists("day_hr_cnt") Then cal.HoursPerDay=SafeDbl(rec("day_hr_cnt"),8) Else cal.HoursPerDay=8
            cal.SetWeekdayHours 1,cal.HoursPerDay:cal.SetWeekdayHours 2,cal.HoursPerDay:cal.SetWeekdayHours 3,cal.HoursPerDay:cal.SetWeekdayHours 4,cal.HoursPerDay:cal.SetWeekdayHours 5,cal.HoursPerDay
            cal.SetWeekdayHours 6,0:cal.SetWeekdayHours 7,0
            Set model.Calendars(cal.CalendarID)=cal
        Case "UDFTYPE"
            Dim isBIMType As Boolean: isBIMType = False
            If rec.Exists("udf_type_id") Then
                If UCase$(SafeText(rec("table_name")))="TASK" Then
                    Dim lbl As String
                    If rec.Exists("udf_type_label") Then lbl=SafeText(rec("udf_type_label")) ElseIf rec.Exists("udf_type_name") Then lbl=SafeText(rec("udf_type_name"))
                    If UCase$(Left$(lbl,9))="BIM_LINK_" Then
                        mUDFTypes(SafeText(rec("udf_type_id")))=lbl
                        isBIMType = True
                    End If
                End If
            End If
            If Not isBIMType Then StoreExtendedRecord model, tbl, rec
        Case "UDFVALUE"
            If rec.Exists("udf_type_id") And rec.Exists("fk_id") And rec.Exists("udf_text") Then
                Dim uId As String:uId=SafeText(rec("udf_type_id"))
                If mUDFTypes.Exists(uId) Then
                    mBIMChunks(SafeText(rec("fk_id"))&"|"&CStr(mUDFTypes(uId)))=SafeText(rec("udf_text"))
                Else
                    StoreExtendedRecord model, tbl, rec
                End If
            Else
                StoreExtendedRecord model, tbl, rec
            End If
        Case Else
            StoreExtendedRecord model, tbl, rec
    End Select
End Sub

Public Sub SaveModelAsXER(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel,ByVal filePath As String)
    TraceEnter "modXER", "SaveModelAsXER", TraceKV("filePath", filePath) & "; activities=" & CStr(model.Activities.Count)
    Dim msg As String
    If Not model.Validate(msg) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError+2000,,"XER export blocked: "&msg
    Dim f As Integer,k As Variant
    f=FreeFile: Open filePath For Output As #f
    Print #f,"ERMHDR"&vbTab&"18.8"&vbTab&Format$(Date,"yyyy-mm-dd")
    Print #f,"%T"&vbTab&"PROJECT"
    Print #f,"%F"&vbTab&"proj_id"&vbTab&"proj_short_name"&vbTab&"last_recalc_date"
    Print #f,"%R"&vbTab&model.ProjectID&vbTab&EscapeXER(model.ProjectName)&vbTab&IIf(model.HasDataDate,XERDate(model.DataDate),"")

    Print #f,"%T"&vbTab&"PROJWBS"
    Print #f,"%F"&vbTab&"wbs_id"&vbTab&"proj_id"&vbTab&"parent_wbs_id"&vbTab&"wbs_short_name"&vbTab&"wbs_name"&vbTab&"seq_num"
    Dim w As clsWBS
    For Each k In model.WBS.Keys
        Set w=model.WBS(k)
        Print #f,"%R"&vbTab&w.WBSID&vbTab&model.ProjectID&vbTab&w.ParentWBSID&vbTab&EscapeXER(w.Code)&vbTab&EscapeXER(w.Name)&vbTab&w.Seq
    Next

    Print #f,"%T"&vbTab&"CALENDAR"
    Print #f,"%F"&vbTab&"clndr_id"&vbTab&"clndr_name"&vbTab&"day_hr_cnt"&vbTab&"week_hr_cnt"
    Dim cal As clsCalendar
    For Each k In model.Calendars.Keys
        Set cal=model.Calendars(k)
        Print #f,"%R"&vbTab&cal.CalendarID&vbTab&EscapeXER(cal.Name)&vbTab&cal.HoursPerDay&vbTab&(cal.HoursPerDay*5)
    Next

    Print #f,"%T"&vbTab&"TASK"
    Print #f,"%F"&vbTab&"task_id"&vbTab&"proj_id"&vbTab&"wbs_id"&vbTab&"clndr_id"&vbTab&"task_code"&vbTab&"task_name"&vbTab&"task_type"&vbTab&"status_code"&vbTab&"target_drtn_hr_cnt"&vbTab&"remain_drtn_hr_cnt"&vbTab&"phys_complete_pct"&vbTab&"target_start_date"&vbTab&"target_end_date"&vbTab&"act_start_date"&vbTab&"act_end_date"&vbTab&"expect_end_date"&vbTab&"suspend_date"&vbTab&"resume_date"&vbTab&"cstr_type"&vbTab&"cstr_date"&vbTab&"early_start_date"&vbTab&"early_end_date"&vbTab&"late_start_date"&vbTab&"late_end_date"&vbTab&"total_float_hr_cnt"&vbTab&"free_float_hr_cnt"
    Dim a As clsActivity
    For Each k In model.Activities.Keys
        Set a=model.Activities(k)
        Print #f,"%R"&vbTab&a.TaskID&vbTab&model.ProjectID&vbTab&a.WBSID&vbTab&a.CalendarID&vbTab&EscapeXER(a.ActivityID)&vbTab&EscapeXER(a.Name)&vbTab&a.ActivityType&vbTab&a.Status&vbTab&a.OriginalDurationHours&vbTab&a.RemainingDurationHours&vbTab&a.PercentComplete&vbTab& _
            IIf(a.HasPlannedStart,XERDate(a.PlannedStart),"")&vbTab&IIf(a.HasPlannedFinish,XERDate(a.PlannedFinish),"")&vbTab&IIf(a.HasActualStart,XERDate(a.ActualStart),"")&vbTab&IIf(a.HasActualFinish,XERDate(a.ActualFinish),"")&vbTab& _
            IIf(a.HasExpectedFinish,XERDate(a.ExpectedFinish),"")&vbTab&IIf(a.HasSuspendDate,XERDate(a.SuspendDate),"")&vbTab&IIf(a.HasResumeDate,XERDate(a.ResumeDate),"")&vbTab&a.ConstraintType1&vbTab&IIf(a.HasConstraintDate1,XERDate(a.ConstraintDate1),"")&vbTab& _
            IIf(a.EarlyStart>0,XERDate(a.EarlyStart),"")&vbTab&IIf(a.EarlyFinish>0,XERDate(a.EarlyFinish),"")&vbTab&IIf(a.LateStart>0,XERDate(a.LateStart),"")&vbTab&IIf(a.LateFinish>0,XERDate(a.LateFinish),"")&vbTab&a.TotalFloatHours&vbTab&a.FreeFloatHours
    Next

    Print #f,"%T"&vbTab&"TASKPRED"
    Print #f,"%F"&vbTab&"task_pred_id"&vbTab&"task_id"&vbTab&"pred_task_id"&vbTab&"pred_type"&vbTab&"lag_hr_cnt"&vbTab&"pred_proj_id"
    Dim rel As clsRelationship
    For Each k In model.Relationships.Keys
        Set rel=model.Relationships(k)
        Print #f,"%R"&vbTab&rel.RelID&vbTab&rel.SuccTaskID&vbTab&rel.PredTaskID&vbTab&"PR_"&rel.RelType&vbTab&rel.LagHours&vbTab&rel.ExternalProjectID
    Next

    Print #f,"%T"&vbTab&"RSRC"
    Print #f,"%F"&vbTab&"rsrc_id"&vbTab&"rsrc_short_name"&vbTab&"rsrc_name"&vbTab&"rsrc_type"&vbTab&"max_qty_per_hr"&vbTab&"cost_per_qty"&vbTab&"clndr_id"
    Dim rs As clsResource
    For Each k In model.Resources.Keys
        Set rs=model.Resources(k)
        Print #f,"%R"&vbTab&rs.ResourceID&vbTab&EscapeXER(rs.ResourceCode)&vbTab&EscapeXER(rs.Name)&vbTab&rs.ResourceType&vbTab&rs.MaxUnitsPerHour&vbTab&rs.PricePerUnit&vbTab&rs.CalendarID
    Next

    Print #f,"%T"&vbTab&"TASKRSRC"
    Print #f,"%F"&vbTab&"taskrsrc_id"&vbTab&"task_id"&vbTab&"rsrc_id"&vbTab&"role_id"&vbTab&"curv_id"&vbTab&"target_qty"&vbTab&"act_reg_qty"&vbTab&"remain_qty"&vbTab&"target_cost"&vbTab&"act_reg_cost"&vbTab&"remain_cost"
    Dim asn As clsAssignment
    For Each k In model.Assignments.Keys
        Set asn=model.Assignments(k)
        Print #f,"%R"&vbTab&asn.AssignmentID&vbTab&asn.TaskID&vbTab&asn.ResourceID&vbTab&asn.RoleID&vbTab&asn.CurveID&vbTab&asn.BudgetUnits&vbTab&asn.ActualUnits&vbTab&asn.RemainingUnits&vbTab&asn.BudgetCost&vbTab&asn.ActualCost&vbTab&asn.RemainingCost
    Next

    WriteCombinedUDFTables model, f
    WriteExtendedXERTables model, f
    Print #f,"%E"
    Close #f
    TraceExit "modXER", "SaveModelAsXER", TraceKV("filePath", filePath) & "; extendedRecords=" & CStr(model.ExtendedRecords.Count)
End Sub

Private Sub LegacyWriteBIMUDFTables(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel,ByVal f As Integer)
    If model.BIMLinks.Count=0 Then Exit Sub
    Dim payloads As Object:Set payloads=CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Dim k As Variant,b As clsBIMLink,s As String
    For Each k In model.BIMLinks.Keys
        Set b=model.BIMLinks(k)
        If payloads.Exists(b.TaskID) Then s=CStr(payloads(b.TaskID)) Else s=""
        If Len(s)>0 Then s=s&";"
        s=s&b.ModelKey&"~"&b.ObjectID
        payloads(b.TaskID)=s
    Next
    Dim maxChunks As Long,cc As Long
    For Each k In payloads.Keys
        cc=(Len(CStr(payloads(k)))+239)\240
        If cc>maxChunks Then maxChunks=cc
    Next
    Print #f,"%T"&vbTab&"UDFTYPE"
    Print #f,"%F"&vbTab&"udf_type_id"&vbTab&"table_name"&vbTab&"udf_type_name"&vbTab&"udf_type_label"&vbTab&"logical_data_type"
    Dim i As Long
    For i=1 To maxChunks
        Print #f,"%R"&vbTab&(990000+i)&vbTab&"TASK"&vbTab&"BIM_LINK_"&Format$(i,"00")&vbTab&"BIM_LINK_"&Format$(i,"00")&vbTab&"FT_TEXT"
    Next
    Print #f,"%T"&vbTab&"UDFVALUE"
    Print #f,"%F"&vbTab&"udf_type_id"&vbTab&"fk_id"&vbTab&"udf_text"
    Dim payload As String
    For Each k In payloads.Keys
        payload=CStr(payloads(k)):cc=(Len(payload)+239)\240
        For i=1 To cc
            Print #f,"%R"&vbTab&(990000+i)&vbTab&CStr(k)&vbTab&EscapeXER(Mid$(payload,(i-1)*240+1,240))
        Next
    Next
End Sub

Private Sub FinalizeExternalRelationships(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel)
    Dim k As Variant,r As clsRelationship
    For Each k In model.Relationships.Keys
        Set r=model.Relationships(k)
        If Not model.Activities.Exists(r.PredTaskID) Then r.IsExternal=True
    Next k
End Sub


Private Sub FinalizeBIMUDFLinks(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel)
    If mBIMChunks Is Nothing Then Exit Sub
    Dim taskIds As Object:Set taskIds=Dict()
    Dim k As Variant,parts As Variant
    For Each k In mBIMChunks.Keys
        parts=Split(CStr(k),"|"):If UBound(parts)>=1 Then taskIds(CStr(parts(0)))=True
    Next k
    Dim taskId As Variant,payload As String,i As Long,key As String,refs As Variant,ref As Variant,pair As Variant,b As clsBIMLink
    For Each taskId In taskIds.Keys
        payload=""
        For i=1 To 99
            key=CStr(taskId)&"|BIM_LINK_"&Format$(i,"00")
            If mBIMChunks.Exists(key) Then payload=payload&CStr(mBIMChunks(key))
        Next i
        If Len(payload)>0 Then
            refs=Split(payload,";")
            For Each ref In refs
                pair=Split(CStr(ref),"~")
                If UBound(pair)>=1 Then
                    Set b=New clsBIMLink
                    b.TaskID=CStr(taskId):b.ModelKey=CStr(pair(0)):b.ObjectID=CStr(pair(1)):b.LinkID=b.TaskID&"|"&b.ModelKey&"|"&b.ObjectID
                    If model.Activities.Exists(b.TaskID) Then If Not model.BIMLinks.Exists(b.LinkID) Then Set model.BIMLinks(b.LinkID)=b
                End If
            Next ref
        End If
    Next taskId
End Sub

Private Sub AggregateActivityCosts(ByVal model As clsScheduleModel)
    Dim k As Variant,asn As clsAssignment,a As clsActivity
    For Each k In model.Activities.Keys:Set a=model.Activities(k):a.BudgetCost=0:a.ActualCost=0:Next k
    For Each k In model.Assignments.Keys
        Set asn=model.Assignments(k)
        If model.Activities.Exists(asn.TaskID) Then
            Set a=model.Activities(asn.TaskID)
            a.BudgetCost=a.BudgetCost+asn.BudgetCost
            a.ActualCost=a.ActualCost+asn.ActualCost
        End If
    Next k
End Sub


========================================================================
FILE: ThisWorkbook.txt
========================================================================

Option Explicit

Private Sub Workbook_Open()
    On Error GoTo EH
    TraceEnter "ThisWorkbook", "Workbook_Open", TraceKV("workbook", Me.Name)
    If gSchedule Is Nothing Then Set gSchedule = New clsScheduleModel
    TraceExit "ThisWorkbook", "Workbook_Open", ScheduleTraceContext()
    Exit Sub
EH:
    TraceError "ThisWorkbook", "Workbook_Open", Err.Number, Err.Description, Erl, TraceKV("workbook", Me.Name)
    MsgBox "Excel Schedule Studio could not initialise its workbook state." & vbCrLf & Err.Description, vbExclamation, APP_NAME
End Sub

Private Sub Workbook_BeforeSave(ByVal SaveAsUI As Boolean, Cancel As Boolean)
    On Error GoTo EH
    TraceEnter "ThisWorkbook", "Workbook_BeforeSave", _
               TraceKV("SaveAsUI", SaveAsUI) & "; " & TraceKV("Cancel", Cancel)
    If Not gSchedule Is Nothing Then
        CreateHistorySnapshot "Before workbook save " & Format$(Now, "yyyy-mm-dd hh:nn:ss")
    End If
    TraceExit "ThisWorkbook", "Workbook_BeforeSave", ScheduleTraceContext()
    Exit Sub
EH:
    TraceError "ThisWorkbook", "Workbook_BeforeSave", Err.Number, Err.Description, Erl, _
               TraceKV("SaveAsUI", SaveAsUI) & "; " & TraceKV("Cancel", Cancel)
    ' Do not block the user's workbook save solely because the optional history snapshot failed.
End Sub
